## French: release and search pages (FR-REL+SEO)

The plan's FR-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-fr.md` §6.2, the table rows 6–7 of §1.2, the browser table of §2, §10
risks 6–7, §11 D1–D5 and Q1, answered yes on 2026-11-11: «24 000» with U+202F) after FR0–FR6 and FR-HELP merged (main c569054):
the catalog (3,594 keys), all 33 maps, every VIREO original and the 38 help modules complete. User decision 5 of 2026-11-09
(AGENTS.md): French turns on once all of its sub-fronts are in. PT-REL and PT-SEO are the model; the registry made the release one
field and the SEO table one row. AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: fr has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: `?lang=en`,
`?lang=es-419`, `?lang=pt-BR` or `?lang=fr` exactly (that load only, not stored), then the stored choice (`en`, `es-419`, `pt-BR` or
`fr`), then the first browser language: `fr`, `fr-FR`, `fr-CA`, `fr-BE`, `fr-CH`, `fr-LU`, `fr-SN`, any `fr-*` in any case (`FR-ch`)
open French (plan §2, D1: one neutral French; a later `fr-CA` would be its own registry line); `frr`, `fur`, `br`, `oc`, `wa` and a
browser whose first language is another (`['en-US','fr-FR']`, `['es-MX','fr-FR']`, `['pt-BR','fr-FR']`) stay as they were.
`?lang=FR`, `?lang=fr-FR`, `?lang=fr-CA` are no tag. The selector lists English, Español, Português (Brasil) and Français on every
load, each in its own `lang`. A change from French is stored (the preview note no longer shows for fr). `<html lang="fr">`, the title
and the failure notices are FR0's.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for Spanish and Portuguese: a released bundle builds strictly, and a split one (every production
French load) keeps the English fallback its per-module catalog needs, so strictness is static. `src/i18n/fr/fr.test.ts` now checks the
registry line (`released:true`, `thousands`, `help`), `createI18n(fr,'fr')` without fallback, and every map of `MAP_IDS` against its
English shape without `partial`; the planner's `complete` holds (`split.test.ts` item 7), and `check-i18n-dist.mjs` requires marker
text in every fr module and no empty area. `node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang fr`: 3594/3594 keys, 33/33 maps, 736 originals
translated, "0 render sites or module literals still pending". `infrastructure.test.ts` adds French to the PT-REL review's formatter
check: through `index.ts`'s `formatNumber`, 14 values × 10 option sets print English's with only the digit-group comma swapped for
U+202F («24 000», «31 147», «1 234.5»), `fixed`/`scientific` unchanged, two export SHA-256 unchanged.

**Tests.** The French specs open French by the browser language alone, as PT-REL did for Portuguese.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `FRENCH_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `tests/fixtures/language.ts` `contextLocale` gives it
  `fr-FR` (FR0's `FRENCH_LOCALE`). Every language-parameterised spec (`shell`, `observatory`, `e01`–`e06`, `e08`, `radio`, `help`,
  `exports-identity`) now runs French in an fr-FR browser without `?lang=`; FR0's shell titles «a ?lang=fr preview fetches…» read
  «a French browser fetches…». No spec or map table changed for that: `langQuery`/`contextLocale` and `translatedFor` follow the
  registry.
- `areas.spec.ts`: FR0's "a partial language (French) fetches only its core, and no chunk for an empty area" (an en-US browser with
  `?lang=fr`) is gone; PT-REL's released-language test is one loop over Portuguese and French (the Portuguese title kept: «in a
  Brazilian Portuguese browser › Brazilian Portuguese (released) fetches its core…»; French «in a French browser › French
  (released)…»), with no empty area. The partial-language case stays covered by the qaa-XA fixture (`partialLanguage.test.ts`,
  `split.test.ts`), as after PT-REL (plan §1.2 #6).
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: four options with their `lang` on every load; FR0's two preview tests became FR-REL's detection test
  (fr, fr-FR, fr-CA, fr-BE, FR-ch, `['fr-SN','es-MX']` → fr; `['en-US','fr-FR']` → English; `['es-MX','fr-FR']` → Spanish;
  `['pt-BR','fr-FR']` → Portuguese; frr and br → English; a stored fr beats a pt-BR browser and a stored English an fr-FR one, `?lang=`
  beats both; a change from French to Portuguese and back is stored with a clean address) and a keyboard test in an fr-FR browser
  without `?lang=`. The first test's English case `['fr-FR','es-MX']` became `['de-DE','es-MX']` (its title says «de»); PT0's
  keyboard test steps over four options (its title «all three options» → «all the options»).
- `narrow.spec.ts` and `browsers.spec.ts` already loop the released languages: French joins with no change but a comment (the 320 px
  sweep of home, the 16 routes, the menu, the selector and its confirmation, OpenVireo's help and About; the engine smoke).
- `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts`: the scripts.css computed-style walk gains fr (320 and 1440 px).
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: PT-REL's routes test and home test are one loop over pt-BR and fr (Portuguese titles kept):
  every route of `dist/` in an fr-FR browser fetches the fr core and exactly its area's modules and logs nothing; an fr-FR home shows
  four options under the release CSP; fr-CA and fr-BE browsers open French and read «31 147» (the variable-star scope and the
  result line); the 404 page is quadrilingual.
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (FR0's test became FR-REL's; `UNRELEASED` closes every language; the "after ES-REL" English case
  `['fr-FR']` became `['de-DE']`), `languages.test.ts` (three released; four selector options; the gate rows of the planned-languages
  table now use Indonesian `id`, as PT-REL moved them to fr; plan §1.2 #7), `partialLanguage.test.ts` (the fixture's selector lists
  fr too), `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` (fr as released).

**Release text.** `public/404.html` is quadrilingual (English, Spanish, Portuguese, then «Page introuvable … Cette adresse ne fait pas
partie d’OpenVireo. Ouvrir l’observatoire.» under `lang="fr"`, linking `/?lang=fr`; the title adds «Introuvable»). README and
THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt carry "French translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's files and texts are unchanged." (the notice
paragraph mirrors Spanish's and Portuguese's, with the same uncertainty about whether the recorded CLEA License.rtf terms cover a
translation). `src/i18n/fr/index.ts`'s comment no longer says "partial until its release".

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front FR-SEO"); what touches the language rules:
- **Data only** (PT-SEO's design): an `fr` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['fr']`, `ogLocale:'fr_FR'`, prefix `/fr`,
  `app:'/?lang=fr'`), `src/seo/labs.fr.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX.fr='/fr'` (checked equal to the registry's `pagePath`), the `/fr/*`
  header rule and `public/og-image-fr.png` (rendered by `scripts/render-og-image.mjs fr`, LTR, twice with the same SHA-256). No
  renderer, plugin, boot or `index.html` body change. The observatory footer's labs link reaches `/fr/labs/` in French.
- **`hreflang` `fr` alone**: ISO 639-1 without a region is valid for Google and names one neutral French for every `fr-*` searcher,
  as the app opens it for every `fr-*` browser. The alternates on all 36 pages and in the sitemap are `en`, `es`, `pt-BR`, `pt`,
  `fr`, `x-default`.
- **Text rules**: written for this front from OpenVireo's own English pages (no batch): «vous» and vous-imperatives («Pointez»,
  «Mesurez», «Consignez», «Enregistrez»), infinitive for links and buttons («Ouvrir le TP», «Ouvrir l’observatoire»), sentence case,
  «TP» for a lab (D4), IAU OAE terms from the fr glossary («décalage vers le rouge», «diagramme en cône», «raies spectrales», «largeur
  équivalente», «séquence principale», «amas d’étoiles», «radiotélescope», «mesure de dispersion», «relevé»), the app's own French lab
  names, U+202F before `: ; ? !` and ’ written as characters by the French fixer (`fixSourceLiterals` over `labs.fr.ts` and the fr
  row), and the footer «Ce texte en français est une adaptation web d’OpenVireo, pas un texte du CLEA.» with the credit to Project
  CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF. The home's «Comment ça marche» adds the line on numbers («24 000», U+202F), as Portuguese's
  does, and says the language selector is at the top of the page (Front HEADER moved it there).
- **Tests** (`src/seo/languages.test.ts`): French runs the SC4 framework with profile `fr` and glossary field `fr` like the others;
  two French cases were made explicit rather than loosened: a title may start «OpenVireo : » (French puts U+202F before the
  colon), and a lab name may equal the English one only where its row says so (`sameName:['pulsars']`: «Pulsars» is French too). The
  profile's lint found three things the first draft got wrong, all fixed in the text: descriptions that dropped «CLEA»/«VIREO» or
  «TP» where the English names them, a bracketed «(Gettysburg College, avec le soutien…)» read as an English gloss, and «National
  Science Foundation» in prose failing the title-case rule (now «la NSF (National Science Foundation)»).

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`, compared with main c569054 built the same way, chunk hashes normalised).

classification 29.8; pulsar+radio 15.2; photometer 14.0; lss 9.1; cluster 8.6; exposure 8.2; cluster+photometer 5.4; pulsar 4.2;

## Front LAUNCH-1: the "Explore the sky" landing (2026-11-12)

A launch audit found that "Explore the sky" landed on the telescope-profile form, with the sky field and its Open dome about
1,850–2,300 px down on a desktop and 3,900 px down on a phone; that the camera, Multi-Spec and the first-visit tour asked to "turn
tracking on" while the control sat in the collapsed clock bar; and that the Pleiades guide's first step needed a button inside the
collapsed Original hot lists. Layout and navigation only: no VIREO behaviour, state contract or numerical code changed.
- **Order of the observatory page** (`src/OpenVireo.tsx`): heading, the three first steps, the sky field with the target card
  beside it (or below it on a phone), the feedback line, then the telescope profiles, the CCD camera, Multi-Spec, Move the
  telescope and the site clock, then the scope note. Same components and props. The dome cover's text sits in the upper part of the
  field, and on a phone the three steps share one row, their titles wrapping anywhere so a long translation cannot widen the page (`src/observatory.css`), so the field's Open dome is on the first screen at
  1440 × 900 and 375 × 812 (Open dome at y 461 and 565 instead of 2,712 and 4,132).
- **Show tracking control** (`src/observatory/TrackingShortcut.tsx`, provided by the shell): beside the camera's and Multi-Spec's
  "turn tracking on", the field spectrometer's `instrumentAccess.tracking` (from the Spectrometer page it opens the observatory
  first), the Hubble card's exact-centre note and in the tour's observatory step (which it ends). It opens the clock bar's
  disclosure and focuses Turn tracking on/off; it never presses it, so tracking and its release of spectral integration stay with
  VIREO's one button.
- **The Pleiades guide's list step** (and Continue while it is next) goes to the observatory with Original hot lists open and Load
  exercise list (PLEIADES.HTL) focused (its summary when the button is disabled).
- **New keys** (4 languages, same lines in each catalog): `runningClock.shortcut` "Show tracking control" (es «Mostrar el control de
  seguimiento», pt-BR «Mostrar o controle de acompanhamento», fr «Afficher la commande de suivi»), after `runningClock.trackingOff`;
  `pleiadesLab.goHotList` "Go to the exercise list" (es «Ir a la lista del ejercicio», pt-BR «Ir para a lista do exercício», fr
  «Aller à la liste de l’exercice»), after `pleiadesLab.goObservatory`. Glossary terms as in the existing tracking and hot-list
  strings; no number, so no thousands separator; the French fixer changed nothing. Pins: `coverage.test.ts` audits 148 UI files
  (TrackingShortcut), FR4 covers 650 keys, and `dynamic-key-sites.json` has the guide's new ternary.
- Not given a shortcut: Object X's instructions and guide step (they list tracking after opening the dome and pointing, and their

A launch audit found reverse-engineering wording in the primary text of the labs, classroom wording, a footer that read "local
educational research", no skip link, the dome cover's large Open dome below the fold with real browser chrome, and three small
rough edges. Presentation and wording only: no numerical code, state contract, export or file the site writes changed.
- **Research notes.** Provenance, reconstruction and uncertainty texts stay complete in the DOM, one click away, behind the existing
  `details.research-notes` disclosure ("Research notes"), written as plain markup rather than the `ResearchNotes` component (each
  component instance counted against `tests/render-budget.spec.ts`; the files this front touched now use the markup throughout).

  `.telescopeContext`, `starExposure.domain`, `.telescopeContext`, `skyProfiles.context`), Uniform draws and Reconstructed slit

## Indonesian: the folder, lints, ASCII groundwork and shell (ID0)

The first front of Indonesian (`id`), plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §1–§6 "ID0" in the research root, following FR0.
User decisions of 2026-11-19 (AGENTS.md; the plan's Q1 and Q2): U+202F between thousands («24 000», the decimal point kept, files
unchanged) and «Anda», capitalised. Indonesian stays hidden (decision 5 of 2026-11-09): only a `?lang=id` preview reaches it until
ID-REL. Its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**The registry line.** `{"tag":"id","dir":"id","catalog":"id.json","budgetFactor":1,"htmlLang":"id","pagePath":"id","autonym":"Bahasa
Indonesia","englishName":"Indonesian","match":["id","in"],"released":false,"thousands":" ","notices":{…}}`, no `help` yet. `in`
is the ISO 639 code withdrawn in 1989 that old stacks still send (`in-ID`); `Intl.getCanonicalLocales('in')` is `['id']`. Once
released, `id`, `id-*`, `in` and `in-*` browsers in any case open it; Malay (`ms`, `ms-*`, `zsm`), Javanese, Sundanese and `en-ID`
stay English (plan §2); `?lang=in`, `?lang=ID` and `?lang=id-ID` are no tag. The notices are plain ASCII: «Bahasa Indonesia tidak
dapat dimuat; situs ditampilkan dalam bahasa Inggris. Muat ulang untuk mencoba lagi.» and «Sebagian teks bahasa Indonesia tidak dapat
dimuat; bagian itu ditampilkan dalam bahasa Inggris. Muat ulang untuk mencoba lagi.» `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` takes this
line instead of its stand-in.

**The folder** `src/i18n/id/`, as FR0's: `index.ts` (`INDONESIAN`), `id.json`, `originals.json`, `maps/`, `glossary.json`,
`identical.json` (7 rows), `lint-allowed.json` (empty) and `id.test.ts`.
- **Translated:** FR0's shell slice, the 130 catalog keys, and the welcome's 27 `home.*` keys (Front HOME added them after FR0; FR1
  translated them for French), 157 keys and 1,702 English words; the complete maps `appShell`, `helpMenu`, `languageChoice` (legend
  «Bahasa / Language»), `projectLinks` and `tour`; the partial maps `objectXLab` (`nav`, `guideNav`), `polish` (`clusterGuide.nav`,
  `results.help`) and `variableStars` (`nav`); the eight VIREO captions the shell shows (Help «Bantuan», Topics... «Topik...»,
  Websites «Situs Web», 2MASS Catalog «Katalog 2MASS», About This Exercise... «Tentang Praktikum Ini...», Results Editors «Editor
  Hasil», Observational Results «Hasil Pengamatan», Pulsar Results «Hasil Pulsar»; Project CLEA and NVO kept, in identical.json).
  ID1 translates every original.
- **Style** (plan §3): «Anda», base-form verbs for buttons and menu items («Simpan investigasi», «Batal», «Muat ulang halaman»),
  base-form imperatives for instructions, no «silakan» where the English has no "please"; capitalisation mirrors the English for
  VIREO names, sentence case elsewhere; a lab is «praktikum» («Panduan praktikum Hubble»), the tour «tur», instructor mode «mode
  instruktur», Demonstration Mode «Mode Demonstrasi» with VIREO's English in brackets at the first mention in prose, a backup
  «cadangan». The phone menu button is «Menu»; the page bar's More is «Tindakan» (its accessible name «Tindakan investigasi lainnya»
  must start with the visible word, WCAG 2.5.3; «Lainnya» cannot start a natural phrase).
- **How it was translated:** three Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`, from the English, French and Spanish shown only as meaning
  context, one brief with the plan's §3–§5): the glossary seed, the 157 keys, and the eight maps with the eight originals. The Opus
  implementer read every row and changed 13 strings: «nilainya bukan bilangan berhingga» for "not finite" (not «tidak terhingga»,
  infinite), «Daftar garis spektrum» for the line list, «Konfirmasikan hitungan positif» for "Commit positive counts", «Jeda dulu
  sebelum analisis», «lakukan pembacaan langit baru» for "acquire fresh sky" (FR0's review correction), the receiver Record as
  «rekaman penerima (Record)» (Record is «Catat» only for a measurement), File › Preferences as «File › Preferensi» with the VIREO
  names glossed, «di bagian atas halaman» for "in the page header", «diagram HR pengamatan»; and in the maps the More button, «Langsung
  ke konten» for the skip link, «diagram kerucut» for the wedge plot help (the glossary's term, not the batch's «plot irisan») and the
  NAVO gloss followed by its Indonesian.
- **Glossary** (`glossary.json`, `id` field, `glossaryFrom(…,'id')`): the French glossary's 156 terms and 209 names re-targeted from
  the English, with the plan's §5.2–§5.3 forms (Save «Simpan», Record «Catat», Find «Temukan», Search «Cari», Slew «Gerakkan», Init/Go
  «Siapkan»/«Mulai», Scan «Telusuri», Clear «Kosongkan», Reset «Atur Ulang», Hot List «daftar objek», file «file», browser «browser»).
  Indonesian meN- drops the first letter of a verb in p, s, t or k (pulih → memulihkan, simpan → menyimpan), so those stems list both
  forms («pulih|mulih», «simpan|nyimpan», «tekan|nekan», «sapu|nyapu», «kedip|ngedip», «tampil|nampil», «potret|motret»). Restore
  investigation JSON is «Pulihkan investigasi (JSON)»; the Tools button «tombol Menu» (as fr and pt-BR). Ten `forbidden` rows: kepunahan,
  klaster, «geser merah», redshift outside glosses, «berkas» for a computer file (only a narrow pattern: «berkas» is the radio beam),
  peramban, «magnitudo tampak», «magnitudo absolut», «tetapan Hubble», pulsa. No IAU OAE Indonesian glossary exists: these are usage
  (Indonesian Wikipedia, ITB/Bosscha, BRIN, KBBI), not an official standard. Loose rows that later fronts should check before use:
  field of view («bidang pandang»), finding chart, epoch («epos|epok»), colour excess, aperture, readings, counts, tracking, slew and
  drive (the stem «gerak» is broad), trace, preset, bin, exercise («latihan|praktikum»).

**The Indonesian typography fixer** (`src/i18n/lint/indonesianTypography.ts`, pure, idempotent; `indonesianTypography.test.ts`, 7
tests). Indonesian punctuates as English does, so it only writes what batches cannot type reliably: straight "…" pairs → “…” (‘…’
inside an existing “…”; an odd count is left for the quotes rule, and a VIREO `fragment|` original keeps its straight quotes), and a
comma, space or U+00A0 between thousands → U+202F. A comma after a lone 0 («0,650») is a decimal comma the digits rule fails, never a
group. Placeholders, «A1» samples, URLs and `?lang=` values are kept. It reuses FR0's `fixSourceLiterals` for map and help sources
(characters, never escapes). Fix mode: `env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx vitest run src/i18n/id/id.test.ts`, refused in CI;
ID0's run wrote U+202F in three catalog values («50 000», «1 000» twice).

**The `id` lint profile** (`src/i18n/lint/profiles.ts`): Latin script, letter boundaries, Indonesian case folding, plural category
`other` only (CLDR: «1 bintang», «3 bintang»; no English override). `REGISTER.id:'anda'`, «kamu» expressible.
- **Register «Anda»:** kamu, kau, engkau, dikau, dirimu, kalian; the enclitic -mu on a fixed list (datamu, untukmu, hasilmu…), never
  a bare `\p{L}+mu` («ilmu», «semu», «temu»); lower-case «anda»; «Saudara» after a word. Pair-aware: «Silakan/Silahkan/Mohon/Tolong»
  and -lah imperatives (Kliklah, Pilihlah…) at a sentence start only when the English sentence is a bare command.
- **Typography:** the shared thousands rules with U+202F; guillemets; ‘ ’ at the top level; a space before : ; ! ? (the key «tombol ?»
  and «tanda ?» pass, as French's «touche ?»); U+202F outside a number; a Latin letter with a diacritic outside glosses (Å and
  Lemaître pass); joined prepositions (dimana, disini, diatas, kedalam… as whole words, so «kedalaman», «keluar» pass); a separated
  di- prefix before 27 verbs («di buka»); a repeated word; reduplication after a number or quantifier; «ke2»/«ke{n}»; 30 non-KBBI
  spellings; English spellings of loanwords; 21 Malay words; English function and UI words; claims («terjemahan resmi», «terjemahan
  CLEA», «ini adalah teks CLEA»; «bukan terjemahan resmi», «bukan teks CLEA» and «teks CLEA tidak berubah» pass).
- **The fixer's view and the paired checks** (`typographyCheck`): what the fixer would still change (straight quotes); a period
  between thousands («24.000») where the English groups the same digits with a comma; a U+202F group whose digits the English
  writes as a decimal («1 234» for "1.234"); and title case (three capitalised words, «Anda» not counted) only where the English has
  no such run, since VIREO's multiword names keep their capitals as in Windows Indonesian.
- **Plan deviation:** the ordinal rule flags «ke2» and «ke{n}» only, not «ke 2», which is also the preposition («Atur ke 30 detik»).
- **Fixtures** (`fixtures.ts`, `id`): 52 rows, a good and a bad string per rule, the register both ways, and the words the affix rules
  must not hit («magnitudo semu», «ilmu», «kedalaman», «keluar», «diameter»).
- **Calibration** on the shell (grapheme ratio, English over 12 graphemes, 136 strings): minimum 0.81, 5th percentile 0.87, median
  1.07, 95th percentile 1.45, maximum 1.82; French's median was 1.22. The band stays 0.7–1.8 (reported, not failed).

**ASCII groundwork** (plan §1.2 #1, #2, #6): Indonesian is the first language written in ASCII alone.
- `scripts/check-i18n-dist.mjs`: a map literal or original is a marker only with a letter outside ASCII, so Indonesian's maps-only
  modules (`pulsar`, `pulsar+radio`) would have no marker at release and fail. Now, only for a module with no marker by that rule,
  its map literals and originals of 16+ characters with at least two spaces that no English source, no other language and no other
  module of the language holds are markers; the report says «(found by its ASCII map text)». A module with a marker keeps exactly
  its markers, so es, pt and fr results do not change: `src/i18n/distCheck.test.ts` runs the check over the registry and asserts no
  module of es-419, pt-BR, fr or id uses the fallback, and an ASCII fixture language is found by it, fails when its marker is
  outside its chunk, and takes no non-prose literal.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts` `INDONESIAN_TEXT`: "letters" are words, `/(?<!\p{L})(?:yang|dan|untuk|dengan|tidak|Anda|dari|pada|ini|
  atau)(?!\p{L})|[“” ]/u`; none of them is in a text file of `public/original` or the export fixtures. `exports-identity.spec.ts`
  looks for them only in UTF-8 text files (`ASCII_LETTERS`), as for the script pseudo-locales, since binary data can spell a short
  ASCII word.
- The partial-language preview test of `areas.spec.ts` and the two preview tests of `language-selector.spec.ts` come back for
  Indonesian (FR-REL had removed them); ID-REL turns them into release tests.

**Tests.** `shell.spec.ts` runs Indonesian through `?lang=id` (`done:{shell}`); `areas.spec.ts` "a partial language (Indonesian)
fetches only its core, and no chunk for an empty area" (only `photometer` holds text); `language-selector.spec.ts`: id, id-ID, ID-id,
in-ID, ms-MY browsers and a stored id get English with four options and fetch no Indonesian module, `?lang=id` gets Indonesian, its
title and five options, a change stores nothing; the keyboard across five options. `exports-identity.spec.ts`: the toolbar Save
investigation family in Indonesian. Vitest: `languages.test.ts` (`[es-419 ✓, pt-BR ✓, fr ✓, id ✗]`, the notices, `in`/Malay rows),
`locale.test.ts` (before and after a simulated release), `direction.test.ts`, `pageBar.test.ts`, `rules.test.ts` (twelve profiles,
`id:'anda'`), `id.test.ts` (the registry line, the shell and welcome covered, map shapes, the fixer a no-op and no escape in a
source, `formatNumber` «24 000», «2 400 000», «1 234.5», «31 147», only `other`, and the lint clean).

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`): id 2 chunks, 8.5 KB gzip — core 8.2 KB gzip (24.6 KB raw after the review), photometer 0.3 KB; seven
areas empty. Deploy limits pass.

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, Opus, high effort; head 57d29ab). Verdict: merge after small fixes. Ran `npx tsc -b`, Vitest over
`src/i18n`, `src/components`, `src/seo`, `src/help` (1,541 passed), `playwright test tests/i18n --list` against origin/main (475 →
490: only the 15 Indonesian tests added, none renamed or removed), and the Indonesian Playwright tests on port 6305 (15 passed).
Confirmed: «Anda» throughout, no kamu/-mu, no unprompted silakan, no Malay, no diacritics, no claim; U+202F as characters; the fixer
idempotent and refused in CI; no `\b`/`\w`; the dist fallback only for markerless modules and es/pt/fr unchanged; the marker words in
no text file of `public/original` or `tests/fixtures`. Fixed after it:
- *Fix mode, medium:* `help/*.ts` sources now get the thousands rewrite only (`quotes:false`): plan §4.6 keeps VIREO's straight quotes
  in Latin-1 help, which the fixer would have turned into “ ” at ID-HELP.
- *Glossary, medium:* press-and-hold «tahan|nahan|tekan terus|nekan terus» (meN-); bin as a word or «kanal» («bintang» satisfied
  `bin`); analyzer «penganalisis» («analisis» satisfied `analis`); tab as a word («tabel» satisfied it); the observing site
  «lokasi», not «situs» (a website or an archaeological site): «Parameter Lokasi», «Informasi Lokasi», «Atur ulang lokasi radio»; Naked
  Eye Star «Bintang Kasatmata» (KBBI); the forbidden `redshift` row passes inside a bracketed gloss.
- *Strings:* the pulsar record on the welcome is «rekam sinyal pulsar» (Record is «Catat» only for a measurement); «catatan opsi» for
  a data record; «naik tegas»; «normalisasikan»; «Batalkan penyimpanan/pemulihan yang tertunda»; «saat ini» for "current" (not
  «terkini», latest); «Investigasi v11 lengkap»; «file telah tersimpan»; «File › Preferensi (Preferences)»; «terlebih dahulu» for
  "first" in the radio blocks and the pause message, as the maps write it.
- *Lint false positives:* «padam» and «tarikh» left the Malay list, «sila» is read only as a sentence-initial please, «tetapan» has its
  own message (pengaturan or opsi; konstanta for a constant); «Tombol ?» at a sentence start passes; «bukanlah terjemahan resmi» passes;
  «diantaranya» is caught. Two fixture rows.
- *Titles:* the generated shell titles read «an Indonesian chunk…» (the article follows the name's first letter; no other title changes).
## The digit notice (HI0, 2026-11-19)

Decided on 2026-11-09 (scripts plan Q7: numeric inputs do not accept full-width or other non-ASCII digits and say so clearly) and
assigned to Front HI0 by the coordinator on 2026-11-19 (C1), for every language: Hindi keyboards type ०–९, Japanese and Chinese
input methods in full-width mode ０–９, Arabic and Persian ones ٠–٩ and ۰–۹, and an English, Spanish, Portuguese or French visitor
may have such a keyboard too. Before, `Number('१२')` was NaN and the field showed its own message, which does not say why.

- **What it does.** `src/components/DigitNotice.tsx`, mounted once beside the app (`src/App.tsx`; the welcome shell has no field),
  listens on the document for `beforeinput`, `input`, `compositionend` and `focusout`. When a watched field receives a decimal
  digit (Unicode `Nd`) other than 0–9, a notice appears under the field: `app.digitNotice` "Type digits as 0–9. Digits such as
  {digits} are not read as numbers here." (es «Escribe los dígitos como 0–9. Aquí los dígitos como {digits} no se leen como
  números.», pt-BR «Digite os algarismos como 0–9. Aqui, algarismos como {digits} não são lidos como números.», fr «Saisissez les
  chiffres sous la forme 0–9. Ici, les chiffres tels que {digits} ne sont pas lus comme des nombres.»), `{digits}` being the distinct
  digits typed (at most six). It goes when the field holds none (and a number field is not left with an unparsable value), or when
  focus leaves the field. **Nothing else changes:** the value stays as typed, no digit is converted, every field keeps its own
  check and message, and no field's markup is touched (one listener instead of edits at about 80 input sites).
- **Watched fields** (`src/numericDigits.ts`, pure): `<input>` of type text, search, number or tel (no type is text), not
  read-only or disabled, and not inside `[data-any-digits]`. Free-text fields are watched too: every file the site writes reads
  digits as 0–9, so the sentence is true there as well. Textareas, email, URL and password fields are not watched.
- **Number fields.** Chromium drops digits it cannot parse from `type=number` (value `''`, `validity.badInput` false), so the
  text is read from `beforeinput` (its `data`, or the pasted or dropped plain text), and the `input` event that follows keeps the
  notice. Input-method text is read once committed (`compositionend`), never while it is being converted.
- **Placement.** A manual popover (`popover="manual"`), so it is drawn in the top layer above a modal `<dialog>` and its backdrop;
  it is rendered inside the field's dialog (or the body), so the modal dialog does not make it inert. Fixed under the field,
  clamped to the viewport (at most 320 px wide), following scroll and resize. `role="status"`, its text set one frame after it
  appears so screen readers announce it; `pointer-events:none`, never focused. Style in `src/style.css` (`.digit-notice`).
- **Tests.** `src/numericDigits.test.ts` (the detector: Devanagari, full-width, Arabic-Indic, Persian and Bengali digits; ASCII,
  letters, superscripts, Roman numerals and fractions bring none; the watched types); `src/numericDigitsAudit.test.ts` reads every
  `<input>` in `src/` and pins in `src/digit-notice-sites.json` each one whose type it cannot show is watched and every
  `data-any-digits` opt-out (both lists empty today); `tests/digit-notice.spec.ts` (a text field in the observatory's modal Set
  Date/Time dialog: the notice is inside the dialog, open as a popover, under the field and on top at its centre, gone when the
  digits are or on Tab, the value kept; Object X's number field; no notice for ASCII digits, other letters or non-decimal numerals;
  the notice in Spanish, Portuguese and French).
- **Indonesian** (registered by ID0 while HI0 was open) got «Ketik angka sebagai 0–9. Angka seperti {digits} tidak dibaca sebagai
  bilangan di sini.» when HI0 was rebased on it, and its shell pin 130 → 131. **Languages to come** add `app.digitNotice` to their
  catalog (it is a core key); nothing else.

## Hindi: the folder, the digit notice and the shell (HI0)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-hi.md in the research root. Hindi (`hi`, Devanagari) is the first non-Latin language; the
script groundwork (SC1–SC12) already gives it its fonts and line heights, the `qps-deva` stress, the `hi` lint profile, plurals,
Unicode help, canvas labels and screen dates. HI0 adds the language behind its `?lang=hi` preview.

- **Decisions** (AGENTS.md, 2026-11-19). The user: thousands grouped as English does («2,400,000», no lakh grouping), Western
  digits 0–9 on screen. The coordinator: आप with the polite -एँ form for buttons and instructions; «।» after Devanagari (Microsoft's
  Hindi guide writes «.»); CLDR's plural rule kept (0, 0.5 and 1 are `one`), checked on every counted message by the reviewers; the
  shared digit notice ("The digit notice (HI0, 2026-11-19)" above) built here for every language.
- **Registry** (`src/i18n/languages.json`): `hi`, `<html lang="hi">`, autonym «हिन्दी» (CLDR's own; the selector reads «हिन्दी
  (Hindi)»), `match:["hi"]`, `released:false`, no `thousands` (en-US comma), no `help` until HI-HELP, and the two notices in Hindi.
  `en-IN`, Urdu, Marathi, Nepali and other Indian-language tags stay English (`languages.test.ts`, `locale.test.ts`). **Budget
  factor 1.4**, measured on the shell: Hindi is 1.14× French gzip over the same 158 keys, 1.19× over the 40 longest, 1.13× over
  the five shell maps (Devanagari is 2.0× French's raw bytes; gzip recovers most of it); 1.19 × 1.1, rounded up, is 1.4 (the
  plan projected 1.6). HI1 measures again on the observatory and the 762 originals.
- **Folder** `src/i18n/hi/`: `index.ts` (`HINDI`), `hi.json` (161 keys: FR0's 131 shell keys with `app.digitNotice`, the 27
  `home.*` keys and the footer's `sky.attribution`, `sky.labsLink` and `sky.aboutPrivacy`, added after FR0), the shell maps (appShell, helpMenu, languageChoice, projectLinks, tour, complete; objectXLab, polish and
  variableStars, the menu names), 8 originals (Help, Topics..., Websites, 2MASS Catalog, About This Exercise..., Results
  Editors, Observational Results, Pulsar Results; Project CLEA and NVO kept in `identical.json`), `glossary.json`, `identical.json`,
  `lint-allowed.json` (one entry: the «? कुंजी» of the tour's help step is a key name, not punctuation) and `hi.test.ts`.
- **Translation.** Five Sonnet batches (two of 79 catalog keys, the maps with the originals, the glossary's 156 terms, its 209
  names) from the English, with French and Spanish as meaning context; the Opus implementer read every row and corrected:
  «गैर-आधिकारिक» for unofficial (the batches wrote «अनधिकृत», unauthorised, and «अनौपचारिक», informal), «ऑब्जेक्ट X» as the name
  (one batch wrote «वस्तु X»), Pleiades «कृत्तिका» (Hindi Wikipedia's कृत्तिका तारागुच्छ; one batch wrote «प्लीएडीज़»), «फ़ोटोमेट्री»,
  «बृहत् पैमाने की संरचना», «आवर्तकाल» for pulsar periods, «अज्ञात» for a coined word, a clearer decimal-point error, the wedge plot
  «शंकु आरेख», «पल्सर पैरामीटर» (plainer than प्राचल), and twelve lint findings (VIREO names to the glossary's forms: आभासी
  वेधशाला, परीक्षण मोड, टूल्स बटन, जर्नल; two stems widened: वस्तु सूची/सूचियाँ, घुमा/घूम).
- **Terms** checked on Hindi Wikipedia (2026-11-19): galaxy «आकाशगंगा» (as its हबल का नियम article; its आकाशगंगा article is the Milky
  Way, so the Milky Way is «मिल्की वे»), redshift «अभिरक्त विस्थापन» (with «रेडशिफ़्ट» in brackets at a first mention), Hubble
  constant «हबल नियतांक» (the plan's स्थिरांक is forbidden), wavelength «तरंगदैर्घ्य» (from दीर्घ, as NCERT writes it; the plan's
  दैर्ध्य is forbidden). No IAU OAE or current CSTT list was available: the glossary's `about` records the terms as usage.
- **Lint** (`src/i18n/lint/profiles.ts`, `case 'hi'`): a space before , ; : ? ! after Devanagari; English loanwords without their
  nukta (फाइल, ब्राउजर, जूम, फिल्टर, फोटो…; stems, never native words with फ or ज); a nukta on क ख ग; pañcamākṣara where the standard
  writes the anusvara (सम्बन्ध, सम्पादित…) and «हिंदी» (one form of the name, «हिन्दी»); formal words (चयन करें, परिवर्तित करें, पुनः
  प्रयास करें, अतः); English UI verbs outside a bracketed gloss or “ ” quotes. Fixtures in `fixtures.ts` (the two existing fixtures
  that wrote «फाइल» now write «फ़ाइल»). Length ratios stay reports for the reviewer.
- **Tests.** `hi.test.ts` (registry line, the catalog covers the shell and the welcome, map shapes, originals, NFC and no
  Devanagari digit or «हिंदी», «24,000»/«2,400,000», CLDR plurals, every lint rule); the registry fixture takes Hindi's real line;
  `pseudoScripts.test.ts` no longer counts `?lang=hi` as junk (it is Hindi's preview). Playwright: `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`
  `HINDI_TEXT` (`?lang=hi`, `done:{shell}`, letters U+0900–U+097F; “ ” are not Hindi's alone) and `tests/fixtures/language.ts`
  `HINDI_LOCALE='hi-IN'`, so `shell.spec.ts` runs Hindi; `areas.spec.ts` "a partial language (Hindi) fetches only its core" (as
  FR0's); `language-selector.spec.ts` (only `?lang=hi` opens and offers «हिन्दी (Hindi)»; hi, hi-IN, HI-in and en-IN browsers and a
  stored hi get English and request nothing of Hindi); `exports-identity.spec.ts` the toolbar family in `hi`; `digit-notice.spec.ts`
  the notice in Hindi.
- **Review (HI0, independent Opus).** No blockers. Fixed: the More button «अधिक» / «अधिक जाँच क्रियाएँ» (the visible word starts
  the accessible name; `pageBar.test.ts` gains Hindi); «दर्ज करें» kept for Record only (Enter is «लिखें»); recording pulsars is
  «पल्सरों के सिग्नल रिकॉर्ड करें», not «पल्सर दर्ज करें»; check vs investigation («मिलान» for the SHA-256 check); glossary names
  (पल्सरों का रेडियो खगोल विज्ञान, the Lab Table «प्रयोगशाला की मेज़», प्लेट नियतांक, «चयन» for a selection noun since «चुनाव» reads
  first as an election, both now forbidden forms, duplicates removed); button names quoted where they are a sentence's subject
  (“जाँच सहेजें”, “छोड़ें”, “सहेजें”); one form each of H-R आरेख, फ़ॉर्मेट and माप; the reader-gender question of the tour; the load
  notices no longer contain the core text «पेज रीलोड करें» (the dist check found it outside the core chunk). The digit notice: it goes
  with a field that leaves the page (Firefox fires no focusout then), Firefox's own conversion of other digits in a number field
  (१५ → 15) brings no notice, a deletion in a number field Chromium already emptied ends it, it opens above a field near the
  bottom, and its text changes in place for the same field; `digit-notice.spec.ts` adds paste and removal and passes in Chromium and
  Firefox (`--browser=firefox`). The lint's bare-nukta list gains इंटरफेस, कॉन्फिगरेशन, प्रोफाइल, ओवरफ्लो, फाइंडर.
- **The real-Hindi walk** (`tests/scripts/hindi.scripts.ts`, `npm run test:scripts`): the main suite's fonts have no Devanagari
  face, so its layout checks would measure tofu. Under the scripts config's Noto Sans Devanagari, at 320 and 1440 px: the welcome,
  the observatory's chrome, the header menu, each tour step, the language dialog, the instructor-mode confirmation and About, each
  with Devanagari on screen, `layoutProblems()`, `verticalInkProblems()`, and the welcome heading drawn by Noto Sans Devanagari
  (and DejaVu for its Latin) only. Each HI front adds its area. **`narrow.spec.ts` at HI-REL** skips Hindi with this reason (its
  320 px sweep under DejaVu would measure tofu); the real-Hindi walk is Hindi's narrow sweep.

## Indonesian: the observatory and VIREO's names (ID1)

The second Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID1" in the research root, with FR1 as its model.
Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never
CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR1's 555 observatory keys (the instruments' prefixes and the 104 guide keys the observatory files render; about 8,150
  English words). `id.json` now holds 712 keys. `id.test.ts` pins the prefix set, the 104 guide keys (`ID1_GUIDE_KEYS`) and `home.*`.
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `camera`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`,
  `observationMetadata` and `polish` (partial since ID0).
- **All of VIREO's originals at once:** the 762 texts, 739 in `originals.json` and 23 kept identical with an `identical.json` reason
  (OK, Zoom, Mode, Data, Item, Minimum, Min, Plot, Plot Data, Pulsar, Radio, Radio..., Asteroid, Edit..., File and its fragment,
  Gain:, ID #:, Diameter ('):, HH:MM:SS, V - Mv =, NVO, Project CLEA). `General` is «Umum» and the Site Information longitude
  letters are «BT»/«BB» (Bujur Timur/Barat; `ID_ONLY_ORIGINALS`). A label with a colon is never identical («Bujur:», «Lintang:»).
- **Fragments** keep VIREO's join spaces and its straight `"` where another piece closes it (`" Not Found.` → `" tidak
  ditemukan.`, the photometer's readings question), each with a `quotes` allowance as in French; the saved observations file pair,
  both pieces ours, uses “ ”; `fragment|Meter Radio Telescope` is «Teleskop Radio {diameter} Meter» (a `placeholders` allowance).
  VIREO's button names quoted in its own messages read in Indonesian («“Tidak” => …», «“Batal”», «“Pilihan” -> “Plot”»).

**How it was translated.** Ten Sonnet 5.5 batches, from the English with the reviewed French and Spanish (and Portuguese for the
originals) as meaning context: first the originals in three batches (250, 251, 251), read and corrected by the implementer, then
the catalog (3 × 185 keys) and four map batches (observatory, camera, the six small maps, polish), whose brief carried the
corrected originals so every web text names VIREO's controls exactly as the originals translate them. Then fix mode and the lint.
The Opus implementer read every row and changed about 90 strings beyond typography:
- **Originals (19):** «denyut» for the pulse width (never «pulsa», phone credit), «Praktikum Selesai?», «Pelajar (Daftar Pencarian)»
  (not «Siswa»), «Kesalahan Masukan!», «setempat» for local time, «Mohon maaf.» for VIREO's apology, «Latar Dasar», «Dek.:» and
  «Det.:», the quoted button names above, «Layar Teleskop» for VIREO's Telescope Display.
- **One form per term across batches:** «sideris» (sidereal; plan §5.2), «akuisisi» (acquisition; one batch wrote «perolehan»),
  «saluran» for a channel (the originals' «Multisaluran», «Tambah Saluran»), «dipulihkan» for "recovered" (one batch wrote
  «ditemukan kembali»), «Keluar» for instructor mode's Leave (one batch wrote «Tinggalkan»), «Berlaku langsung di sini» for "Live
  here", «layar teleskop» for VIREO's Telescope display apart from the telescope view «tampilan teleskop», «spektrometer
  multiobjek» (KBBI writes multi- joined), «AR»/«Dek.» and «jam» for the hour unit, «praktikum» for an exercise (and «latihan» only
  for the classification practice), «dikonfirmasi» for committed (not «dikomit»), «kesalahan» for error (not «galat»), «entri» for a
  file record, «Spasi» for the Space key, «mengeklik» (KBBI), «pintasan».
- **VIREO texts quoted in web prose in Indonesian, as French does:** «“Simpan perubahan opsi?”», the Multi-Spec «Tidak Ada
  Spektrum» box and its two messages, «Bidang berisi terlalu banyak wilayah!»; the printed Estimated Cluster Distance line keeps its
  English with an Indonesian gloss (the report line is English).
- **Glosses:** the Guide Star Catalog is «katalog bintang pemandu (Guide Star Catalog)», the ATNF catalogue «katalog pulsar ATNF
  (ATNF Pulsar Catalogue; …)», «dkk.» for et al.
- **Glossary:** Telescope Display «layar teleskop», Remove from HR Diagram «Keluarkan dari Diagram HR» (the English's HR, one form),
  help «bantu» (membantu), guide «panduan|pemandu», bin «kanal|saluran», and Topics... excepted in VIREO's Show Help Topics...
  («Tampilkan Topik Bantuan...»).

**Lint changes.** The title-case check now treats the English as title case when it has two capitalised words in a row (hyphenated
words count) or capitalised words for at least half of its words, and reads the English as written (a VIREO name in the English's
own brackets counts): VIREO's captions «No Selection!», «Time-Dependent Data», «Diameter is Invalid!» are Title Case, and their
Indonesian keeps the capitals. Four lint allowances with reasons (the radio caption's `{diameter}`, the two straight-quote pieces,
«of» in the cited University of Massachusetts); 28 identical entries in all.

**Tests.** `vireoSites.test.ts` `STRICT` gains Indonesian. `languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}` with
its 16 maps, so `observatory.spec.ts` runs its nine checks in Indonesian; `walks/observatory.ts`: `SAME_IN.id` (OK, Zoom, Mode, Data,
Pulsar(s), Radio, Item, Minimum, Min, Plot, Gain:, Edit..., File, Filter, Asteroid, ID #:, Diameter ('):, CCD, IR, Multi-Spec, Gain
(e⁻/ADU)), `NOT_SAME_IN.id` (General, E), the telescope-view button's Indonesian name «Teleskop ·», and «an Indonesian» in
generated titles. `exports-identity.spec.ts`: the hot list, options file and Field Information families gain `[id]`. `areas.spec.ts`
pins Indonesian's areas with text: classification, fits, photometer (as FR1).

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, two Opus readers at high effort, as PT3's split). *Originals and maps* (all 739 originals, the
23 identical ones and every string of the nine maps; `vitest src/i18n/id` 9/9): merge after fixes. Confirmed «Anda» throughout, no
Malay or diacritics, VIREO's `...`, colons, CRLF counts and fragment edge spaces kept, the §5.3 names consistent, every identical
original really the same word. Fixed after it: "YYYY/MM/DD" kept as typed (identical; «TTTT/BB/HH» collided with HH:MM:SS in the
same dialog); one word for epoch, «epok» (KBBI «epos» is an epic; glossary narrowed); «Entri Galaksi yang Diperiksa:» (records
scanned, not sought); the band-end sentence made grammatical; «potongan katalog» for a catalog tile everywhere; «Periode (s):» and
«PDot (s/s):»; «pencocokan» for fit; «Jangan Identifikasikan Pulsar»; «ditempatkan di daftar objek» in lower case as the other
messages; «Rata-Rata» capitalised in Title Case captions and «Cacah Rata-Rata» in one order; the HST option label in Indonesian with
VIREO's English; «Eksposur berlangsung…».

*Catalog and code* (the 555 keys, the lint, the tests; `tsc`; Vitest over `src/i18n`, `src/components`, `src/seo`, `src/help`;
`playwright test tests/i18n --list` against origin/main: 490 → 502, every Spanish, Portuguese and French title unchanged; both
Indonesian walks): merge after fixes. Fixed after it:
- *Two Vitest failures:* `dynamic-key-sites.json` refreshed (two Indonesian map heads, as FR1's); `distCheck.test.ts` now lets
  Indonesian use the ASCII fallback only for a module of maps alone — ID1's `classification` holds only the observationMetadata map,
  exactly the case the fallback exists for — while Spanish, Portuguese and French still never use it.
- *Title case, medium:* the looser rule above exempted any English with two capitalised words (703 of 1,451 strings; the reviewer's
  mutation test caught 246 of 324 capitalised mutants against 314 before). Now the English counts as title case with a run of three
  capitalised words, or, in a caption of up to eight words, a run of two or half its words capitalised (345 of 360 caught); one
  allowance for the Virtual Observatory message, whose «Mode Observatorium Virtual» names the option.
- *Strings (about 40):* «cacah» for counts everywhere (five keys wrote «hitungan»), «Kontrol spektrum … diblokir», «aktifkan
  pelacakan», «Selanjutnya:» for every guide's "Next:" (it clashed with Sebelumnya/Berikutnya), «acuan» for reference, «tidak
  tersertifikasi», «akuisisi» for acquire, VIREO's “Akses Semua Instrumen” (Access All Instruments), «undian» for a random draw,
  «Kosongkan pencarian», «Pilih objek» as the target card's title (the guides name it so), the entry titles «Entri PLEIADES.HTL
  {number}», «pelajar» where the English says students, «penggesernya», «filenya», «N/A»; VIREO's Min and Mins «Mnt» (KBBI; no longer
  identical); `SAME_IN.id` loses «Pulsars» (Indonesian writes «Pulsar») and the redundant Min.
- *Names later fronts must keep* (ID1's guide keys quote controls their own fronts translate): Target fotometri, Pengarahan teleskop
  sebenarnya, Tambah isian buku catatan, Hitung B−V, Filter fotometer, Posisi langit, Mulai integrasi, Ukur dengan fotometer, Aktifkan
  fotometer Pleiades, Teleskop siap, Analisis warna–magnitudo praktikum; Sumber klasifikasi, Pustaka latihan asli, Objek latihan,
  Latihan daftar program, Tinjau klasifikasi, Simpan klasifikasi, Coba muat ulang sumber, Standar − latihan, Simpan/Pulihkan
  investigasi lengkap. Those fronts use these forms or update the guide keys with them.

## Indonesian: E01 Hubble and E02 large-scale structure (ID2)

The third Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID2" in the research root, with FR2 as its model.
Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never
CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English. VIREO's own captions on these screens were already Indonesian since ID1.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR2's 786 keys still missing (the prefixes `lab`, `lssLab`, `hubble`, `velocity`, `ownResults`, `spectrum`,
  `spectrometer`, `galaxyExposure`, `skyExposure`, `starExposure`, `ssp`, `exposureIO`, `drift`, `skyProfiles`, `fieldSpec`, `lss`,
  `wedge`, `continuity` and `classLink.open`). `id.json` now holds 1,499 keys; `id.test.ts` pins the set (`ID2_KEYS`).
- **Maps, complete:** `engineView`, `spectralRange` and `wedge` (`src/i18n/id/maps/`), with `plural('id',…)` where English has it.

**How it was translated.** Four Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches and one map batch, from the English with the reviewed French and
Spanish beside each row as meaning context, under one brief that fixed one form per concept and carried ID1's originals and web
names. Then fix mode and the lint. The Opus implementer read every row and changed about 80 strings beyond typography:
- **One form per term:** «pencocokan» for a fit (never «fit»; «mencocokkan garis melalui (0,0)»), «keadaan acak» for the random
  state (not «status acak»), «penjajaran» for alignment (not «penyejajaran»), «pengarahan» for a pointing (not «arah»), «input» for
  an input (not «masukan»; also ID0's `project.help`), «buku kerja» for the workbook, «rata-rata» for mean, «pewaktu» for a timer,
  «kecepatan pekuliar», «Kerangka diam · Å» for the rest frame, «interval maksimum yang dikonfirmasi» for the committed interval,
  «geser-cache» for cached-pan (`lab.limitsExtended` aligned), «AR»/«Dek.» everywhere in prose («AR/Dek.»), «1%» without a space.
- **One form per control:** «Simpan CSV cacah galaksi», «Simpan JSON sesi galaksi», «Pulihkan JSON sesi galaksi (hingga 2 MiB)» as
  the star and sky ones; «Analisis galaksi hasil akuisisi» and «Analisis bintang hasil akuisisi» wherever the guides quote them;
  «Batalkan dan buang»; «Plot catatan saya pada diagram kerucut» in the button and the guide; the wedge is «diagram kerucut».
- **VIREO messages quoted in Indonesian** in VIREO's Title Case, as `originals.json` writes its captions: «Tidak Ada Bintang di
  Bidang!», «Indeks Spektrum Tidak Valid!», «Anda Tidak Dapat Mencatat Spektrum Bintang Ini!» (spectrometer messages not in
  `originals.json`); the Windows runtime's «Access violation … in module ‘CLEA_VEO.exe’» stays English.
- **Glosses:** «katalog bintang pemandu ringkas (Guide Star Catalog)»; redundant ones dropped («Simpan Data (.TXT)», «Plot Data»).
- **Words:** the coordinate fields' labels «Jam asensio rekta» … «Detik busur deklinasi» (the English has no «·»); «Penggunaan
  ditolak» for the field spectrometer's Use refused; the `{workbook}` placeholder that a global replacement had translated restored.
- **Identical (25):** symbols, units and formats (v · km/s · float32, v/D · km/s/Mpc, SHA-256, JD, z, #, V (km/s), °, px), «Status»,
  «log», «file», «bin», «Plot» and VIREO / HUBBLE, each with its reason. One lint allowance (`lss.dialog.reused`).

**Tests.** `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` adds `e01` and `e02` with `E01_E02_MAPS.id`, so the E01 and E02 walks run in Indonesian;
`walks/e01.ts` and `walks/e02.ts` gain `SAME_IN.id` (OK, Zoom, the velocity column's format, VIREO / HUBBLE, and Log, No., File, Status, Bin in E01; OK, Minimum, Plot Data, Status, Plot, File in E02; Indonesian is the first language to keep these computing words). `exports-identity.spec.ts`: a shared `catalogStart(tag, english)` (FR's start
uses it), and the seven E01/E02 families gain `[id]`. `areas.spec.ts` pins Indonesian's areas with text: classification, exposure,
fits, lss, photometer.

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, two Opus readers at high effort: the Hubble, analyser and guide keys with the engine map; the
exposures, field spectrometer, survey and wedge keys with the two other maps, the code and the tests). Both: merge after fixes; no
meaning errors, no claim of CLEA equivalence, placeholders and numbers intact, VIREO's names matching `originals.json`. Fixed after
them:
- *Tests:* the walks' `SAME_IN.id` lacked the identical computing words the E01 and E02 screens show (Log, No., File, Status, Bin;
  Status, Plot, File); added.
- *A quoted control:* two guide rows quoted the VIREO recipe as «… pencocokan melalui nol», the option reads «… melalui titik nol».
- *One form:* the hour unit «jam» everywhere (four keys wrote «h»; the wedge's «12j» is «12 jam»); «berhingga» for finite,
  «dikecualikan» for excluded, «data Hubble», «intersep», «pemutaran ulang» for the replay noun, «cache» (not «tembolok»), «GSC
  ringkas», «Lapisan celah», «Eksposur seed baru» as the button, «cadangan» for a fallback, «Bawaan» for VIREO's Default, «0.4m» as
  the English, «Pusatkan ulang kontrol» as the button, «tampilan cache NGC 7499/7503» (only CorBor and UM-I are cached-pan).
- *Thousands with U+202F* in counts: «12 000», «24 000», «1 000», «10 000 ms», «1 048 576»; typed bounds such as the seed range stay
  as the English writes them, as in French.
- *Words:* «Kolom Catatan» and «isi Catatan» where VIREO's Remarks field could read as records; «kurva … yang diturunkan dari data
  simpan» for the save derivative; «eksposur berdurasi positif»; «akuisisi setiap galaksi … dan kirim cuplikannya»; «tanpa
  pergeseran» for unshifted; «Mengekspos»; «masih tertunda» for pending; «pemeriksa DAT»; «Pembulatan di nilai tengah» for a tie;
  «atur ulang» and «kisi» in the procedure list; the field spectrometer's added page name dropped; smaller wording.
- *Kept:* drift stays «pergeseran» (the glossary's form; a reviewer suggested «hanyutan»).

## Indonesian: E03 spectral classification (ID3)

The fourth Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID3" in the research root, with FR3 as its model.
Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never
CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English. VIREO's own captions on these screens were already Indonesian since ID1.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR3's area still missing, 741 keys: every key of the classification module and of classification+cluster, and the
  core keys E03 renders (the prefixes `class`, `classLines`, `classLink`, `classAtlas`, `classImport`, `classLab`, `feature`,
  `cursor`, `probe`, `photo`, `combined`, `combinedWidth`, `viewport`, `inspection`, `sampledTrace`, `reverseTrace`, `sampledDiff`,
  `range`, `widthSource`, `widthCoordinates`, `sharedMarks`, `comparisonMark`, `paneSource`, `panMarks`, `obs`, `nativeResults`,
  `nativeEdit`, `nativeDelete`, `nativeList`, `nativeText`, `nativeCsv`, `program`, `programImport`, plus
  `continuity.restoreExposure` and `ssp.header`). `id.json` now holds 2,240 keys; `id.test.ts` pins the 772 keys of the E03
  prefixes (`ID3_KEYS`, as FR3's 771 with the key en.json gained since).
- **Maps:** none new (E03 renders no map that ID1 did not translate).

**How it was translated.** Four Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches, from the English with the reviewed French and Spanish beside each row as
meaning context, under ID2's brief (one form per concept, ID1's originals and the web names earlier fronts fixed). Then fix mode and
the lint (18 findings: «cuplikan» for a snapshot, «kisi titik ujung» for the endpoint grid, «tekan dan tahan», «CLEA» kept in the
DAT policy, «halaman kode (code page)», a literal “?” quoted, eight placeholder formats and the hash name in `identical.json`, the
option «Data Bergantung Waktu» allowed its capitals in `lint-allowed.json` as in `lss.dialog.reused`). The Opus implementer then read
every row; with the lint, 121 of the 741 strings changed:
- **One form per term:** a sample is «sampel», sampled is «tersampel» (the option «Jejak intensitas tersampel», «fluks tersampel»),
  to sample is «mengambil sampel», a sampler «pengambil sampel»; a snapshot is «cuplikan» (the batches used «cuplikan» for both);
  the unknown spectrum is «tidak dikenal» (not «tak dikenal» or «tidak diketahui»); a comparison pane is «panel perbandingan»; a
  response is «respons», an answer «jawaban», a grade «nilai» (not «tingkat» or «penilaian»); an encoding is «penyandian» (not
  «pengodean»); an input «input» (not «masukan»); alignment «penjajaran»; the random state «keadaan acak»; provenance «asal-usul»;
  finite «berhingga»; acquired «hasil akuisisi»; printed «tercetak»; a guard «pengaman»; «AR» for RA in prose and field names.
- **One form per control:** «Standar berdekatan» wherever the guides quote the Adjacent standards display; «Atur ulang jendela
  panjang gelombang» where the help names the Reset button; the web notebook's remarks are «keterangan» (VIREO's own Remarks field
  stays «Catatan», ID2); the Delphi type names Single, Double and Extended stay as the English writes them, as in French.
- **Words:** «SSP dengan nilai bertanda» for a signed SSP (not «bertanda tangan», signed in ink); «Mode Selisih yang tercatat» for
  the recorded Difference mode; «Spasi» for the Space key; «Ini bukan nilai.» for "This is not a grade."; smaller wording.

**Tests.** `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` adds `e03`, so `e03.spec.ts` runs in Indonesian (the walk, View original, and the 320 and 1440 px
layouts). The E03 export family of `exports-identity.spec.ts` gains `[id]` (Indonesian has done `e01`, so its star exposure buttons
go through the catalog like every E03 control). `areas.spec.ts` adds `cluster` to Indonesian's areas with text, since E03 fills the
shared classification+cluster module that the cluster routes also fetch (as for French after FR3).

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, two Opus readers at high effort: the classification, comparison, cursor, probe, photo, trace,
range and width keys; the observation notebook, the native DAT/TXT/CSV inspectors and editors, the program list, and the tests and
docs). Both: merge after fixes; no meaning reversals, no claim of CLEA equivalence, every hedge and placeholder kept, VIREO's names
matching `originals.json`, the test wiring as FR3's. Fixed after them:
- *Quoted controls:* the reverse option is «Balik tampilan jejak» in the guide as on the checkbox; `classLab.introOptions` now
  equals `lab.introOptions` (same English: «menu praktikum VIREO», «Klasifikasi bintang»); «Berikutnya dalam Daftar» for VIREO's
  Next on List; the page title «Klasifikasikan spektrum bintang» as an imperative, like the guide's.
- *One form:* «berhingga» (six keys wrote «terhingga»); «Objek X»; «sampel» for printed samples; «asal-usul hasil akuisisi»;
  «panel perbandingan»; «pusat» for a center; «hasil akuisisi» for acquired; «tertunda» for pending; «zoom»; «keterangan» for the
  web notebook's notes; «Konverter»; «tanggal Julian»; «pemakaian ulang/penambahan» for the reuse/append setting; «cacah rata-rata
  per detik»; «dikonfirmasi»; round-half-to-even as «pembulatan ke genap terdekat», as the exposure keys write it; typed field
  values are text, never «entri» (a DAT record).
- *Meaning and grammar:* "prepends" as «menambahkan di depannya»; unsupported spacing, units or encoding; «seperti dalam program
  terkompilasi»; the immediately preceding edit; a missing noun in `sampledDiff.grid`; «angka format VIREO» where native and original
  would both read «asli»; quoted label names (“foto tidak tersedia”); a teaching exercise as «praktikum pembelajaran»; smaller wording.


## Indonesian: E04 Pleiades and E05 variable stars (ID4)

The fifth Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID4 ∥ ID5" in the research root, with FR4 as its model;
it ran beside ID5 (radio telescope). Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The translations are web
adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English. VIREO's own captions on these screens were
Indonesian since ID1, so this front adds web text only.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR4's area, 587 new keys (about 8,600 English words): every key of `ph`, `pa`, `pleiadesLab`, `nc`, `db`, `ce`,
  `clusterLab`, `ownHr` and `obsHr`; with ID1's 64 guide and photometer keys, the 651 keys are complete (`id.test.ts` pins them,
  `ID4_KEYS`). `id.json` now holds 2,827 keys.
- **Maps, complete:** `clusterHr` and `clusterData` (new), and `variableStars` (partial since ID0, only `nav`), with the
  variable-star counts grouped with U+202F («31 147», the registry's `thousands`, written as the character).
- **Identical to English** (`identical.json`, 21 rows with reasons): band letters and symbols (`obsHr.blockedField.*`, `obsHr.ebv`,
  `obsHr.modulus`, `obsHr.colBV`, `obsHr.colIndex`, `nc.axisColor`, `db.columnEbv`, `ce.inspectedInfo`), placeholder formats
  (`ph.fixed`, `ph.seriesSecondsOption`, `variableStars.typeOption`), SHA-256, «Filter», «Target» and «Status» (the same words in
  Indonesian), «Min / ampl.», the empty action header, the em dash for no period and the help viewer's OK.

**Turn-off and the other terms (glossary, before the batches).** ID0's glossary rows already held turn-off › «titik belok» and
membership › «keanggotaan»; the brief wrote «titik belok deret utama (turn-off)» at a string's first mention, as the plan (§5.2)
and Indonesian Wikipedia's «titik belok deret utamanya» suggest, and «titik belok» after it. Absolute and apparent magnitude are
«magnitudo mutlak» and «magnitudo semu» (the forbidden «absolut» and «tampak» never appear); reddening «pemerahan», interstellar
extinction «ekstingsi antarbintang», colour index «indeks warna», distance modulus «modulus jarak», isochrone «isokron», ZAMS
«deret utama usia nol (ZAMS)», ages «juta tahun» and «miliar tahun» (spelt out, as French did; Spanish and Portuguese keep Myr).

**How it was translated.** Five Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches (113 and 112 `ph` keys; `pa`, `pleiadesLab` and `nc`, 108; `db`, `ce` and
`clusterLab`, 128; `ownHr` and `obsHr`, 126) and one for the three maps, from the English with the reviewed French and Spanish as
meaning context, under ID2/ID3's brief extended with the E04/E05 terms above. Then fix mode and the lint (the notebook-entry glossary
term, «pembacaan langit baru» for fresh sky, the manual's title «Fotometri fotolistrik Pleiades» with its English gloss, a doubled
«tetap», the VIREO Tools items glossed in brackets since `originals.json` has no entry for them, the hyphen of «warna-magnitudo»
where the English has one). The Opus implementer then read every row and changed about 90 strings:
- **One form per term:** a notebook entry is «isian buku catatan» (the batches wrote «catatan», «entri» and «isian»; «entri» stays a
  record of a DAT or catalogue file, «catatan» a record of VIREO's Observational Results list, as ID3); aperture «apertur» (as ID1's
  photometer and `originals.json`; one batch wrote «bukaan»); a model trial «percobaan» (one batch wrote «uji coba»); retry the
  model «Coba muat ulang model yang dipilih» on both buttons; the exercise list «daftar praktikum» (not «daftar latihan»: «latihan»
  is the E03 practice); exclude and exclusion «kecualikan» and «pengecualian» for the web's own exclusions, «Keluarkan» only for
  VIREO's Remove from HR Diagram; «versi web ini» for "this port"; «Catat Hubble» for Hubble Record, as ID3; «Entri daftar objek
  {n}» for a hot-list entry; the cluster laboratory «laboratorium»; «tidak dimigrasikan» for "not ported"; «32-bit».
- **Quoted controls:** «Muat set data terpilih», «Pulihkan semua titik yang dikecualikan» and «Tipe Daftar» (VIREO's Type of
  Listing) where the maps quote them.
- **Words:** «tetap» for the pinned field (not «tersemat»), «Dikecualikan oleh Anda», «Entri katalog asli», «diatur ulang» (not
  «direset»), «tampilan teleskop terbaru», «Pasangan salinan Anda», «Kembali ke laboratorium».

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/dynamic-key-sites.json`: the id variableStars map's `printCurveLayout` head, as for Spanish, Portuguese and French.
- `src/i18n/id/id.test.ts`: the two new maps and `variableStars` complete (`ENGLISH_MAPS`, `COMPLETE_MAPS`), the ID4 coverage check
  (`ID4_KEYS`, 651), the fixer a no-op and the id lint over 2,827 keys.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` gains `e04` and `e05`; `E04_E05_MAPS.id`. So `e04.spec.ts` and `e05.spec.ts`
  run in Indonesian.
- `tests/i18n/walks/e04.ts` `SAME_IN.id`: Target, Filter, Gain (e⁻/ADU), File, OK. `walks/e05.ts` `SAME_IN.id`: Item, ID #:, OK,
  Data, File, Diameter ('):, Status, Min / ampl. (each in `identical.json` or ID1's identical originals).
- `tests/i18n/e05.spec.ts`: the View original test accepts, per language and listed exactly (`WEB_ALSO_ORIGINAL`), a web text that is
  also a VIREO caption's translation: only Indonesian's variable-star column «Tipe», which is also VIREO's «Tipe» (Type); every other
  text and language is checked as before.
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the E04 and E05 families gain `[id]` (`ID_E04(key)` throws for a key Indonesian lacks) and
  `catalogCount` writes «31 147» for Indonesian.

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, two Opus readers at high effort: `ph`, `pa` and `nc`; `pleiadesLab`, `db`, `ce`, `clusterLab`,
`ownHr`, `obsHr`, the three maps and the tests). Both: merge after fixes; no claim of CLEA equivalence, every hedge and placeholder
kept, VIREO's names matching `originals.json`. Fixed after them:
- *Pin:* `split.test.ts` needs the new `printCurveLayout` head in `dynamic-key-sites.json` (FR4 added French's the same way).
- *Quoted controls:* the notebook button is «Tambah isian buku catatan» (ID1's guide name, now also `ph.record`); the pointing panel
  «Pengarahan teleskop sebenarnya» in ID1's two guide keys and ID1's list of names above; «Konfirmasi pencocokan model yang dipilih»
  in the cluster guide; «Plot catatan pengamatan saya» on the button and where `ownHr.adaptation` quotes it.
- *One form:* «diagram HR» (VIREO's own «Diagram HR», ID1's form; the batches had brought in «H-R»); «AR {ra} jam» (ID2's hour unit);
  «potongan katalog» for a catalogue tile (ID1); a record of VIREO's Observational Results list «catatan» also in the
  saved-observation HR tool, a cluster catalogue record and the DAT records «entri»; VIREO's Remarks «isi Catatan» where «catatan»
  could read as records (ID2); «Catat Hubble»; «ringkas» for compact; «daftar praktikum»; «Batalkan» for the web dialog's Cancel;
  «tampilan teleskop»; «pencocokan» for a fit.
- *Meaning:* the galaxy sky gate's level «bisa di atas atau di bawah 80» (not «di kedua sisi»); round-half-to-even «ke bilangan
  bulat terdekat (nilai tepat setengah ke bilangan genap)»; «terlebih dahulu» before the sky reading it qualifies; a nearby (not
  nearest) GSC star; the callback count «jumlah», not detector «cacah»; «acuan historis 410 tahun cahaya dari manual»; «Kurva emas …
  kurva ungu»; «log(usia/tahun)» where the English writes log.
- *Test:* the E05 View original exemption narrowed to «Tipe» in Indonesian (the first version also stopped checking other
  languages' captions that a web map happens to share).

## Indonesian: the radio telescope (ID5)

The sixth Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID4 ∥ ID5" in the research root, with FR5 as its
model; it ran beside ID4 (E04 and E05). Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The translations are
web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English. VIREO's own radio captions were Indonesian
since ID1, so this front adds web text, and one rendering context for the direction buttons.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR5's 646 keys, every key of `radio.*`, `radioRoute.*`, `radioAccess.*`, `radioReceiver.*`, `radioSimOptions.*` and
  `pulsarParameters.*`; 645 were new (`radioRoute.nav` came with ID0), about 9,950 English words. `id.test.ts` pins the 646 as
  `ID5_KEYS`; `id.json` now holds 3,472 keys with ID4's.
- **Maps, complete:** `radioAntenna` (324 strings), `radioR1`, `radioRecordSave` and `radioFinishSave`.
- **Still English until ID6:** the receiver's Record Save dialog also renders texts of the `pulsar` map (`pulsar.rawExport.*`,
  `pulsar.save.how`, `.extensionRule`, `.origin`, `.warning`, `.choose`, `.chooseAgain`, `.refusal.stale`). `radio.spec.ts` lists
  them as `TODO_ID6_PULSAR_KEYS` (FR5's mechanism), allowed only in `id` and only on that step; ID6 removes the list. ID6's
  `pulsar.save.open` and `pulsar.rawExport.open` must read «Simpan tangkapan sebagai .PLR (terverifikasi)…» and «Unduh tangkapan
  sebagai .PLR…», which `radioReceiver.record.scope`, `.yesGated` and `radioSave.scope` quote.
- **Kept as recorded:** pulsar names, Pulsars.dat values, the stored ID “UNKNOWN”, «No Data», file names and hashes, and the
  constants the English writes ungrouped (the seed range 0 to 4294967295, the int32 bounds).

**The direction buttons: U, S, T, B.** VIREO's panel buttons N, S, E and W read «U», «S», «T» and «B» (Utara, Selatan, Timur, Barat).
ID1 had translated VIREO's E and W as the Site Information longitude letters «BT» and «BB» (Bujur Timur, Bujur Barat), and the panel
read the same originals, so its buttons would have read «BT» and «BB». The panel now asks for its captions in a `direction` context
(`vireo(caption,'direction')` in `RadioControlPanel.tsx`, every site that shows a button's caption or its original), and
`originals.json` gains `direction|N` «U», `direction|E` «T» and `direction|W` «B» (S stays S). The plain originals keep their
meaning, so the longitude letters stay «BT»/«BB»; a language with no `direction|` entry falls back to the plain original, so Spanish,
Portuguese, French and the other languages render exactly as before. Every string that names the buttons says U/S/T/B; the keyboard
note names the physical keys E, N, S and W and which button each holds («N untuk tombol U, E untuk tombol T, W untuk tombol B»),
since the code reads the typed letter (`src/core/radioKeyboard.ts`).

**How it was translated.** Seven Sonnet 5.5 batches, from the English with the reviewed French and Spanish as meaning context, under
ID2–ID4's brief extended with the radio terms and the direction rule: four catalog batches (174 keys of `radio`, `radioSimOptions`
and `pulsarParameters`; 130 of `radioRoute`; 141 of `radioAccess`; 200 of `radioReceiver`) and three map batches (the two halves of
`radioAntenna`; the three small maps). Then fix mode and the lint (ID0's glossary names «Masukan skenario» for Scenario inputs and
«Dengarkan jejak» for Trace audition, «audisi» for an audition, «memulihkan» for restore, glosses without a second `...` or ▸, three
sentence-case rephrasings, 18 placeholder formats and identical words in `identical.json`, and a `lint-allowed.json` row for VIREO's
Telescope Type names «Dapat Diarahkan Penuh» and «Hanya Transit» in `radioR1`). The Opus implementer read every row and changed
145 strings (70 catalog, 75 map):
- **One form each:** slewing (noun) «gerakan» in the whole antenna map (one half wrote «gerak»; Kecepatan Gerak and the slew timer
  «pewaktu gerak» keep VIREO's word); «asensio rekta» and «deklinasi» in lower case in prose; on/off «aktif»/«nonaktif» (the map had
  «menyala»/«mati»); aperture «apertur», as ID1's «Apertur Antena» and ID4; "not ported" «tidak dimigrasikan» (the batches wrote
  «dipindahkan» and «diport»), kept apart from a mark «tidak dipindahkan» (not moved); the dome state «keadaan kubah»; Record armed «dalam keadaan siaga»; Receiver Open «pembukaan
  penerima»; built-in defaults «nilai bawaan» beside compiled defaults «nilai bawaan terkompilasi»; a catalogue record «entri»; typed
  text «isian», never «entri»; the receiving runs «proses penerimaan».
- **Quoted controls:** «Gunakan nilai bawaan terkompilasi: diameter 100, opsi akses nonaktif» on the button and wherever it is quoted;
  the page header «bagian atas halaman» and the instructor bar «bilah mode instruktur».
- **Meaning:** a slew answered No is «Gerakan tidak dikonfirmasi» (FR5 and PT5's correction); the altitude-limit message «batas
  cakrawala (arah: {direction})»; «hanya opsi Hanya Transit (Transit Only) yang mengubah perilaku VIREO»; «Tanggal tidak valid».
- Term choices kept from the batches: recording (noun) «rekaman», capture «tangkapan», Record «Catat», tick «detak», sweep «sapuan»,
  mark «tanda» (Tanda pertama/kedua), audition «audisi» and «Dengarkan jejak», reseed «pengaturan ulang seed», host clock «jam
  komputer», Search from top / Find next «Cari dari awal» / «Temukan berikutnya», Match case «Cocokkan huruf besar/kecil», Move
  up/down «Naikkan»/«Turunkan».

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/id/id.test.ts`: the four radio maps in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`, the ID5 coverage check (`ID5_KEYS`, 646), the
  three `direction|` originals (in `ID_ONLY_ORIGINALS`, with «BT»/«BB» still pinned), a source check that every
  `vireo(manualButtonCaption(…))` and `vireo(RADIO_LAB_CAPTIONS[…])` in `RadioControlPanel.tsx` passes the `direction` context (the
  walk never reaches the leftover-button note, so the walk alone could not catch a missed site), the fixer a no-op and the id lint.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` gains `radio`; `RADIO_MAPS.id`. So `radio.spec.ts` runs its checks in
  Indonesian (the walk against English, View original, the Indonesian originals, the canvas pieces, 320 and 1440 px).
- `tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts`: `TODO_ID6_PULSAR_KEYS` (above). `walks/radio.ts`: `SAME_IN.id` (VIREO's File, OK and Mode, ID1's
  identical originals) and `NOT_SAME_IN.id` (No, no, Hrs and Bytes, which Indonesian translates; VIREO's Min,
  which Indonesian writes «Mnt»; and the button letters N and E, so an English letter left on a direction button fails the walk).
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the ES8a radio family gains `[id]`: the same names and SHA-256 as English, and the route's
  Indonesian `h1`.
- `tests/i18n/areas.spec.ts`: Indonesian's pinned areas gain `pulsar` and `radio` (`pulsar` fetches the shared pulsar+radio module,
  which holds the `radioR1` map).

**Independent review** (2026-11-19, two Opus readers at high effort: `radio`, `radioRoute`, `radioSimOptions`, `pulsarParameters` and
the antenna map; `radioAccess`, `radioReceiver`, the three small maps, the direction code and the tests). Both: merge after fixes; no
claim of CLEA equivalence, every hedge and placeholder kept, the U/S/T/B buttons and the E/N/S/W key note correct, the other
languages unchanged. Fixed after them:
- *Code:* the leftover-button note (`antenna.manual.stateLeftover`) read its original letter without the `direction` context, so a
  leftover E or W button would have named «BT»/«BB»; it now passes the context, and the source check above guards every site.
- *Replacement slips:* «bukaan» › «apertur» had turned «pembukaan» (Receiver Open) into «pemapertur» in three strings, and
  «dipindahkan» › «dimigrasikan» had turned three "not moved" marks into "not ported"; both restored.
- *Meaning:* «Byte opsi akses aktif» (the byte is set, not a set of bytes); the numeric-keypad keys (not the digit row);
  «(bukan nilai bawaan yang dipulihkan)»; «tidak ada bagian radio yang membacanya»; «Tambahkan Objek Saat Ini (Add Current)»; the
  key-release outcome names the keyboard key and the button it holds apart; «Tujuan pesan penutupan penerima» (recipient, not
  receiver); «Tertutup, sebagaimana jendela utama membiarkannya saat dimulai».
- *One form:* «gerakan» for the slew noun (five «penggerakan» outside VIREO's names), «keadaan» for state, «nonaktif» and
  «aktifkan/nonaktifkan» for options and Tracking, «disiagakan» for an armed timer and the Record Save intro, «yang disertakan» for
  the bundled Pulsars.dat (not «bawaan», the word for defaults), «Masukan» for the scenario's inputs, «Data opsi» (not «Rekaman», the
  recording), «Det. Horz.» (VIREO's Horz. Secs), «ms» (not «md»), «1 000 entri» with U+202F, «halaman» for the web route and «jalur
  awal» for the captured startup route, «Opsi yang berlaku» for live options, «memegang» for holding the current pulsar data, «rutin»
  (not «rutinitas» or «hook»), «Gerakan tidak diperlukan», «Bergerak…».

## Indonesian: E06 pulsars and E08 Object X (ID6)

The seventh Indonesian front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2 "ID6 ∥ ID-HELP" in the research root, with FR6 as its
model; it ran beside ID-HELP (the Indonesian help files). Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. The
translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English, and E08's object stays hidden
until the reveal in Indonesian too. VIREO's own captions on these screens were Indonesian since ID1, so this front adds web text only.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** the 134 keys left, FR6's scope: every key of `pulsarLab.*`, `plr.*` and `fits.*` (137 with ID0's three `nav` keys),
  about 2,100 English words. `id.json` now holds every en.json key (3,606); `id.test.ts` pins the 137 as `ID6_KEYS` and checks that no
  en.json key is missing.
- **Maps, complete:** `pulsar` (443 strings), `pulsarSavedRows` (83), `pulsarLoadSave` (25), `objectXLab` (completing ID0's two menu
  names), `objectXGuide`, `objectXMeasure` and `objectXSession`. With them every Indonesian map but ID-HELP's `webHelp` and
  `helpViewer` is complete, and the pulsar panel (`createPulsarTranslator`, which takes only a complete map) reads Indonesian as a
  whole.
- **The verdict:** Indonesian has no article, so the verdict is the same sentence for every kind: «Objek Anda adalah bintang.»,
  «… galaksi.», «… kuasar (QSO).», «… pulsar.», «… asteroid.»; «Jawaban Anda benar: objek Anda adalah …» when the answer is right
  (`kindId` unused). The answer legend is «Objek saya adalah».
- **Hidden identity:** no Indonesian string names or describes a target, and every string reads the same for every target; the kind
  words appear only in `scope` and `spectrumHelp` (as in English), the five answer options and the verdict, which renders only inside
  `objectx-reveal`; «objek Anda adalah» appears only in the verdict. The walk's leak check matches ObjectX.HTL's remarks as whole
  words, case and all, so «Asteroid» starting a sentence before the reveal counted as a leak (the remark "Asteroid"): the three
  strings that began with it now begin otherwise («Saat daftar asteroid disusun, asteroid dari file orbit…», «Jumlah asteroid yang
  dapat diamati pada …», «Daftar asteroid yang dapat diamati saat ini»).
- **The receiver's Record Save dialog** (ID5) rendered 16 texts of the `pulsar` map in English; they are Indonesian now, and
  `TODO_ID6_PULSAR_KEYS` is gone from `tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts` (`PULSAR_SOURCE` stays for Hindi's `TODO_HI6`). The buttons the radio
  strings quote read as ID5 asked: `pulsar.save.open` «Simpan tangkapan sebagai .PLR (terverifikasi)…» and `pulsar.rawExport.open`
  «Unduh tangkapan sebagai .PLR…»; `pulsar.save.choose` is «Pilih file dan simpan…».
- **Kept as recorded:** pulsar names, file names (.PLR, .CSV, ObjX-42-01.FIT, ObjX_MPC.CMO.sav…), the stored «No Data», VIREO's file
  label `Table Number:` (written «label file (Table Number:)»), the Object X codes and ObjX labels, ObjectX.HTL's identifier and remark
  after the reveal, the asteroid designations and note codes, the recorded VizieR rows, FITS cards, hashes, UT and JD.

**How it was translated.** Four Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`), from the English with the reviewed French and Spanish as
