meaning context, under ID2–ID5's brief extended with this front's terms (pulse «denyut», dispersion measure «ukuran dispersi (DM)»,
dispersion delay «tunda dispersi», channel «saluran» as `originals.json`'s Multisaluran, pan «geser» and never the telescope's
«gerak», zoom in/out «Perbesar»/«Perkecil», blink comparison «perbandingan kedip», astrometric solution «solusi astrometri», plate
constants «konstanta pelat», near-Earth «dekat Bumi», reveal «Ungkap identitas»), the three fixed button wordings and the
hidden-identity rule: the catalog (134 keys), the `pulsar` map in two halves (225 and 218 strings) and the six small maps (written in
French's TypeScript shape). Then fix mode (a no-op) and the strict lint (identical rows, glosses with one ellipsis and one ▸, VIREO's
«Tidak Ada Pulsar di dalam Berkas!» with its English, «UT radio diperlukan», «memulihkan», sentence case where the English has it).
The implementer's read of every string against the English (three Opus readers, one per set of files, about 860 strings) changed
110 (12.8 %; 52 catalog and Object X, 45 `pulsar`, 13 saved-rows and load-save), most for one form:
- **One form each:** a time interval «selang waktu»/«selang» (VIREO's «Selang Waktu Antartitik Data:» and the Review column «Selang»;
  PanTimer's «interval» stays); not saved «belum disimpan» and marked as written «sudah ditulis»; form fields «kolom»; Record armed
  «Catat dalam keadaan siaga» and disarm «membatalkan siaga Catat» (ID5's forms); confirmed rows «yang dikonfirmasi»; a supplied file
  «yang Anda berikan» (ID5's; one reader wrote «sediakan»); Keep «Pertahankan» (Keep this spectrum, the kept spectrum), never Save's
  «Simpan»; "stored" next to a Save «disimpan di memori» or «dipertahankan»; a field of the sky «bidang» (the site's word, about 240
  strings; the brief's «medan» was nowhere else); pointing «pengarahan»; alignment «penjajaran»/«dijajarkan» (ID2); VIREO's Yes (save
  first) «Ya (simpan terlebih dahulu)» in all five strings that name it; the display-factor buttons «Turunkan»/«Naikkan».
- **Quoted controls:** the guide quotes the map's own labels («Simpan pengukuran yang tercatat (.CSV)», «Ekspor pengukuran yang
  tercatat (.csv)…», «“Gunakan file ini”», «“Muat rekaman (.PLR)”»); `step.period.formula` names the «kolom Periode pada tanggal
  tangkapan» (an invented label before, FR6's lesson); `step.point.help`'s "Note P, DM…" is «Tuliskan nilai…» (the batch's «Catat»
  is VIREO's Record).
- **Meaning:** hedges restored («tidak dipulihkan», not «tidak berhasil dipulihkan»; `data.reference.help`'s dropped "never"); the
  raw export's one receiver («penerima tersebut», not «keduanya»); `channel-erased` conditional («akan hilang»); the native writer's
  reserved-name message (the original program changes the interval); `append.cancelled` «Penambahan dibatalkan»; "kept only in this
  browser tab" «hanya dipertahankan di tab browser ini».
- Term choices kept from the batches: recording «rekaman», capture «tangkapan», Record «Catat», recorded «tercatat»; viewer
  «penampil»; Find «Temukan»; offset «offset byte»; buffer «buffer»; «Δ (SA)» for the astronomical unit (Indonesian «satuan
  astronomi»); the delay formula's subscripts «f_rendah»/«f_tinggi»; «Kedipkan»/«Hentikan kedipan» for the blink buttons.

**Shared files (add-only).** `identical.json` gains 49 rows (FR6's reasons in Indonesian terms: the seven empty statuses, SHA-256,
the gain field names, Zoom, Gain, Horizontal, Panel, File, Target, interval, #, the dash, CR LF and LF, OK, the placeholder-only
formats, the telescope apertures, the date placeholder, the list's band letter and note numbers, x, y, UT, «pulsar» and
«asteroid»); `lint-allowed.json` gains three (`pulsar.nativeSave.problem.reserved`'s straight quote and spaced : ?, the
`objectXLab.imageSkipped` sample, PT6's and FR6's reasons, and `pulsarSavedRows.savedRows.find.note`, VIREO's option name «Cocokkan
Huruf Besar/Kecil»). No glossary row was added or changed, and no existing entry of a shared file changed.

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/id/id.test.ts`: the seven maps in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`, the ID6 coverage check (`ID6_KEYS` and every
  en.json key), the fixer a no-op and the id lint.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` gains `e06` and `e08`; `E06_MAPS.id` and `E08_MAPS.id`. So `e06.spec.ts` and
  `e08.spec.ts` run every language check in Indonesian, including E08's identity check at every step before the reveal: the verdict
  pattern of `walks/e08.ts` gains «Objek Anda adalah» and «Jawaban Anda benar», `VERDICTS.id` pins the five verdicts, and
  `SAME_IN.id` keeps «pulsar», «asteroid» and the camera's Target, Filter, Gain (e⁻/ADU) and File. `walks/e06.ts`: `SAME_IN.id` (Zoom,
  Horizontal, Gain, Panel, File, OK, Edit…) and `PANEL_IN.id` («Panel {n}», as Spanish).
- `tests/i18n/e06.spec.ts`: the originals test skips a VIREO caption the language keeps by an `original:` row of its
  `identical.json` (Indonesian's «Edit...», which Windows Indonesian writes the same); no other language's result changes, since no
  other language keeps one of those captions.
- `tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts`: `TODO_ID6_PULSAR_KEYS`, its matcher and its clause are removed; the Record Save step passes in
  Indonesian with no exemption.
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the ES8b family (E06) and the ES9 family (E08) also run in id, with the route's Indonesian
  `h1` checked; the E08 investigations before and after the reveal are byte-identical to English's (the one documented mask).

**The dist check** (`scripts/check-i18n-dist.mjs`). Indonesian's `pulsar` and `pulsar+radio` modules, maps alone, are found by ID0's
ASCII fallback (their prose literals). `pulsar.nativeSave.problem.reserved` lists the backslash, written `\\` in the source: the
fallback read the escape as two characters and never found it in the chunk, which the bundler writes otherwise, so the module failed
with 340 markers of which 339 were present. The fallback now leaves out a literal holding a backslash (a fixture test in
`src/i18n/distCheck.test.ts`); it applies only to fallback modules, so every es, pt-BR, fr, hi, ja, zh-Hans and ru result is unchanged.
A doc comment's straight apostrophes («receiver's … CLEA's») also made a false literal; the `pulsar` map's comment writes ’.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). id: 13 chunks, 217.2 KB gzip in all; core 93.5 KB gzip (299.9 KB raw: the
`pulsarLab` and `plr` keys and the Object X maps live there, as for fr and pt-BR), pulsar+radio 13.3 KB (the `pulsar` map with
`radioR1`), pulsar 3.6 KB (the saved-rows and load-save maps), fits 2.5 KB. Budgets: core 120/150, a module 60/90, the sum 300/360 KB.

**Verification** (2026-11-20, worktree `claude/i18n-id-id6` with main eb7093cf merged). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 6,419 tests in 484 files;
`npm run test:resources`, `npm run build` with every check, `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright (Chromium, DejaVu fontconfig, two
workers): the Indonesian `e06` and `e08` tests 12 (the first runs failed on Indonesian's identical words missing from the walks'
`SAME_IN.id`, «Edit...» expected to change, and a sentence-initial «Asteroid» before the reveal, all fixed); `radio` in every language
and `areas` 57; the exports-identity `[id]` variants of the E06 and E08 families 2.

**Independent review (ID6).** Two Opus readers read every string this front adds against the English: the 137 catalog keys and the six
small maps, and the 443 strings of `pulsar`. The Indonesian holds: «Anda» throughout, base-form imperatives, VIREO's names as
`originals.json`, ID5's radio terms, every quoted button equal to its label (the radio strings' two .PLR buttons included), every hedge
and placeholder kept, and the hidden identity (no string names or implies a target before the reveal; «objek Anda adalah» only in the
verdict). Both said "merge after fixes"; fixed:
- *Meaning* (low): `pulsar.guide.noData` «Belum ada yang dapat diukur» (it said "nothing has been measured yet"); `pulsar.outcome.activated`
  given its verb («kini ditampilkan bersama properti datanya»); `pulsar.guide.sameChannel` «denyut lain yang datang kemudian» (a later
  pulse, not the next); `pulsar.reject.byte` reordered so «yang didukung» governs the sentence; the reference stars «hampir segaris»
  (nearly collinear) in `objectXMeasure`; `fits.fit` «Paskan citra ke tampilan» (fit to the view, not adjust); `fits.axes` «memindahkan
  pilihan» (the arrow keys move the selection; «geser» is pan); `pulsarLab.recover.tick` «agar pulih kembali»; `objectXLab.spectrumAdaptation`
  reordered so "offered" governs the ranges.
- *One form* (low): a stored field of a row «kolom» (not the sky's «bidang»); «selang» for the sample interval and the blink interval;
  «yang Anda berikan» in two more catalog strings; «ditulis» (not «tertulis») for a file written; pending readings «belum dicatat»; the
  native writer «penulis file»; VIREO's file label «label file (Table Number:)» in `nativeSave.note`, quoted «“Table Number: ”» where the
  saved-rows viewer quotes the line.
- *Quoted controls* (low): the saved-rows viewer's read-cancelled notes quote «“Gunakan file ini”»; `pulsarLab.recover.catalog` quotes
  «“Coba lagi katalog yang disertakan”».
Kept: «disimpan di tab ini» in `pulsar.rawExport.problem.not-memory`, the same wording as ID5's `radioReceiver.record.scope`; the gloss
«(Set Coordinates)» after «Gerakkan ▸ Atur Koordinat…» (a second ▸ fails the glyph rule).

## Indonesian: help (ID-HELP)

The Indonesian help front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §4.6 and §6.2 "ID6 ∥ ID-HELP" in the research root, with
FR-HELP and PT-HELP as its models. Indonesian stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=id` reaches it. VIREO's 38 bundled help
files and OpenVireo's own help are translated as web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`,
their bytes, SHA-256 and provenance are unchanged, and «Lihat aslinya (bahasa Inggris)» shows VIREO's exact text.

**What is translated.**
- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/id/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'id',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`) in VIREO's own file format,
  as ES-HELP describes it: LF, every TXT line at 72 columns or fewer, titles at 65 or fewer, the same LST rows, kinds and file tokens
  (the unterminated `'Klasifikasi spektrum' 'begin` and VarTypeHlp `'begin` rows reproduced; HUBHLP and LSSHLP keep their blank lines
  and stay refused; MLTSPHLP and SPLSTHLP stay empty; OBJXHLP and TOOLSHLP, byte-identical originals, have identical translations),
  each section header the row title it opens, SPECLNS.LNL equal to the original and SPECLNS.TXT's line headers VIREO's. Where VIREO's
  English names a topic that does not exist (MEASHLP's «Individual Spectral Features» and «Measurem Individual Spectral Lines»), the
  Indonesian names one that does not exist either («Ciri spektrum satu per satu», «Ukur garis spektrum satu per satu»), so the
  related-topic links resolve to the same rows as VIREO's. About 18,600 English words, 16,600 Indonesian.
- **Characters** (plan §4.6): the Indonesian help set is ASCII plus U+202F, which the screen's thousands use («2 400 000»);
  `LATIN1_HELP.id` is `' '`, read as U+00A0 in the check that the text parsers read a Latin-1 set as the byte parsers read its
  bytes (Portuguese's mapping). Quotes are VIREO's straight `"`, an ellipsis is `...`; a test refuses any other character.
  `foldHelpText` needs nothing new (U+202F already reads as a space).
- **Markers:** the related-topics marker «Topik terkait:» (`help:{related:'TOPIK TERKAIT'}` in `src/i18n/languages.json`); VIREO's
  "See also:" reads «Lihat juga:»; VIREO's NOTE is «CATATAN:» followed by a capital, as a sentence after a label (Microsoft's
  «Catatan:»), and reads as a remark everywhere; CAUTION and WARNING are «PERHATIAN:» and «PERINGATAN:».
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («Terjemahan: adaptasi web OpenVireo (bukan teks CLEA)» · «Lihat aslinya (bahasa Inggris)» ·
  «Lihat terjemahan», the fold note, the Spectral Line Table window) and `webHelp` (Bantuan › Topik bantuan…, with the plan's
  sentence on numbers in the second paragraph of «Apa itu OpenVireo»: «Di layar, angka memakai titik desimal dan spasi tipis di
  antara ribuan (misalnya 1.5 dan 24 000); ketik juga desimal dengan titik. File yang disimpan situs tetap dalam bahasa Inggris,
  seperti yang ditulis VIREO.»). Its VIREO-name column names VIREO's menus in Indonesian with no English gloss, as French's does. One
  `identical.json` row: `map:helpViewer.spectralLines.ok` (OK).

**How it was translated.** Seven Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`), from the English with the reviewed Portuguese and French help
as meaning references and models of the format, under one brief (ID0–ID6's register and terms, the format rules above, VIREO names
from `originals.json` in straight quotes with VIREO's English in parentheses at the first mention in a topic, the optical and radio
slew buttons «"U"», «"S"», «"T"», «"B"»): VIRMNHLP with the two reminders; PULSRHLP with PLSLSHLP and PULSRLST; PLSANHLP, PULSRANL,
SPECHLP and SPECLNS; PHOTOHLP, OBSRVHLP and CAMHLP (coupled through ObsrvHlp.txt); CLASSHLP and MEASHLP; HRAnalHlp, VarTypeHlp,
WdgPltHlp and the eight index-only files; and the two maps. The implementer's read of every line against the English (three Opus
readers, one per set of files) changed 156 places in 30 modules and the two maps, most for one form:
- **One name per thing:** the index titles shared by VIRMNHLP, OBXMNHLP, HUBHLP, LSSHLP and the instrument sets read the same in
  sentence case («Memulai», «Masuk», «Pilih teleskop», «Ganti teleskop optik», «Minta waktu teleskop», «Akhiri praktikum», «Akses
  keyboard», «Editor Hasil», VIREO's own name, «Muat data tersimpan»); VIREO's Log In is «Masuk», as ID0's helpMenu map; Start/Resume
  Count and Stop Count «Mulai/lanjutkan pencacahan» and «Hentikan pencacahan» (the glossary's «cacah»; SPECHLP had «penghitungan»,
  which is close to «perhitungan», computation); VIREO's tools «alat pengukur spektrum», «Alat analisis pulsar radio», «Alat diagram
  kerucut LSS», «Pengolahan Citra (DS9)»; HR diagram «diagram HR» (as ID4; HRAnalHlp had «H-R»); the variable-star types «Tipe …»
  (the `variableStars` map); Re-center «Pusatkan ulang penggeser» (`spectrum.recenter`); topic titles in the base form for English
  imperatives (PULSRHLP and PLSLSHLP had me- verbs); VIREO's "press" «tekan» and "click" «klik»; the software «Observatorium
  Pendidikan Virtual»; the exercises «Pencarian Objek X» (The Quest for Object X) and «Struktur Skala Besar Alam Semesta».
- **Names of the web screens:** the pulsar analysis tool's menus follow the `pulsar` map of ID6 («Hasil» (Results) › «Catat
  pengukuran saat ini...», «Tinjau pengukuran yang tercatat...», «Simpan pengukuran yang tercatat...», «Kosongkan daftar pengukuran»,
  «Langkah Geser»), keeping VIREO's own `...` and capitals as FR-HELP did; webHelp's labels equal the catalog's, the maps' and the
  originals' (looked up one by one, including ID6's «Atur jam observatorium»).
- **Meaning:** dropped "if you like" restored twice; OBSRVHLP's instrument area «diberi garis tepi merah» (was "underlined");
  PHOTOHLP «faktor 10» (was "multiples of 10"); HRAnalHlp «dengan langkah 0.05» and «seharusnya»; SPECLNS «Pita» for the G band.
- **Format:** VIREO's two spaces after a full stop where the original has them, paragraphs rewrapped at 72 after edits.
- Kept from the batches: «Bidang Pandang» for VIREO's View slider (the web's `sky.view` «Bidang pandang»), «BELUM ADA LANGIT!»
  (NO SKY!), «Jelajahi...» (Browse...), «login» as a noun in prose («informasi login»), «Detik Horizontal», «Gain Vertikal» and
  «Kenaikan Frekuensi» in VIREO's capitals, «keypad numerik».

**Tests.**
- `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts`: the structure checks run for Indonesian (registry `help`), with its marker and `LATIN1_HELP.id`;
  a new block runs the id profile over each paragraph of every Indonesian help text: its typography patterns, the TXT files' view of
  the fixer given the English original (less its straight-quote change, which the help keeps), the «Anda» register's patterns and the
  glossary's `forbidden` words and patterns, plus no character other than ASCII and U+202F. No allowance is needed.
- `src/i18n/id/id.test.ts`: the registry's `help`, `helpViewer` and `webHelp` in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`, the lint over the
  two maps.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT.done` gains `help` and `HELP_TEXT.id` its rows (Indonesian has no accents, so the
  search test types «POSISI VERTIKAL» and finds «posisi vertikal», a case check). `help.spec.ts` runs its eleven tests in Indonesian.
  `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=id`.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). id: 38 help chunks, 135.7 KB raw, 49.4 KB gzip in all, none loaded or preloaded by
`index.html` (largest VIRMNHLP.TXT 20.1 KB raw / 6.3 KB gzip, PULSRHLP.TXT 18.7 / 6.1); the core chunk 89.2 KB gzip (288.3 KB raw, the
`webHelp` and `helpViewer` maps), 13 chunks 195.7 KB gzip in all (on this branch, without ID6). The help viewer chunk gains the 38
Indonesian loader entries of the shared `import.meta.glob` table, as for es, pt-BR and fr.

**Verification** (2026-11-20, worktree `claude/i18n-id-help` with main 5fcf960a merged). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 6,505 tests in 484 files;
`npm run test:resources`, `npm run build` with every check, `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright (Chromium, DejaVu fontconfig, two
workers): `i18n/help`, `i18n/shell`, `i18n/areas`, `clea-help`, `cluster-hr-help`, `help-unicode` and `page-bar` 195 of 196 before the
review fixes (the one failure, a Spanish areas test that saw the core chunk fetched twice under load, passed in the rerun), then
`i18n/help`, `i18n/areas`, `help-unicode` and `clea-help` 88 after them; `npm run test:pages` help-chunk smokes 5, Indonesian included.

**Shared entries changed.** ID4's `clusterHr.linkNote` (the note beside the cluster HR help) now says «Klik ganda atau tekan Enter pada
judul di bawah “Topik terkait” (Related Topics)», as the help viewer and the help files name them (it said «Klik dua kali» and
«“Topik Terkait”»). No other existing entry of a shared file changed; `identical.json` gains one row.

**Independent review (ID-HELP).** Three Opus readers read all 38 modules and the two maps against the English, every quoted name checked
against `originals.json`, `id.json`, the maps and ID6's worktree; no wrong meaning a reader would follow, no link that resolves
differently from VIREO's, and the format and the character rule hold. Each said "merge after fixes"; fixed:
- *VIREO's English at a first mention* (low): VIRMNHLP's «"Pencarian Objek X" (The Quest for Object X)» in «Atur tanggal & waktu»,
  «Observatorium Virtual (Virtual Observatory)», «"Teleskop" (Telescope)->"Optik" (Optical)» in two topics; PHOTOHLP's Reading, Sky,
  Start, Object, Telescope, Mean Sky Counts, NO SKY!, Record/Review and the Results Editors path, as CLASSHLP and MEASHLP have them.
- *One form* (low): «medan» was left in PULSRHLP for the telescope field (now «bidang», the site's word); «tidak disimpan» for "not
  saved" in PHOTOHLP, CLASSHLP and MEASHLP (now «belum disimpan», ID6's form); OBSRVHLP's Tracking said «menyalakan/mati» (now
  «mengaktifkan», «aktif», «nonaktif», ID5's); «penunjuk mouse» in OBSRVHLP (now «kursor», as `originals.json` and the other sets);
  «login» for VIREO's Log In in PULSRHLP and PULSRANL (now «isian kotak dialog Masuk (Log In)»); CLASSHLP's display options now
  E03's «Foto Skala Abu-abu» and «Balik Skala Abu-abu Fotografis».
- *Names* (low): PULSRANL's Results menu items in VIREO's capitals («Catat Pengukuran Saat Ini...», «Tinjau Pengukuran yang
  Tercatat...», «Simpan Pengukuran yang Tercatat...», «Kosongkan Daftar Pengukuran»), as the file's other VIREO names; «Waktu Awal
  Data» (the web's Properti data dialog); MEASHLP's link to «Simpan spektrum untuk analisis» in SPECHLP's case; PULSRHLP's
  cross-reference «Hapus objek dari "Daftar Objek"» equal to its title.
- *Meaning* (low): MEASHLP «Anda mungkin merasa mengukur garis satu per satu lebih akurat» (it said the reader feels more
  accurate); PHOTOHLP «menghilangkan kontribusinya» (it said "subtract them", against the same point's warning); HRAnalHlp «kurva
  itu» (not «keduanya»); WdgPltHlp «anggota lain dalam kelompok Anda»; CAMHLP «ada petunjuk lain» (not «diarahkan», which reads as
  pointing); PHOTOHLP «pindahkan bintang» (not the pan word «geser»); PULSRHLP «salinan "permanen"», «disimpan di memori (RAM)»,
  «tidak dapat bergerak»; SPECLNS «Rasio kekuatan He II 4541/He I 4471»; VIRMNHLP «pilihan ini» for a menu item (not «entri») and
  «garis titik-titik»; webHelp «identifikasi objek tak dikenal»; helpViewer «Asli: teks bahasa Inggris VIREO apa adanya».
Kept: «penggerakan» for the slew noun in PULSRHLP (also the sound and polish maps' word); «interval pembaruan» in WdgPltHlp (VIREO's own
«Interval» in `originals.json`); no gloss after «Bantuan» in related-topic lines (VIRMNHLP has none).

**Left for later fronts.** ID6 (E06 and E08) ran in parallel and had not merged when this front was handed back; the names that depend
on it, for the coordinator to recheck at merge: PULSRANL's and PLSANHLP's pulsar analysis menus and controls (above), webHelp's «Atur
jam observatorium», «Simpan pengukuran yang tercatat (.CSV)», «Panduan Objek X» and «Mulai praktikum pulsar»; ID6's worktree used
these forms when this front was handed back. ID-REL: `released:true`, after which Indonesian help follows the browser language.

## Russian: plural families, the folder, lints and shell (RU0)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md in the research root. User decisions of 2026-11-19 (AGENTS.md): thousands with U+202F on
screen («24 000», the decimal point kept), « » with „ “ inside, and the radio hold keys following the physical key (RU5). Russian is
registered before its release (`released:false`, `budgetFactor` 1.2): only `?lang=ru` reaches it, and only the shell is translated.

**Catalog plural families (generic, plan §1.2 #1).** Russian needs four forms («1 запись / 3 записи / 5 записей / 1.5 записи»)
where en.json has `.one` and `.other`. A catalog may now hold a key en.json lacks when it is one more form of an en.json family:
- `src/i18n/plural.ts` `pluralFormBase(key, en, language)`: every segment naming a CLDR category (zero, one, two, few, many) is
  replaced by `other`; the result must be an en.json key (the base, whose {params} the form keeps), en.json must hold the `one`
  sibling at each such segment (so the segment is a plural axis, not a word), and each category must be one the language selects.
  Two-level families work by segment: `obsHr.kindWarning.few.many` has the base `obsHr.kindWarning.other.other`.
- `createI18n` accepts such keys (and refuses any other unknown key, a category the language never selects, or other {params});
  a strict catalog still needs every en.json key.
- `pluralKey` (src/i18n/index.ts) returns the chosen category's key when the active messages hold it (`pluralFormKey`: the
  segment where the `one` and `other` keys differ, renamed), otherwise the `other` key as before. English, Spanish, Portuguese and
  French have no such key, so they choose exactly as `plural()` did; their chunks are unchanged.
- `scripts/i18n-split.mjs` keeps a copy of the rule (`pluralFormBase`, kept equal by src/i18n/pluralFamilies.test.ts) and puts a
  form in its base key's module; a form without its base, or a key of no family, is a plan problem.
- `scripts/i18n-batch.mjs extract` emits the missing forms of an area's families as `kind:'plural-form'` items (with the category
  to write); `merge` accepts them and orders each after its base. `scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs` counts them when a language has
  categories beyond one/many/other.
- `pluralFamilyKeys(head, en, language)` lists every key a language needs for a family (20 for `obsHr.kindWarning` in Russian), and
  the `pluralForms` lint takes `*` for the category segment of a two-level axis. src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts requires all four forms of
  any family the catalog starts (RU1 brings `sky.searchMatches`, RU4 the `obsHr` families).

**Count-neutral wordings (plan §1.2 #2).** In the 86 `countedMessages` of src/i18n/plural-sites.json that are not plural families,
`countBeforeNoun` (src/i18n/lint/russianTypography.ts) flags a Cyrillic word right after `{count}`, `{n}` or `{total}` unless it is
a preposition or conjunction («{n} из {total}»), a unit symbol, or a neuter short participle («отмечено»). Write «Записей: {count}».

**Russian typography fixer (plan §1.2 #3).** src/i18n/lint/russianTypography.ts, pure and idempotent like French's: thousands with
U+202F; straight and top-level English quotes into « », quotes inside « » into „ “ (only when the text's quotes balance); a spaced
hyphen, en dash or em dash between words, or an em dash glued between letters, into U+00A0 + «— »; U+00A0 inside «т. е.» and
between a number or placeholder and a unit symbol. It keeps placeholders, the lint's «A1» samples, URLs, `?lang=` and ASCII-only
bracketed glosses. Fix mode: `env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx vitest run src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts` (refused under CI); the
batches write ordinary spaces and the fixer writes the characters literally. The `ru` profile reports whatever it would still change.

**Profile additions (plan §4.4).** `thousandsRules(' ')`; U+202F only between digits; Ukrainian and Belarusian letters (і ї є ґ
ў) and words; a longer ё list (stems such as приемник*, звездн*, and the short forms определен, сохранен…, never «все» or «звезды»,
which need context); title case (three capitalised Cyrillic words); Latin unit symbols outside a bracketed gloss; the en and em
dashes counted as one glyph class (the fixer turns a spaced en dash into « — »). The length band stays 0.8–1.8: on the shell the
ratio's median is 1.12, its 5th percentile 0.84 and its 95th 1.50.

**Folder and shell.** src/i18n/ru/: `ru.json` (the 131 shell keys, HI0's digit notice `app.digitNotice` among them, and the 27 `home.*`
keys), the shell maps (appShell, helpMenu,
languageChoice, projectLinks, tour) complete and objectXLab, polish and variableStars partial (menu names), the 10 shell originals,
`glossary.json` seeded from the French glossary (156 terms, 209 names, 8 forbidden rows, retargeted from the English), `identical.json`
and `lint-allowed.json`. Tests: `RUSSIAN_TEXT` (`done:{shell}`, letters Cyrillic, « » and U+202F), the shell spec, the partial-language
case of areas.spec.ts, the selector on `?lang=ru` («Русский (Russian)», five options), and the toolbar Save investigation export.

Decisions taken in RU0's translation: guides are «Руководство: <тема>» (lab.nav and pleiadesLab.nav carry a glossary allowance for
"lab"); VIREO is feminine («VIREO открывала», «программа»); Restore investigation JSON is «Восстановить
исследование (JSON)»; Preferences is «Настройки» (Options stays «Параметры»); Optical Simulation Options is «Параметры оптического
моделирования», quoted in the nominative where prose would inflect it; session «сеанс», backup «резервная копия», counts (detector)
«отсчёты»; glossary names that prose inflects are stems (Демонстрационн, виртуальн, правки).

**Size.** The same shell text gzips to 1.18× French (the plan's proxy measured 1.177×), so `budgetFactor` 1.2 holds; French's core
(112.5 KB at release) projects to about 132 KB for Russian, under the 144 KB warning.

**Corrections.** Sonnet batches (shell catalog, shell maps and originals, glossary); the implementer corrected 11 of 157 catalog
strings and 6 map strings (ё, a wrong gender for VIREO, quoted VIREO names in the nominative, the Restore name across catalog and
maps, an awkward «счёты», a gloss order), and 6 glossary names inflected in prose. The independent review (no blocker) added:
VIREO's Topics... is «Разделы...» beside the web «Разделы справки…» (they were one name); every guide in the menu reads
«Руководство: <тема>»; demonstration mode in lower case in prose; «предпросмотр обсерватории» (not «предварительной»); two
rephrasings; the batch extract shows each plural form the closest English (`one.few` the `one.other` sentence); «та» and «заполнен»
are Russian (dropped from the leakage and ё lists); the fixer leaves ordinary spaces between digits to the lint (two numbers may
stand side by side) and a hyphen between two placeholders (a subtraction); the count rule's participle exemption is narrower.
## Japanese: the folder, lints and shell (JA0)

The first front of Japanese (`ja`), plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md` §1–§6 "JA0" in the research root, built on the script
groundwork (SC1–SC12) and following FR0. Decisions (AGENTS.md): です/ます (2026-11-09), system fonts only, full-width digits refused
with the shared notice, print dates in the screen language, the decimal point kept, files unchanged; the coordinator settled on
2026-11-19 the katakana long vowel (ブラウザー) and that the full-width notice shows in every language (Hindi's HI0 builds it; Japanese
translates its key, `app.digitNotice`, since HI0 merged into this front). Japanese stays hidden (decision 5 of 2026-11-09): only a `?lang=ja` preview reaches it until
JA-REL. Its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**The registry line.** `{"tag":"ja","dir":"ja","catalog":"ja.json","budgetFactor":1.4,"htmlLang":"ja","pagePath":"ja","autonym":"日本語",
"englishName":"Japanese","match":["ja"],"released":false,"notices":{…}}`. No `thousands`: Japanese writes «24,000» and «1.5» natively,
which is what the site prints, so no visible adaptation exists. No `help` until JA-HELP. Once released, `ja`, `ja-JP`, `JA-jp` and
`ja-JP-u-ca-japanese` open it; `jv` (Javanese), `ryu` (Okinawan) and `en-JP` do not; `?lang=JA` and `?lang=ja-JP` are no tag. The
selector shows «日本語 (Japanese)» (a non-Latin autonym carries its English name). `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` takes this line
instead of its stand-in. `budgetFactor` 1.4 is provisional: on the shell's 157 keys Japanese is 1.08× French in raw UTF-8 bytes but 0.98× in gzip (the
budgets are gzip), and the JA0 core is 9.6 KB gzip (29.5 KB raw). The margin is kept for the Unicode help set, which compresses
less well than Latin-1; JA1 confirms the factor with the largest partial core and JA-HELP with the help chunks.

**The folder** `src/i18n/ja/`, mirroring FR0's: `index.ts` (`JAPANESE`, the manifest the planner parses), `ja.json`, `originals.json`,
`maps/`, `glossary.json`, `identical.json`, `lint-allowed.json` and `ja.test.ts`.
- **Translated:** the shell slice (FR0's 130 keys and HI0's `app.digitNotice`, «数字は半角の0–9で入力してください。{digits}のような
  数字は、ここでは数値として読み取られません。») and the 27 `home.*` keys of the welcome page (Front HOME came after FR0): 158 keys; the complete maps `appShell`, `helpMenu`, `languageChoice` (legend «言語 / Language»),
  `projectLinks` and `tour`; the partial maps `objectXLab` (`nav`, `guideNav`), `polish` (`clusterGuide.nav`, `results.help`) and
  `variableStars` (`nav`); the 10 VIREO captions the shell shows (Help «ヘルプ», Topics... «トピック...», Websites «ウェブサイト»,
  2MASS Catalog «2MASSカタログ», About This Exercise... «この実習について...», Results Editors «結果エディター», Observational Results
  «観測結果», Pulsar Results «パルサー結果»; Project CLEA and NVO kept).
- **Style** (plan §3): です/ます in sentences; buttons, menu items and headings a noun or dictionary form with no 。 («調査を保存»,
  «閉じる»); no second-person pronoun; kana for auxiliaries and formal nouns; the katakana long vowel; a lab is «実習», the tour
  «ガイドツアー», an investigation «調査», instructor mode «講師モード», Demonstration Mode «デモンストレーションモード» with VIREO's
  English in full-width brackets at the first mention; the phone menu button «メニュー»; the `?` key «「?」キー».
- **How it was translated:** three Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`, from the English, French and Spanish shown only as meaning
  context, one brief with the plan's §3–§5): the 157 keys (`scripts/i18n-batch.mjs merge --lang ja`), the eight maps with the 10
  originals, and the glossary seed. The Opus implementer read every row against the English and changed: `helpMenu.exitNote` and
  `radioOff` (VIREO's captions glossed in brackets or given as their Japanese originals instead of bare English with spaces),
  `polish.results.help` (one ›, as the English), the tour's «「?」キー» (a bare `?` after kana is punctuation to the fixer), and
  `project.sessionHelp` («「セッションを保存」には», the button's own name).
- **Glossary** (`glossary.json`, `ja` field, `glossaryFrom(…,'ja')`): the French glossary's terms and names re-targeted to Japanese
  from the English (substring matching: `boundary:'none'`), with the review's additions: 169 terms and 215 names, 9 `forbidden` rows
  (レッドシフト, クラスター for a star cluster, 視等級, 消光, 発光線 and 放射線, スペクトロメーター, フォトメーター, ケフェイド, あなた; the
  long vowel is the profile's own rule). The plan's §5.2 had «ケフェイド変光星»; the review found that the IAU OAE Japanese glossary
  gives «セファイド» (astro4edu.org term 54, linked to astro-dic.jp), so Cepheid is «セファイド» and ケフェイド is the forbidden second
  form. The review added the decided terms that had no row (青方偏移, 時角, 恒星時, ユリウス日, 視差, パーセク, 光年, ドップラー,
  視線速度, 後退速度 for both "recession" and "recessional", 巨星分枝, 星間減光, ノイズ, スペクトル分類, セファイド) and names (Object X
  «天体X», Show Spectrum «スペクトルを表示», Observer's Notebook «観測ノート», Preferences «環境設定», exact Delete «削除», Edit «編集»,
  Open «開く»), narrowed exercise to «実習» and tracking to «追尾», and set Radio clock scenario «電波の時計のシナリオ», Horizon Limit
  «地平線の制限», Possible Cluster Member «星団メンバー候補». The implementer set
  Topics... «トピック...» (with French's `except` for VIREO's Show Help Topics...), Help topics… «ヘルプトピック…», Tools button
  «「メニュー」ボタン» (as French), Record/Review «記録／見直し» (確認 is Confirmation), Radio configuration «電波の設定» and radio clock «電波の時計» (電波時計 is a
  radio-controlled clock in Japanese). Loose rows the later fronts tighten as their areas arrive (the batch's own list): stars
  («恒星|星»), count (counters 個/件/枚), shoulder, grayscale, remark, phone, reveal, trace, readout, journal, preset, fallback,
  audition, analyzer, merge, press.

**The CJK punctuation fixer** (`src/i18n/lint/cjkPunctuation.ts`, pure, idempotent; `cjkPunctuation.test.ts`). Written for every
CJK language: a style names the letters, the comma and the quotes (`JAPANESE_STYLE` 、 「」『』; `SIMPLIFIED_CHINESE_STYLE` ， “ ”‘ ’;
`TRADITIONAL_CHINESE_STYLE` ， 「」『』), so Chinese reuses it. In order: straight "…" pairs (and, where the quotes are 「」, top-level
“…”) into the style's quotes, the inner pair inside an open quote; an ASCII ( ) pair into （ ） when either bracket touches CJK text
(spaces between ignored); ASCII , . : ; ? ! right after a CJK letter, a closing full-width bracket or quote, or ？ ！ into the
full-width mark (also across spaces when the mark ends the text or a space or closer follows: «テスト , 次» → «テスト、次»); spaces
next to a full-width mark or bracket, and between a CJK letter and a Latin letter, a digit, a {placeholder}, a template value or a
URL, removed (`text-autospace` gives the visual gap). Passes repeat until nothing changes, so the result is idempotent (a removed
space can put a mark right after a bracket). Never: VIREO's `...`, a `.` before a Latin letter, digit or `{` («.SSP», «.{ext}»), `:`
before `/`, `?lang=` values (ASCII), URLs (which end at a space, a CJK character or a full-width mark), {placeholders}, line breaks,
text between two Latin characters («12 km/s»), a `?` key name («テスト ?キー»; the strings write «「?」キー»). Through FR0's
`fixSourceLiterals` it rewrites only string literals and writes characters, never escapes.
- **Fix mode:** `env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx vitest run src/i18n/ja/ja.test.ts` rewrites `ja.json`, `originals.json`,
  `maps/*.ts` and `help/*.ts` (refused under `CI`); run it once more without the variable to check. Each JA front: batches → fix
  mode → strict lint → the implementer's read → the independent review.

**The `ja` lint profile additions** (`src/i18n/lint/japanese.ts`, wired in `profiles.ts`; fixtures in `fixtures.ts`):
- `typographyCheck`: what the fixer would still change, and each Han character outside JIS X 0208 and its Windows-31J extensions —
  the set is built on first use by decoding every double-byte Shift_JIS (Windows-31J) code with the runtime's ICU
  `TextDecoder('shift_jis')` (6,718 characters: 6,355 of JIS X 0208, 360 NEC/IBM extensions, 々〆〇; nothing downloaded or bundled),
  so a Chinese form left by a model (图, 设, 显, 时, 间, 选, 键, 观, 测, 谱) fails. The few 常用 2010 forms outside that set (剝, 頰, 塡,
  𠮟) fail too; `lint-allowed.json` may accept one character in one string with a reason.
- Kana-preferred words (公用文 usage): 下さい, 出来る, て頂く, 致す, 為に, 事が (no kanji before it: 記事 passes), 様に, とき after kana,
  又は, 及び, 但し, 是非, 宜しく, 予め, 殆ど, 何故, 有る, 無い, て居る, 迄, 貰う, 解る/判る (物 and 所 are left out: 生き物, 読み物
  and 入れ物 are correct).
- The katakana long vowel of 38 -er/-or/-ar loans (ブラウザ, フォルダ, フィルタ, パラメータ, コンピュータ, ユーザ…) where the word ends
  (no katakana or ー follows: モニタリング is another word).
- Over-polite forms in the register rule (させていただく, いただけますでしょうか, でございます, して参ります, 〜ております。).
- The length band is [0.15, 1.1] of the English in graphemes, calibrated on the shell (natural Japanese ran 0.19–1.08).

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/ja/ja.test.ts`: the registry line, the 158 keys, map shapes, originals Spanish knows, the fixer a no-op with no `\uXXXX`
  escape in a map, numbers as English (no `thousands`), one plural category, the Shift_JIS set, and the whole ja lint.
- Shared rows (add-only): `languages.test.ts` (registry, notices, the detection TABLE's ja-JP, JA-jp, ja-JP-u-ca-japanese, jv, ryu,
  en-JP), `locale.test.ts` (before and after the release), `direction.test.ts`, `pseudoScripts.test.ts` (ja is no junk tag any more),
  `pageBar.test.ts` (Japanese «その他» starts «その他の調査操作» with no space), `tests/fixtures/language.ts` (`JAPANESE_LOCALE`
  ja-JP), `tests/i18n/languageText.ts` (`JAPANESE_TEXT`: letters kana, ー and 「」, which no English text, VIREO file or export has;
  `done:{shell}`).
- Playwright: `shell.spec.ts` runs for Japanese through `translatedFor('shell')`; `areas.spec.ts` brings back FR0's partial-language
  test for Japanese (core only, no chunk for an empty area); `language-selector.spec.ts` (only `?lang=ja` opens and offers it; the
  keyboard across five options); `exports-identity.spec.ts` (the toolbar's Save investigation byte-identical in `ja`, no kana in the
  file); `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts` gains a real-translation walk (home, observatory, header menu, tour, instructor-mode
  confirmation at 320 and 1440 px with `layoutProblems()` and `verticalInkProblems()`) for every script language in
  `translatedFor('shell')`, under the pinned CJK fonts; `tests/digit-notice.spec.ts` gains `ja` in its language loop and a
  Japanese case: full-width «１２．５» typed on the preview brings the Japanese notice and the field keeps «１２．５» unconverted. Locally the Japanese specs run with the scripts fontconfig
  (`FONTCONFIG_FILE=~/.cache/openvireo-test-fonts/scripts/fonts.conf`): the verify fontconfig holds DejaVu only and draws Japanese
  as empty boxes.

**Independent review** (Opus, high effort). Findings fixed: the fixer was not idempotent («テスト (abc) .» needed two runs), turned a
curly pair inside 「」 into «『b」», and let a URL swallow the Japanese after it (so the fixer and the lint disagreed); Cepheid
«セファイド» (IAU OAE) instead of the plan's ケフェイド; a name row («電波時計シナリオ») contradicting its term row («電波の時計»); a
forbidden long-vowel row without the profile's end-of-word rule (フィルタリング failed); Preferences «環境設定» so that File ›
Preferences no longer collides with Radio configuration «電波の設定»; the decided terms and names that had no row; exercise and
tracking narrowed to one form; two duplicate rows; the 物 and 所 kana rules dropped for their false positives (生き物); 〜ております。
added; the length band calibrated; and translations: `home.title` keeps "rebuilt" («ブラウザー向けに再構築»), `home.factFaithful`
(behaviour recovered *from* the original; each web change marked), the tour's «1分もかかりません。» and save-step heading
«作業はファイルとして保存», «OpenVireoの概要とプライバシー», «観測のHR図» and «.PLRファイル» in `project.toolbarHelp`, «周期と分散量度»,
«スペクトル解析ツール», «まずは空から», the Run Exercise gloss at its first mention. `language-selector.spec.ts` also checks that a
ja browser and a stored ja fetch nothing of Japanese. The review could not read astro-dic.jp (403): the other §5.2 rows (分散量度,
転向点, 等時曲線, 輝線, 光度階級) rest on the IAU OAE where it has them and on current usage; JA1 and JA4 check them when their areas
arrive.
## Hindi: the observatory and VIREO's names (HI1)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-hi.md §12 in the research root, with FR1 as its model. Hindi stays behind `?lang=hi`.

- **Catalog:** FR1's 552 observatory keys (the 20 instrument prefixes, `hubbleHtl.reason.*` and the 104 guide keys), about 8,100
  English words; `hi.json` holds 713 keys. **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`,
  `sound`, `observationMetadata`, `camera` and `polish`. **All 762 VIREO originals (764 with N and S):** 753 in `originals.json`, 11 kept in
  `identical.json` with a reason (RA:, RA, Dec:, YYYY/MM/DD, HH:MM:SS, PDot (S/S):, PEpoch (MJD):, ID #:, V - Mv =, NVO, Project CLEA);
  `General` «सामान्य» and the compass letters `E` «पू», `W` «प», `N` «उ» and `S` «द» (`HI_ONLY_ORIGINALS`, as French's).
- **How.** Thirteen Sonnet batches: five of about 150 originals first (they fix VIREO's names), then, with the merged originals and
  the glossary as reference, four catalog batches (165, 118, 146, 123 keys) and four map batches (observatory; camera; polish; the six
  small maps). The Opus implementer read every row. Decisions taken while reading (`glossary.json` and the plan §12):
  - **Pointing.** The verb is «इंगित करें» (Point telescope «दूरबीन इंगित करें», Point to coordinates «निर्देशांक की ओर इंगित करें»,
    Point to HTL selection «HTL चयन की ओर इंगित करें»), the noun «दिशा»; «घुमाएँ» stays VIREO's Slew. The batches had «घुमाएँ»,
    «लगाएँ», «साधें», «पॉइंटिंग» and «इशारा»; HI0's step 2 and tour now say «इंगित करें» too.
  - **Record vs committed.** «दर्ज करें» records a measurement (a classification, an equivalent width, Record Z); the radio receiver's
    bare Record button is «रिकॉर्ड करें» (a glossary exception); a committed interval or position is «पक्का», never «दर्ज».
  - **One form each:** उपकरण (instrument), पुनर्प्राप्त (recovered; the batches wrote प्राप्त, obtained, or बरामद), डोमेन (a checked
    domain, beside क्षेत्र for a field), आयात (import), पुरस्सरण (precession), होरा कोण, जूलियन तिथि, अप्रमाणित (uncertified), «यहाँ
    तुरंत लागू» (Live here), वेब अनुकूलन (web adaptation), गाइड स्टार कैटलॉग (Guide Star Catalog), z (lower case, as French), km/s,
    आधार (Base), «अक्षर» for characters, «क्या आप निश्चित हैं» for Are you sure, «तारीख» and «बाकी» without the Urdu nukta (Microsoft's
    rule: only ज़ and फ़; the lint caught ख़ and क़ in 13 strings).
  - **Joins in VIREO's word order.** «वस्तु » + id + « को प्रेक्षण सूची में जोड़ा गया।» (no verb agreement with the name), «तारागुच्छ » +
    name + « के सभी तारे वस्तु सूची में रखे गए», «इस शीर्षक के अंतर्गत कोई विषय चुनें: "…"»; the pieces VIREO closes with a straight
    `"` keep it (`lint-allowed.json`, as French).
  - **Glossary.** Patterns too broad for Hindi narrowed (Pleiades and Pulsars before `.HTL`, help in "will not help", counts in
    "counts as", stars in Guide Star Catalog); stems widened to the Hindi forms (गिन for counts, ट्रैक for tracking, लौटा/बहाल for
    restore, मर्ज/विलय); Telescope view and Original hot lists became terms with every case form (दूरबीन का/के/की दृश्य, मूल वस्तु
    सूचियाँ/सूचियों); new terms for web adaptation and pointing; new forbidden forms: चुनाव, स्थिरांक, मार्गदर्शक तारा कैटलॉग.
- **Names later fronts must keep.** HI1's guide keys quote E03 and E04 controls that HI3 and HI4 translate: «वर्गीकरण की समीक्षा
  करें», «वर्गीकरण सहेजें», «स्रोत फिर से लोड करें», «अभ्यास वस्तु», «वर्गीकरण स्रोत», «मूल अभ्यास लाइब्रेरी», «मानक − अभ्यास», «पूरी जाँच
  सहेजें/पुनर्स्थापित करें»; «कृत्तिका फ़ोटोमीटर सक्रिय करें», «फ़ोटोमीटर से मापें», «फ़ोटोमेट्री लक्ष्य», «दूरबीन की वास्तविक दिशा», «दूरबीन
  तैयार», «फ़ोटोमीटर फ़िल्टर», «आकाश की स्थिति», «B−V निकालें», «इंटीग्रेशन शुरू करें», «नोटबुक प्रविष्टि जोड़ें», «प्रयोग वर्ण–कांतिमान
  विश्लेषण». Those fronts use these forms or update the guide keys with them.
- **Tests.** `hi.test.ts` (HI1 keys and guide keys present, nine maps complete, 762 originals translated or kept, never both);
  `vireoSites.test.ts` `STRICT` gains hi; `languageText.ts` `HINDI_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}` with the 16 observatory maps,
  so `observatory.spec.ts` runs in Hindi; `walks/observatory.ts` `SAME_IN.hi` and `NOT_SAME_IN.hi` (General, No, Min, E, Angstroms);
  `exports-identity.spec.ts` the hot list, options file and Field Information families in `hi`; `areas.spec.ts` `PARTIAL` pins
  Hindi's areas (classification, fits, photometer); `dynamic-key-sites.json` gains the two Hindi map heads (fieldInfo's edit-layer
  caption, polish's Pulsars.HTL error), as French's. The real-Hindi walk adds the observatory: the open dome, Set Date/Time, Site
  Information, Enter Sky Coordinates for Slew, the options dialog, the hot-list entry form, the hot list and its popup menu and the
  Star Data List, at 320 and 1440 px.
- **Review (HI1, two independent Opus readers; no blockers).** Reader A (catalog and maps): Leave is «छोड़ें», never «बाहर निकलें»
  (Exit); prints quote VIREO's literals in Hindi, as they print («(दर्ज नहीं)», «(स्क्रीन की छवि अगले पेज पर प्रिंट हुई है)»); two
  «उतने … जितने … नहीं» sentences that inverted "more than"; Slew to coordinate draft as a command; रीडिंग feminine; «रैप» for wrapped
  counts; the naked-eye caption without a counted verb («दृश्य में तारे: {count}», since 0 stars is possible); «शुरू नहीं किया जा सकता»
  for Start blocked (not «बंद»); one form each for Next («आगे:»), admitted («स्वीकृत»), calibration («कैलिब्रेशन»), random
  («यादृच्छिक»), library («लाइब्रेरी», so the E03 name is «मूल अभ्यास लाइब्रेरी»), unsaved, route («मार्ग»), RA/Dec lines («विषुवांश …
  · क्रांति …»); and the remaining instrument, recovered, committed, H-R and pointing slips. Reader B (originals, glossary, tests): the
  Site Information latitude letters N «उ» and S «द» (they showed in Latin beside «पू»/«प»; `HI_ONLY_ORIGINALS` and `NOT_SAME_IN.hi`
  gain them, so 764 originals are covered); Show Help Topics... «सहायता विषय दिखाएँ...»; the Horizon Limit hint with «इंगित»; a tighter
  pointing term and preset pattern; one form for text file («पाठ फ़ाइल»), current hot list («वर्तमान»), auto («स्वचालित»), item
  («प्रविष्टि»), merge («विलय»), simulated, Monochrome Displays («एकवर्णी डिस्प्ले», apart from Demonstration Mode's «प्रदर्शन»);
  noise stays «नॉइज़» for the detector and «शोर» in signal-to-noise (a glossary term records both); and the walk now also opens Site
  Information, Enter Sky Coordinates for Slew and the Star Data List. Reader B ran the observatory spec for Spanish, Portuguese and
  French with the widened text collector: 27 passed.
- **Budget factor 1.3** (from 1.4): over the 713 keys, 751 originals and 14 maps Hindi is 1.15× French gzip (maps 1.16×); 1.16 × 1.1,
  rounded up. The core is 74.7 KB gzip by the planner's estimate.


## Russian: the observatory and VIREO's names (RU1)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU1" in the research root, following FR1 and ID1. Russian stays behind `?lang=ru`.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR1's and ID1's observatory keys (the instruments' prefixes and the Hubble hot-list reasons) and the 104 guide keys
  the observatory files render (read from `FR1_GUIDE_KEYS`, so the two never drift): 555 keys, about 8,160 English words. `ru.json`
  holds 715 keys. `sky.searchMatches` is the first plural family with all four Russian forms («1 подходящая цель / 3 подходящие цели
  / 5 подходящих целей», `.few` and `.many` beyond en.json, RU0's rule).
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`, `observationMetadata`, `camera`
  and `polish` (partial since RU0).
- **All of VIREO's originals at once:** the 762 texts (Spanish's 748 originals and 14 identical, plus `General` «Общие» and the
  longitude letter `E` «В»; `W` is «З»). 757 are in `originals.json`; five are kept with an `identical.json` reason (NVO, Project
  CLEA, HH:MM:SS, V - Mv =, PEpoch (MJD):). RU0's two kept names moved out of `originals.json`, so a kept original is never also
  translated. The two pieces VIREO joins with an untranslated straight `"` keep it (`quotes` allowances, as in pt and fr); the saved
  observations pair, both pieces ours, uses « ». `fragment|Meter Radio Telescope` is «Радиотелескоп {diameter} м».

**How it was translated.** Ten Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning context only): three catalog
batches (186, 186, 185 items with the two plural forms), three originals batches (246 each), four map batches (observatory; camera;
the six small maps; polish). Two map batches were interrupted by an app restart after writing their files; both files were complete
(map parity) and were not rerun. Then fix mode, then the strict lint, then an Opus read of every original and every catalog row; the nine maps were checked by
the lint, the consistency sweeps below and spot reads, and the independent review reads them in full.

**Decisions and corrections (implementer).**
- One form per VIREO name. Find is «Найти» (Find Text String «Найти текст», Find Pulsar Data «Найти данные пульсара»), Search
  «Поиск»; identification is «отождествление» (Pulsar Identification, Identify Recorded Object); Access Pulsar List «Открыть
  список пульсаров»; Access 0.4/1.0/4.0 Meter «Перейти к телескопу 0.4 м»; Horizon Limit «предел горизонта» everywhere; Spectral
  Range «Спектральный диапазон»; Comma Delimit «Через запятую»; Telescope Display «изображение телескопа» (not «дисплей»); the
  telescope and finder views «вид телескопа» / «вид искателя»; Master Receiver «основной приёмник»; Retain Current Data «Оставить
  текущие данные» (Save is «Сохранить»); Analyze Pulsar Data «Анализировать данные пульсаров»; Dispersion «Мера дисперсии».
- Detector counts are «отсчёты» («Базовое число отсчётов», «Базовое число фотонов»); Detect Sky «Определить фон неба»; pixel
  «пиксель» in every form; «USER OPTIONS!» keeps VIREO's capitals («ПАРАМЕТРЫ ПОЛЬЗОВАТЕЛЯ!»); GCVS is ОКПЗ in VIREO's text.
- VIREO is feminine throughout (RU0's decision; five sentences fixed); Object X is a name («Объект X», «Объекта X»), and its hidden
  identity is its «природа», not «личность»; telescope sizes in prose «0.4 м» (six strings wrote «0.4m»); quoted VIREO messages read
  as their originals («В поле не найдено звёзд!», «Номер лабораторного стола (не записан)»).
- Glossary: names that are VIREO originals now read as the reviewed original; names that prose inflects are stems (ПЗС-камер,
  ИК-камер, предел, основн, изображени, многоканальн, измеренн, Задать дату); terms widened for Russian forms (click «щелч», «кнопк»;
  practice «тренировочн»; tracking «отслежива»; restore «восстан»; draft «чернов»; standard time «поясн»; "guide" no longer matches
  Guide Star Catalog).
- Glyph counts: the `qsoList` menu paths gloss each VIREO name in place («Файл (File) › Список QSO (QSO List)») so the › count
  equals the English.

**Tests.** `ru.test.ts`: the observatory and guide keys present, every VIREO original translated or kept (never both, `E` «В»,
`W` «З»), the nine maps complete, the fixer a no-op, the ru lint clean. `vireoSites.test.ts` strict for ru. `languageText.ts`:
`RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}` with the 16 maps; `walks/observatory.ts` `NOT_SAME_IN.ru` (General, E) and the
«Телескоп ·» button. `exports-identity.spec.ts`: the hot list, options file and Field Information families gain `[ru]`; `N()` takes
Russian through the preview-language branch. `areas.spec.ts`: the partial case pins Russian's areas with text (`classification`,
`fits`, `photometer`).

**Size.** The ru core module is 75.6 KB gzip after RU1 (all originals and the observatory maps), within the 144 KB warning (×1.2).

**Review** (independent, two Opus readers: A the 557 catalog keys and nine maps, B the 757 originals, glossary and tests). No
blocker in A; one in B, fixed:
- *Join spaces (blocker, fixed).* Three LSS wedge pieces (`fragment|File`, `fragment|does not contain LSS redshift data!`,
  `fragment|Cannot access results file`) had lost the spaces VIREO joins them with («Файлname.TXTне содержит…»). A new `ru.test.ts`
  check requires every `fragment|` piece to keep the reviewed Spanish piece's edge spaces.
- *Meaning and grammar (fixed, about 30 strings).* «добавлен» (not «добавлено») after «Объект …»; the options dialog's sibling
  group boxes «Параметры» and «Режимы» (Options; a glossary `except`); U+2212 for the minus in «UT − местное время» and «… − 2 400
  000»; «выбранный объект №» (not «выбор», which reads as "elections"); «кроме случая, когда…» for Fast Scope Change; «интервал не
  меньше 100 мс»; «не совпадает в точности»; «Наведите телескоп» (not «Наведитесь»); «её красном смещении» after «Запись»; the
  classification width rule; «(его включило «{guide}»)», since the guide names already start «Руководство:»; ««-»» (the hyphen the
  file name writes, which the fixer had turned into a dash).
- *Quoted names equal their labels (fixed).* The telescope and palette buttons, «Задать дату/время, ID», «Видимая зв. вел. (V):»,
  «Все переменные звёзды ОКПЗ в области.», «Открыть доступ к Multi-Spec» and «Управление многоканальным спектрометром CLEA»; a quoted
  name is never inflected inside its quotes («в списке «Фильтр фотометра»», «при следующем нажатии «Пуск»»); Object X inflected stays
  unquoted («Объекта X»).
- *One form per term (fixed).* A photometer reading is «показание» (counts stay «отсчёты»; Reading Sky «Показание неба»); Activate
  «включите»; field engine «механизм полей»; Guide Star Catalog «каталог опорных звёзд»; acquiring a Hubble galaxy is «получить
  спектр»; local standard time «поясное»; «Front T5» as written; ID «ID» (not «ИД»); character limits «(не более 12 симв.)»; units
  «Мин.», «Сек.:»; Information «Сведения»; Save All/Clear All on spectra «Сохранить все»/«Очистить все»; VIREO's standing
  checkboxes imperfective («Показывать идентификатор объекта», «Вычислять скорость…», «Определять фон неба», «Ограничивать
  наведение»…), with every quote of them; Virtual Observatory (All Instruments) «(все приборы)»; classLab step titles infinitive.
- *Glossary (fixed).* RU1's loose stems became regex `terms` with stem and head noun («предел\p{L}* горизонт», «основн\p{L}*
  при[её]мник», «изображени\p{L}* телескоп», «спис\p{L}* QSO», «Параметры (?:ПЗС-|ИК-)?камер»…); «кнопк» and «учит» left the click
  and count terms.
- Nits applied: «ед. АЦП», «имена пользователей», «потенциальная яма», «это не защита» (also in RU0's two instructor-mode strings),
  «(1.0E-6 м)», «Список программы наблюдений...», and the areas comment names `photometer`'s source (RU0's two photometry keys).

**The walks only collected Latin text (fixed for the observatory).** `texts()` in `tests/i18n/walks/observatory.ts` kept a text only
when it had two Latin letters (`[A-Za-z]{2}`), so the Russian walk collected no Cyrillic text: its "reads differently" check passed
vacuously and its check that VIREO's fixed names appear on screen failed. It now keeps two letters of any script (`\p{L}{2}`); every
language's observatory walk passes with it (47 tests). The other walks (e01–e08, radio, help) have the same filter; each RU front
widens its own walk, and a note in the coordinator's shared notes tells the Hindi, Japanese and Chinese sessions.

**Names later fronts must keep.** RU1's guide keys quote E03 and E04 controls that RU3 and RU4 translate: «Источник классификации»,
«Исходная тренировочная библиотека», «Тренировочный объект», «Тренировка по списку программ», «Эталон − тренировочный объект»,
«Проверить классификацию», «Сохранить классификацию», «Повторить загрузку источника», «Сохранить полное исследование»,
«Восстановить полное исследование»; «Включить фотометр Плеяд», «Цель фотометрии», «Фактическое наведение телескопа», «Телескоп
готов», «Фильтр фотометра», «Положение неба», «Вычислить B−V», «Начать накопление», «Добавить запись в журнал», «Анализ цвет —
звёздная величина (работа)», «Измерить фотометром». Those fronts use these forms or update the guide keys with them.

## Hindi: E01 Hubble and E02 large-scale structure (HI2)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-hi.md §6.2 in the research root, with FR2 as its model. Hindi stays behind `?lang=hi`.

- **Catalog:** the 786 remaining keys of FR2's E01/E02 prefixes (continuity, lab, lssLab, hubble, velocity, ownResults, spectrum,
  spectrometer, the galaxy, sky and star exposures, ssp, exposureIO, drift, skyProfiles, fieldSpec, lss, wedge) and `classLink.open`;
  `hi.json` holds 1,499 keys. **Maps, complete:** `engineView`, `spectralRange` and `wedge` (with `plural('hi', …)`, CLDR's one for 0
  and 1). Five Sonnet catalog batches and one map batch from the English (French and Spanish as meaning only), then the
  implementer's pass and two independent Opus readers (reader A the E01 keys, reader B the E02 keys, the maps and the tests); 77
  review fixes on top of the lint fixes.
- **Decisions taken while reading** (the glossary records the terms):
  - The wedge diagram is «शंकु आरेख» (cone diagram, as French), redshift «अभिरक्त विस्थापन», «रेडशिफ़्ट» in brackets at a first
    mention; Plot «प्लॉट करें», as VIREO's Plot Data «डेटा प्लॉट करें» (the batches also wrote «आरेखित करें»).
  - **One form each:** acquired «अधिग्रहीत» (the spelling; «प्राप्त» stays for received), acquisition «अधिग्रहण», line shift
    «खिसकाव», cached pan «कैश्ड पैन», evidence «साक्ष्य», provenance «उद्गम», native «मूल», export «निर्यात करें» (beside import
    «आयात»), adaptation «अनुकूलन» but conversion «रूपांतरण» (a normalisation pass had merged them; the reader caught it), merge
    «विलय», random «यादृच्छिक», committed «पक्का किया गया», Activate «सक्रिय करें» (two keys with the English Activate
    spectrometer had «चालू/सक्रिय»; the E02 export family found it), arc minutes and seconds «चाप-मिनट/चाप-सेकंड», Declination
    degrees «क्रांति की डिग्री», Poisson «पॉइसन», noise «शोर» in these exercises.
  - VIREO's messages quoted in prose are in Hindi («क्षेत्र में कोई तारा नहीं मिला!», «आप इस तारे का स्पेक्ट्रम दर्ज नहीं कर
    सकते!»); Delphi's «Access violation … in module ‘CLEA_VEO.exe’» stays English, as French keeps it. VIREO names in prose carry
    their English at a first mention («“z दर्ज करें” (Record Z)», «“डेटा सहेजें” (Save Data, .TXT)»).
  - A sentence that ends on a Latin number, unit or name ends with «.» (the brief's rule; nine strings had «।»).
  - Every control quoted in a sentence reads as its own label (a script compared each short English label with the sentences that
    quote it: «अधिग्रहीत आकाशगंगा का विश्लेषण करें», «स्पेक्ट्रोमीटर सक्रिय करें», «प्रेक्षण परिणाम में देखें», «तारकीय अधिग्रहण»).
- **Tests.** `hi.test.ts` pins every key of the E01/E02 prefixes and the three maps complete; `HINDI_TEXT.done` gains e01 and e02
  with `E01_E02_MAPS.hi`, so `e01.spec.ts` and `e02.spec.ts` run in Hindi; `SAME_IN.hi` holds only «v · km/s · float32» (E01) and
  «RA» (E02); the telescope-view word list gains «दूरबीन ·»; the seven E01/E02 export families gain `hi`; `areas.spec.ts` pins
  Hindi's areas (classification, exposure, fits, lss, photometer); the real-Hindi walk runs the E01 and E02 walks at 320 and 1440 px.
- **The walk collectors read every script.** The E01 and E02 walks collected only text with two Latin letters, so every
  all-Devanagari string was invisible to their "reads differently" and View original checks; they now take two letters of any
  script with any combining marks between them (`\p{L}\p{M}*\p{L}`). RU1's `\p{L}{2}` in the observatory walk takes the marks too:
  a Devanagari vowel sign, virama or nukta is a mark, so «ग्रे» or «फ़ोटो» has no two adjacent letters. Latin languages collect the
  same texts as before, except the symbols «Δλ · Å», now in the shared same-text set.

- **Decisions taken while reading:**

    «मिलान» (right or wrong); inspection is «निरीक्षण», an inspected file «निरीक्षित».
  - **One form each:** candidate «संभावित» (the batches had «उम्मीदवार», the election word), printed «मुद्रित» (Printed-coordinate
    traces «मुद्रित-निर्देशांक ट्रेस»), endpoint grid «अंतबिंदु-ग्रिड», feature «लक्षण», shoulder «कंधा», pane «पैन», draft «मसौदा»,
    practice «अभ्यास», recovery copy «रिकवरी कॉपी», transformation and conversion «रूपांतरण», Program List «कार्यक्रम सूची» (VIREO's
    Program List... «कार्यक्रम सूची...»), Time-Dependent Data «समय पर निर्भर डेटा», Results Editors «परिणाम संपादक», Set classification
    wavelength range «वर्गीकरण की तरंगदैर्घ्य सीमा तय करें», Inspect comparison panes «तुलना पैन देखें», noise «शोर», as HI2.
  - HI1's guide keys quote E03's controls: every quote matches HI3's label («वर्गीकरण की समीक्षा करें», «वर्गीकरण सहेजें», «मानक −
    अभ्यास», «कार्यक्रम सूची का अभ्यास» after a one-word change, «पूरी जाँच सहेजें/पुनर्स्थापित करें»), and the guide's «विशेषताएँ» became
    «लक्षण».
  - The glossary's remarks term accepts the plurals «टिप्पणियाँ/टिप्पणियों» and restore the noun «पुनर्स्थापना» and «लौट».
