## Hindi: help (HI-HELP)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-hi.md §4.6 and §6.2 "HI6 ∥ HI-HELP" in the research root, with FR-HELP as its model and SC7's
Unicode help as its ground. Hindi stays behind `?lang=hi`. VIREO's 38 bundled help files and OpenVireo's own help are web adaptations by
OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`, their bytes, SHA-256 and provenance are unchanged, and «मूल देखें
(अंग्रेज़ी)» shows VIREO's exact text.

**What is translated.**
- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/hi/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'hi',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`) in VIREO's own format, Unicode
  Devanagari text (SC7), NFC, LF, no tab: every TXT line at 72 display columns or fewer and every title at 65 or fewer
  (`displayColumns`: a spacing vowel sign counts one column, a virama, nukta, anusvara, chandrabindu or a non-spacing vowel sign none);
  the same LST rows, kinds and file tokens (the unterminated `'स्पेक्ट्रम वर्गीकृत करें' 'begin` and `'परिवर्ती तारों के प्रकार'
  'begin` rows reproduced; HUBHLP and LSSHLP keep their blank lines and stay refused; MLTSPHLP and SPLSTHLP stay empty; OBJXHLP and
  TOOLSHLP, byte-identical originals, have identical translations); each section header is the row title it opens; SPECLNS.LNL equals
  the original and SPECLNS.TXT keeps VIREO's English line headers. Not in `LATIN1_HELP`.
- **Registry:** `help:{related:'संबंधित विषय'}` in `src/i18n/languages.json`; VIREO's "Related Topics:" reads «संबंधित विषय:», "See
  also:" «यह भी देखें:», NOTE «नोट:». Quotes are “ ” (no straight `"` in a TXT file, a test). Index titles shared between files read the
  same everywhere («उपयोगकर्ता इंटरफ़ेस», «डेटा ऑपरेशन», «डेटा फ़ंक्शन», the VIRMNHLP and OBXMNHLP rows, «स्लाइडर स्विच» in CAMHLP,
  PHOTOHLP and OBSRVHLP).
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («अनुवाद: OpenVireo का वेब अनुकूलन (CLEA का पाठ नहीं)» · «मूल देखें (अंग्रेज़ी)» · «अनुवाद देखें»;
  the fold note says what `foldHelpText` does for Hindi: the nukta and ZWJ/ZWNJ are ignored, other Devanagari is compared as written in
  NFC, Latin letters fold case and accents, and «अक्षर-भेद मानें» compares exactly) and `webHelp`, whose second paragraph of «OpenVireo
  क्या है» carries the plan's number sentence (decision of 2026-11-19: the decimal point and the English thousands comma, «1.5» and
  «24,000», only the digits 0–9 read as numbers, files the site saves stay English). Every control the web help names as a subject or
  object is in “ ” with its exact on-screen Hindi label.

**How it was translated.** Six Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish modules as models of format and meaning), writing
the modules directly and checking them with a scratch checker bundled from the project's own parsers (`parseHelpListText`,
`helpTopicBodyText`, `helpLinkTarget`, `displayColumns`; the same checks as `helpTranslation.test.ts`, run on all 38 modules together so
cross-file links resolve): VIRMNHLP with the two reminders; PULSRHLP with PLSLSHLP and PULSRLST; PLSANHLP, PULSRANL, SPECHLP and
SPECLNS; PHOTOHLP, OBSRVHLP and CAMHLP; CLASSHLP and MEASHLP; HRANALHLP, VARTYPEHLP, WDGPLTHLP and the eight index-only files. A
seventh batch wrote the two maps. The implementer unified the shared titles, fixed the lint findings (the web help's “?” key, “समय का
अनुरोध करें”, «आकाश पृष्ठभूमि», «वेब अनुकूलन», a VIREO ", ." slip read as «, ।») and recorded two lint allowances (the number
sentence's 0–9 range and examples; the site clock's full on-screen name with its ·) and one glossary allowance (the stale English
"Tools button" is the shell's «मेनू» button, as French writes «bouton Menu»).

**Review** (four independent Opus readers, 134 fixes, all applied; the checker and the Hindi tests pass after them): VIRMNHLP, the
reminders, the indexes and the two maps (63: control names in the web help put in “ ” with their exact labels, «3 · कोई उपकरण देखें»
and «दूरबीन के दृश्य पर जाएँ» as on screen, the reminder's dropped "CLEA's", the fold note's facts, «आपको आपके» → «अपने»); the radio and
pulsar help (35: the .PLR recording «रिकॉर्ड किया गया», not the measurement verb; meridian «याम्योत्तर» as the radio screen; readout
«रीडआउट»; the receiver's “रिकॉर्ड करें”; VIREO's Frequency Increment slip kept; «स्वेच्छ इकाइयाँ»; pan «खिसकाना» and “ज़ूम इन/आउट” as
the panel); the observatory, photometer, camera, H-R, variable-star and wedge help (17: "by factors of 10", "may not exactly match",
"mimics", «चाप-मिनट», «धुँधली … सीमा», the camera's «छवि सहेजें», «प्रोग्राम» for a program); the spectra help (19: "by a factor of 2",
an added blank line, «माप टूल» not «मापन», «ऐंगस्ट्रॉम» in prose, «तय करें» for a shared Set title).

**Tests.**
- `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts`: the structure checks run for Hindi (registry `help`), titled "the Hindi help files (Front
  HI-HELP)"; a new block runs the hi profile over each paragraph of every Hindi help text (typography: the danda, no space before । or
  , ; : ? !, nukta loanwords, only ज़ फ़ with a nukta, spelling, plain words, English words outside a VIREO English gloss; the आप register;
  the glossary's `forbidden`), with no straight `"` in a TXT file and no Devanagari digit.
- `src/i18n/hi/hi.test.ts`: the registry's `help`, and `helpViewer` and `webHelp` in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `HINDI_TEXT.done` gains `help` and `HELP_TEXT.hi` its rows (Devanagari has no case: the search finds
  «कर्सर की ऊर्ध्वाधर स्थिति» as written); `help.spec.ts` runs its eleven tests in Hindi. `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the
  help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=hi`. `tests/scripts/hindi.scripts.ts`: the real-Hindi walk opens the classification tool's help
  under the Devanagari test face at 320 and 1440 px (the topic list, the wrapped «स्पेक्ट्रमी लक्षण मापें/चिह्नित करें» topic, its print
  preview, File › Exit Observatory's reminder), as SC7's help-unicode walk does for the stand-in set.

**Left for later fronts.** HI6 (E06 and E08) ran in parallel: the help's pulsar-analysis names follow HI6's panel map («मौजूदा माप
दर्ज करें…», «दर्ज किए गए माप देखें…», «दर्ज किए गए माप सहेजें (.CSV)», «गुण…», «खिसकाएँ»), to be rechecked at merge. HI-REL: `released:true`.

## Hindi: release and search pages (HI-REL+SEO)

Merge note: Fronts JA-REL+SEO and RU-REL+SEO reached main first, so the merged tree has eight released languages (Hindi between
Indonesian and Japanese in registry order, Russian last): eight selector options, 72 search pages with ten alternates each, an
eight-language 404, and the unreleased gate rows of `src/i18n/languages.test.ts` on Simplified Chinese (zh-Hans), the only
unreleased language. The counts below are those of the Hindi front as reviewed.

The plan's HI-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-hi.md` §6.2, rows 6–7 of §1.2, the detection rows of §2, §9; user
decisions Q1 and Q2 of 2026-11-19: Western grouping «2,400,000» and Western digits 0–9) after HI0–HI6 and HI-HELP merged (main
06b264b): the catalog (3,606 of 3,606 en.json keys), all 33 maps, 753 VIREO originals and the 38 help modules complete. User
decision 5 of 2026-11-09: Hindi turns on once all of its sub-fronts are in. FR-REL+SEO is the model; the registry made the release one
field and the SEO table one row. AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: hi has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: `?lang=hi`
exactly (that load only), then the stored choice, then the first browser language: `hi`, `hi-IN`, `HI-in`, `hi-Latn` (rare; it opens
Devanagari Hindi), any `hi-*` in any case open Hindi; `en-IN`, `ur`, `ur-IN`, `mr`, `ne`, `bho`, `mai`, `awa`, `raj`, `pa`, `bn` and a
browser whose first language is another (`['en-IN','hi-IN']`, `['en-US','hi-IN']`) stay as they were (plan §2). `?lang=HI`,
`?lang=hi-IN` are no tag. The selector lists English, Español, Português (Brasil), Français, Bahasa Indonesia (released by ID-REL the same day,
merged first) and «हिन्दी (Hindi)» on every load, each in its own `lang` (a non-Latin autonym carries its English name). A change
from Hindi is stored.

**Strict catalog and maps.** `src/i18n/hi/hi.test.ts` now checks the registry line (`released:true`, no `thousands`, `help`),
`createI18n(hi,'hi')` without fallback, every map of `MAP_IDS` against its English shape, and that every map has an English to compare
with. `node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang hi`: 3606/3606 keys, 33/33 maps, 753 originals translated, "0 render sites or module
literals still pending". `infrastructure.test.ts` adds Hindi to the PT-REL review's formatter check: through `index.ts`'s
`formatNumber`, 14 values × 10 option sets print exactly what English prints («24,000», «2,400,000», «31,147»).

**Tests.** The Hindi specs open Hindi by the browser language alone, as FR-REL did for French.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `HINDI_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `contextLocale` gives it `hi-IN` (HI0's
  `HINDI_LOCALE`). Every language-parameterised spec now runs Hindi in an hi-IN browser without `?lang=`. A new
  `LAYOUT_IN_SCRIPTS_WALK` (Hindi) names the released languages whose script the main suite's fonts cannot draw.
- `areas.spec.ts`: Hindi left the `PARTIAL` list for `RELEASED_AREAS` («in a Hindi browser › Hindi (released) fetches its core…»).
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: six options (`RELEASED_OPTIONS`, ID-REL's name, and `RELEASED_VALUES`) on every load, and on each
  preview the released six plus the previewed language; HI0's preview test became HI-REL's detection test (hi,
  hi-IN, HI-in, hi-Latn and `['hi-IN','es-MX']` → hi; `['en-IN','hi-IN']`, `['en-US','hi-IN']`, ur-IN, mr, ne → English;
  `['es-MX','hi-IN']` → Spanish; `['fr-FR','hi-IN']` → French; a stored hi beats a pt-BR browser and a stored English an hi-IN
  one, `?lang=` beats both; a change from Hindi to French and back is stored with a clean address). The keyboard tests of French,
  Portuguese, Indonesian, Russian and Japanese step over Bahasa Indonesia and हिन्दी (merged after ID-REL).
- `tests/i18n/walks/e02.ts`: `SAME_PATTERN_IN.hi` allows the wedge print's date «9/11/2026, 7:00:20 am»: in an hi-IN browser the
  English page dates it by the browser locale and the Hindi page by its screen language (decision 9 of 2026-11-09), and CLDR's
  Hindi date-time (Latin digits, am/pm) reads the same.
- `narrow.spec.ts` and the 320 px check of `browsers.spec.ts` skip Hindi (`LAYOUT_IN_SCRIPTS_WALK`): under the main suite's fonts
  they would measure tofu (decided at HI0); Hindi's 320 px sweep is the real-Hindi walk (`tests/scripts/hindi.scripts.ts`), which
  now opens Hindi in an hi-IN browser. The engine smoke of `browsers.spec.ts` runs Hindi.
- `tests/scripts/rtl-css.scripts.ts` (left-to-right pages see no rtl.css) gains hi, and `rtl.scripts.ts` a test of its own for the
  spectrum's y-axis title RT7 keeps inside its plot in Hindi: at 1440 px it stays put; at 320 px Noto Sans Devanagari's taller
  ascent makes the rotated title cross the plot's left edge, so keepInsideSvg moves it right by 2.2 px (its designed move, as
  for CJK's Multi-Spec title), and the test allows a move only to the right and of at most 3 px. The RT7 test's title and
  languages are unchanged; Hindi's Multi-Spec titles were already in its walk. `routes.scripts.ts`'s scripts.css identity walk does
  not gain hi: scripts.css has `:lang(hi)` rules on purpose. `hindi.scripts.ts` gains the static search pages (`docs/SEO.md`,
  "Front HI-SEO").
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the released-language loop gains hi-IN (every route of `dist/` fetches the hi core and exactly
  its area's modules and logs nothing; the home shows six options under the release CSP; earlier titles kept); hi-IN and hi
  browsers read «31,147»; the 404 page has six languages.
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (HI0's test became HI-REL's), `languages.test.ts` (five released; six selector options; the gate rows
  move to Japanese, the next unreleased language), `partialLanguage.test.ts`, `pseudoScripts.test.ts` (an hi browser in the dev server
  opens Hindi, not qps-deva), `languageDirection.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts`.

**Release text.** The 404 page (built from `src/seo/languages.ts`, Front LAUNCH-3) gains Hindi's block («पेज नहीं मिला», `lang="hi"`,
linking `/?lang=hi#observatory`). README and THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt carry "Hindi translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's
files and texts are unchanged." with the same licence uncertainty as the other languages. `src/i18n/hi/index.ts`'s comment no
longer says "partial until its release".

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front HI-SEO"); what touches the language rules:
- **Data only**: an `hi` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['hi']`, `ogLocale:'hi_IN'`, prefix `/hi`, `app:'/?lang=hi'`),
  `src/seo/labs.hi.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX.hi='/hi'`, the `/hi/*` header rule and `public/og-image-hi.png` (rendered by
  `scripts/render-og-image.mjs hi` under the scripts fontconfig, whose Noto Sans Devanagari draws the Hindi; twice with the same
  SHA-256). No renderer, plugin, boot or `index.html` body change.
- **Text rules**: written for this front from OpenVireo's own English pages (no batch), linted with the `hi` profile and glossary:
  आप with -एँ imperatives, «।» at sentence ends, Western digits, the app's own Hindi lab names, and the footer «यह हिन्दी पाठ
  OpenVireo का वेब अनुकूलन है, CLEA का पाठ नहीं।» with the credit to Project CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF. The home's «यह
  कैसे काम करता है» says that numbers on screen use a decimal point and, as in English, a comma between groups of three digits; it
  shows no example, since the lint's `digits` rule reads a lone «24,000» with no English source as a decimal comma.

## Russian: E01 Hubble and E02 large-scale structure (RU2)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU2" in the research root, following FR2 and ID2. Russian stays behind `?lang=ru`.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR2's area: every key of the prefixes `lab`, `lssLab`, `hubble`, `velocity`, `ownResults`, `spectrum`,
  `spectrometer`, `galaxyExposure`, `skyExposure`, `starExposure`, `ssp`, `exposureIO`, `drift`, `skyProfiles`, `fieldSpec`, `lss`,
  `wedge` and `continuity` (RU2 owns the shared `continuity.*` and `ssp.header`), and `classLink.open`: 786 keys, about 10,460
  English words. `ru.json` holds 1,501 keys. The ru `exposure` and `lss` areas and their shared module now have text.
- **Maps, complete:** `engineView`, `spectralRange` and `wedge` (`notDrawn` and `outside` with all four Russian plural forms;
  `statusLoaded` and `rowCount` count-neutral).

**How it was translated.** Six Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning context only): five catalog
batches (96, 149, 159, 196 and 186 keys: the two guides; Hubble, velocities and Observational Results; the analyser with
`continuity.*`; the exposures, `.SSP`, drift and sky profiles; the field spectrometer, the survey and the wedge) and one for the
three maps. Then fix mode, the strict lint, a script that matches every quoted «…» name to a label, and an Opus read of every
catalog row and map against the English.

**Decisions.**
- **The wedge is «клиновидная диаграмма»** (the plan's choice: the ordinary adjective for wedge-shaped). RU1 had written both
  «клиновая» and «клиновидная»; the three originals (LSS Wedge Plot «Клиновидная диаграмма LSS», Wedge Plot Options, Print Wedge
  Plot...), the observatory and polish maps and the glossary name now read «клиновидная», the glossary terms `wedge plots?` and
  `wedge diagrams?` require it (with letter lookarounds: JavaScript's `\b` does not work around Cyrillic), and a `forbidden` row
  rejects every other «клинов…» form. No Russian source for either form was found. Blueshift is «фиолетовое смещение».
- **Terms, one form each:** acquisition «получение спектра» (never a bare «получение»), counts «отсчёты» (file samples «точки»),
  recipe «методика», seed «начальное значение», random state «состояние генератора случайных чисел», the timer's tick «такт»,
  cached pan «вид с кэшированным сдвигом» and fresh field «заново построенное поле» (RU1's `hubblePoint.help` aligned), native
  «оригинала», equivalence «эквивалентность», interpret «истолковать», rest wavelength «Лабораторная · Å», the Excel formula kept
  as the workbook writes it (`AVERAGE(v/D)`), AGN «АЯГ».
- **Buttons in the infinitive** («Экспортировать CSV ↓», «Экспортировать CSV скоростей», «Экспортировать CSV Хаббла», «Импортировать
  спектр ↑»), and every quoted name equals its label: the guides quote «Изучить полученный спектр для измерения», «Восстановить это
  получение спектра в телескопе», «Открыть „Результаты наблюдений“», «Сохранить снимок (заголовок VIREO)», «Анализировать этот
  спектр», «Видимая зв. вел. (V):», «Шаг z за такт», «Восстановить исследование (JSON)»; VIREO's messages quoted in Russian as
  RU1's `multispec` keys write them («Недопустимый спектральный индекс!», «Нельзя записать спектр этой звезды!»), while the Windows
  runtime's “Access violation … in module ‘CLEA_VEO.exe’” stays English.
- **Counts:** no count before an inflected noun («звёзд: {stars}, галактик: {galaxies}», «Предельное число строк…: {limit}»,
  «Исходных целей Hubble.HTL, поддерживаемых в этой версии: {supported} из {original}», «(галактик: {rows})» for the field option,
  with an `e02.spec.ts` check that accepts the noun before the count).
- **The Hubble.HTL target labels keep their en dash** («CorBor – 1», «UM-I – 3», «Coma – 1/2», «Boo – 1/2»), the name the selector
  shows: the Russian fixer had turned « – » before a digit into « — » (40 places, RU1's `hubblePoint.help` included); its `KEPT`
  spans now hold these labels, with a test.

**Corrections by kind.** The implementer's read changed about 60 strings beyond typography: meaning (the Sky background exposure
page named as a page; «Эти параметры записываются» for "the policy records them"; «приостанавливает экспозицию»; «не входят в
расчёт скоростей»), one form per term (above), quoted names (above), grammar («не восстановлен», «Несертифицированные»,
«Подтверждение измерения» for a dialog title, «Измерить красное смещение» for a section title), and glossary forms (catalog
«каталог|каталож», reference wavelengths «опорные длины волн», callbacks «обратные вызовы»).

**Other data files.** `identical.json` gains 11 rows (the number sign «№», SHA-256, JD, z, the wedge's Z, V / Z, V and Z binary32
and the degree sign). The glossary's catalog term accepts the adjective «каталожн…».

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts`: the three maps registered and complete, an RU2 coverage check (every key of the prefixes and
  `classLink.open`), the fixer a no-op and the lint over 1,501 keys and 19 maps.
- `src/i18n/lint/russianTypography.test.ts`: the target labels keep their en dash; other spaced dashes still become « — ».
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains `e01` and `e02`; `E01_E02_MAPS.ru`.
- **The E01 and E02 walks read any script.** `texts()` in `tests/i18n/walks/e01.ts` and `e02.ts` kept only texts with two Latin
  letters, so a Russian walk compared nothing (RU1 found this in the observatory walk). They now keep two letters of any script
  (`\p{L}{2}`), which also collects the line table's «Δλ · Å» in every language (a symbol and a unit, added to `SAME`). The E01
  walk finds the telescope button by «Телескоп ·» too.
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the seven E01 and E02 families gain `[ru]`; Record measurement uses ID2's
  `catalogStart(locale,…)`, and the LSS family's toolbar save takes Russian's `project.save`.
- `tests/i18n/areas.spec.ts`: the Russian partial case pins `classification`, `exposure`, `fits`, `lss`, `photometer`.

**Size.** ru: 7 chunks; the core module 98.0 KB gzip (French's FR2 core was 83.2 KB: ×1.18, within the ×1.2 budget factor), lss
10.6 KB, exposure 9.8 KB, exposure+lss 1.7 KB.

**Review** (independent, two Opus readers: A the 413 keys of the guides, Hubble, velocities, Observational Results, the analyser
and `continuity.*`; B the 390 exposure, drift, field spectrometer, survey and wedge keys, the three maps and the data and test
changes). No blocker. Fixed:
- *Should-fix (27).* The target labels' en dash (above); placeholder agreement («Пронаблюдайте галактику {id} · {label}»,
  «Галактика {id} · {label} … недоступна»); «Телескоп наведён на объект {id}»; «допуск звезды в щель» (not a fraction of stars);
  «хотя бы с одним каналом с положительным числом отсчётов»; the engine note («переносит поправку z в базовое значение (как
  «Центрировать регулятор»)… за края спектра»); one Russian form of VIREO's "Spectrum Index is Invalid!" (RU1's); the access-violation
  reasons («ближайшая к объекту звезда», «часы VIREO»); «Получение спектров галактик»; «единый параметр VIREO»; «Начальное значение
  генератора»; «Шаг z за такт» in its label's case; «Экспортировать CSV» without the arrow the English quote lacks; two count
  wordings (`lab.coverage`, `lab.next.interpretSupported`); «каждый архивный спектр галактики»; «Для пути с полученными
  спектрами»; the image scaling applied to all fields (`lab.limitsExtended`); «получена восстановленной процедурой сохранения»
  (not «производная»); «результат проверки» (not «отзыв»); «Лабораторная · Å»; samples «точки»; «команда «Записать»»; «в рабочей
  области Хаббла»; «в списке «Галактика для получения спектра»»; «получение спектра» spelled out (7 keys); «введено в окне «Данные
  наблюдений»».
- *Nits (about 25).* One form for equivalence, interpret, status and identification; the seven nits on the guide and analyser
  wording; tautologies («Повторное сохранение даёт те же отсчёты»); «Запасной файл оригинала»; the Default button wording; «в
  считываемые пиксели»; the wedge scheme gloss; and the forbidden row widened to every «клинов…» form except «клиновидн…».
- The readers confirmed: no «клиновая» left anywhere; the glossary regexes use no `\b`; the 11 identical reasons are true; the
  walk and spec changes make every language's checks stricter, none looser.

## Simplified Chinese: the folder, lints and shell (ZH0)

The first front of Simplified Chinese (`zh-Hans`), plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md` §1–§6 "ZH0" in the research root,
following FR0. Decided by the user (AGENTS.md, 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-19): 你; system fonts only; the decimal point kept; files unchanged;
Simplified first, then Traditional adapted from it (Taiwan standard). Chinese stays hidden (decision 5 of 2026-11-09): only a
`?lang=zh-Hans` preview reaches it until ZH-REL. Its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**The registry line.** `{"tag":"zh-Hans","dir":"zh-hans","catalog":"zh-hans.json","budgetFactor":1.4,"htmlLang":"zh-Hans","pagePath":"zh-hans",
"autonym":"简体中文","englishName":"Simplified Chinese","match":["zh-Hans","zh-CN","zh-SG","zh-MY","zh"],"except":["zh-Hant","zh-TW","zh-HK","zh-MO"],
"released":false,"notices":{…}}`, no `thousands` (Chinese writes en-US's comma and point natively), no `help` yet. The English name
is «Simplified Chinese» rather than the plan's «Chinese, Simplified», so the English notice reads «Simplified Chinese could not be
loaded; …» and the selector «简体中文 (Simplified Chinese)». Once released, zh, zh-CN, zh-SG, zh-MY, zh-Hans and zh-Hans-* browsers
open it; zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO, zh-Hant and zh-Hant-* keep English (Simplified offered in the selector) until zh-Hant exists; `?lang=`
takes `zh-Hans` exactly. Notices «无法载入简体中文；已改为显示英文。请刷新页面重试。» and «部分简体中文文本无法载入；该部分以英文显示。请刷新页面重试。».
`tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` takes this line instead of its stand-in; `CHINESE_LOCALE` is zh-CN.

**The folder** `src/i18n/zh-hans/`, as FR0's: `index.ts` (`CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED`), `zh-hans.json`, `originals.json`, `maps/`,
`glossary.json`, `identical.json`, `lint-allowed.json` and `zh-hans.test.ts`.
- **Translated:** the 130 shell keys and the 27 `home.*` keys (157; 1,702 English words), and HI0's `app.digitNotice` (131 shell keys since); the complete maps `appShell`, `helpMenu`,
  `languageChoice` (legend «语言 / Language»), `projectLinks` and `tour`; the partial maps `objectXLab` (`nav` «天体X», `guideNav`),
  `polish` (`clusterGuide.nav`, `results.help`) and `variableStars` (`nav`); the 10 VIREO captions the shell shows (Help «帮助», Topics...
  «主题...», Websites «网站», 2MASS Catalog «2MASS星表», About This Exercise... «关于本实验...», Results Editors «结果编辑器»,
  Observational Results «观测结果», Pulsar Results «脉冲星结果»; Project CLEA and NVO kept).
- **Style** (plan §3): 你, the reader often implicit; buttons verb + object without 请 («保存探究», «恢复探究（JSON）»); 请 only in
  requests («请刷新页面重试»); investigation «探究», tour «导览», lab «实验», instructor mode «讲师模式», Demonstration Mode «演示模式» with
  VIREO's English in full-width brackets at the first mention in prose; VIREO's menu paths quoted («“文件 › 偏好设置”»). The page
  bar's «更多» starts its accessible name «更多探究操作» without a space (`src/components/pageBar.test.ts` allows no gap for zh).
- **How it was translated:** three Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`, from the English, French shown only as meaning context, one
  brief with the plan's §3–§5): the 157 keys, the eight maps with the 10 originals, and the glossary seed. The Opus implementer read
  every row and changed 7 (before the review): Slew «转动» (not «转向») in `project.radioBlock.slewing`, the photometer's «“记录/回看”» as one name,
  `helpMenu.websitesNote` naming NAVO in Chinese and without the dash the English lacks, `instructorMode.confirm.what` quoting the menu
  path instead of spacing it, «学生» where VIREO says students, «哈勃实验的输入» for "Hubble inputs"; and the glossary's Tools button
  «“菜单”按钮» (OpenVireo's phone menu button reads «菜单»).
- **Glossary** (`glossary.json`, `zh-Hans` field): the French glossary's English patterns re-targeted to Chinese: 152 terms (4 dropped
  as context-dependent: counts, filters, bins, fresh sky; listed in `dropped`) and 209 names, 13 `forbidden` rows (您; 群集/集团 for a
  cluster; 红位移; 文档 for a file; Traditional or Taiwan forms such as 射電, 哈伯, 檔案, 軟體; 调查 for investigation; 教师模式…).
  Rows to recheck as their areas arrive: Scan «浏览» (ZH3), Lab Table «实验台», Request Time «申请时间», colour excess «色余», finding
  chart, epoch, aperture, integration (ZH1–ZH6).
- **Independent review** (Opus, high effort; no blocker). Fixed: «谱线列表» for the spectral line list (`error.lineFormat`, was
  «行列表», a list of rows); one verb for Load, «载入» (the notices and three strings had «加载»; the glossary's load term is now
  «载入|刷新» and «加载» is forbidden); the radio clock «射电时钟» in the glossary (it had «无线电时钟»); aperture «口径|孔径» (the
  photometer's aperture is «孔径», ZH1/ZH4 choose per string); «位移后的波长» (z may be negative); «准备和光度计种子草稿» (not
  «预处理», preprocessing); «以使用最新版本继续»; `helpMenu.exitNote` glossing OK, Yes, Cancel and No alike without nested brackets;
  the receiver's Mode button «“模式”（Mode）按钮» (ZH5 confirms); glossary names «观测程序列表», «申请观测时间», «单色显示器», and two
  duplicate rows removed; English nested in Chinese no longer inherits the marks face (below). A single «…» is kept where VIREO and the
  English write one (`正在载入…`, «讲师模式…»: UI convention, and the glyph rule counts it); «……» is for running prose.
- **No Chinese fixer yet.** The zh-Hans profile's typography rule names each mark to change; the CJK punctuation fixer that Front JA0
  builds (`src/i18n/lint/cjkPunctuation.ts`, shared notes 2026-11-19) is reused once merged, rather than a second one here. The full-width
  digit notice is Front HI0's, for every language; ZH0 translates its key after merging HI0: `app.digitNotice`
  «请用半角数字0–9输入。此处不会把{digits}这类字符当作数字读取。» (half-width digits named as such, since a Pinyin input method types
  full-width ones; plan §1.2 #4).

**Lint calibration** (`src/i18n/lint/profiles.ts`): over the 231 shell strings of 12+ characters the grapheme ratio to English is p10
0.24, median 0.33, p90 0.47, so the Chinese length band is 0.15–0.8 (SC4's 0.3–1 reported a third of correct strings). Two strings
remain reported for the reviewer: «变星» for Variable stars (0.14) and «GitHub上的OpenVireo» (0.89). The band is the profile's, so zh-Hant
(adapted from these strings) shares it; ZT0 recalibrates if its ratios differ.

**The Simplified/Traditional table** (`src/i18n/lint/hanVariants.ts`, the script-purity rule of both Chinese profiles) is generated
by `scripts/han-variants.mjs` (local only; `--check` compares): ICU's Hans-Hant and Hant-Hans transforms (ICU 78.3, already installed;
nothing downloaded) propose pairs, kept only when one-to-one and confirmed by the character sets of Node's own decoders (the
Simplified form in GB2312's 6,763 hanzi and not Big5, the Traditional form in Big5's 13,060 standard hanzi and not GB2312). SC4's 71
seed pairs stay (`SEED_PAIRS`), and shared or one-to-many characters (发 后 里 干 面 台 系 只 复 历 范 云 …, `EXCLUDED_CHARACTERS`) are
never listed: 2,046 pairs, with the ICU version and the input and output SHA-256 in the file. It is a lint table, never a converter
(ICU maps 发→發, 面→麵 blindly). `rules.test.ts` checks the bijection, the seed and the exclusions.

**Full-width marks** (`src/theme/scripts.css`, plan §1.2 #3): Chinese “ ” ‘ ’ … — · came from the Latin fonts that start every stack,
narrow and proportional. A `[lang]:lang(zh)` rule now puts `var(--ov-font-marks)` first, set per variant to «OpenVireo CJK Marks SC»
or «… TC»: four `@font-face` rules (Regular and Bold per variant) whose `src` is only `local()` system fonts (PingFang, Microsoft
YaHei/JhengHei, Noto Sans CJK, Source Han Sans) and whose `unicode-range` holds only those marks, so every other character falls
through as before and nothing is fetched (`scripts.test.ts`: four faces, `local()` only, the range). The production CSP
(`default-src 'none'`, no `font-src`) does not block `local()`: checked in Chromium and Firefox with a scratch page; WebKit could not be
launched on this machine (Playwright's WebKit build needs Ubuntu libraries), so it is unverified there and fails soft (narrow marks).
`tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts` proves the face: with DejaVu Sans placed after the marks face the marks still come from Noto Sans
CJK SC/TC, Regular and Bold, and without it from DejaVu. Chromium's `text-spacing-trim` halves adjacent CJK punctuation, as Chinese
typography does; widths are not pinned. A nested element in another language (a VIREO mirror view or the English half of a notice, `lang="en"`) would
inherit the resolved family with the marks face first, so `:where([lang]:lang(zh) [lang])` gives it the stack without the face at
zero specificity (a nested Chinese element keeps it). WebKit, where PingFang lives, is to be checked on a machine that runs it (the
China testers' checklist, refreshed at ZH-REL, covers Safari on an iPhone).

**Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` (registration, notices through the profile, the 157 keys, map shapes, originals, literal characters, en-US
number formatting and the single plural form, the lint with the glossary, the calibration print); `languages.test.ts`,
`locale.test.ts` (preview only before ZH-REL; after it Simplified browsers open Chinese and Traditional ones keep English),
`direction.test.ts`, `pageBar.test.ts`; `tests/i18n/languageText.ts` `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT` (letters: Han and ，。、：；？！（）;
`done` = shell); `shell.spec.ts` in zh-Hans through `translatedFor('shell')`; `areas.spec.ts` brings back the partial-language test
(as FR0) for zh-Hans; `language-selector.spec.ts` (zh, zh-CN, zh-SG, zh-Hans-TW, zh-TW browsers and a stored zh-Hans get English with
no Chinese module fetched; `?lang=zh-Hans` opens Chinese with five options, each in its own `lang`); `exports-identity.spec.ts` the
toolbar's Save investigation byte-identical in zh-Hans; `routes.scripts.ts` the shell in real zh-Hans at 320 and 1440 px (home, the
header menu, OpenVireo's help viewer: no layout problem or cut ink, Han from Noto Sans CJK SC alone) and the marks face.

**The CJK punctuation fixer.** Front JA0's `src/i18n/lint/cjkPunctuation.ts` (merged after ZH0's review) has a Simplified Chinese
style (`SIMPLIFIED_CHINESE_STYLE`: ，, “ ”, ‘ ’). It would change none of ZH0's 266 strings, and `zh-hans.test.ts` keeps it that way
(VIREO originals keep their own quotes); later ZH batches run it before the lint.

**Size.** zh-Hans holds 2 chunks, 9.3 KB gzip (core 9.0). For the same 157 keys the Chinese JSON gzips to 0.97× the French, so Chinese
text is not heavier than French once compressed; the factor 1.4 stays until ZH1's larger sample proposes the final one (likely lower).

## Russian: E03 spectral classification (RU3)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU3" in the research root, following FR3, ID3 and HI3. RU3 is cut from main after
RU2's merge (RU2 owns `continuity.restoreExposure`, `ssp.header` and `classLink.open`, which E03 also renders). Russian stays behind
`?lang=ru`.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR3's area: every key of the classification module and of classification+cluster (`class`, `classLines`,
  `classLink`, `classAtlas`, `classImport`, `classLab`, `feature`, `cursor`, `probe`, `photo`, `combined`, `combinedWidth`,
  `viewport`, `inspection`, `sampledTrace`, `reverseTrace`, `sampledDiff`, `range`, `widthSource`, `widthCoordinates`,
  `sharedMarks`, `comparisonMark`, `paneSource`, `panMarks`), the observation notebook (`obs`), the DAT, TXT and CSV inspectors
  (`nativeResults`, `nativeEdit`, `nativeDelete`, `nativeList`, `nativeText`, `nativeCsv`) and the Program List (`program`,
  `programImport`): 741 new keys (772 with RU1's guide keys and RU2's three shared keys). `ru.json` holds 2,242 keys. The ru
  `cluster` area now has text (the shared classification+cluster module). No map is needed.

**How it was translated.** Five Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning context only; 149, 144, 150, 131
and 167 keys), then fix mode, the strict lint, the quoted-name script over every Russian row, and the implementer's read.

**Decisions.**
- **Terms, one form each** (the glossary's where it has one): shoulder «плечо» (the batches had mixed «край»), endpoint grid «сетка
  конечных точек» (they had «сетка от конца до конца», «сетка концов» and «сетка по конечным точкам»), grayscale «оттенки серого»
  (Reverse photographic grayscale «Обратить оттенки серого на фотографии»), bins «каналы», raw «необработанный», import
  «импорт/импортированный» (one batch had «открыть/открытый», which the rest of the catalog keeps for Open), source-plus-sky
  «отсчёты источника + неба» (RU2's), unknown «неизвестная» (a star) or «спектр неизвестной», source or acquisition identity
  «идентификатор» (never the calque «идентичность»), photo «фотография» (Combined photo and trace «Совмещённый вид: фотография и
  кривая»; «снимок» stays the acquired snapshot), fallback «запасной», printed «напечатанный», inspect (a DAT, TXT or CSV file)
  «просмотр/просмотреть» (never «проверить», which would claim verification), canonical «канонический», file layout «структура»,
  type code «код класса», number encoding «кодирование» (a character set is «кодировка»), JavaScript's Number kept as «Number»,
  integration «интегрирование» (the glossary term now accepts «интегрир»), integrand «подынтегральная функция».
- **Undo is «операция отмены» or «возможность отмены»** where a bare «отмена» could read as the Cancel button; the disabled Undo
  button reads «Нечего отменять в DAT».
- **The brief's class.save was wrong:** Save classification session is «Сохранить сеанс классификации» (`class.save`), while
  «Сохранить классификацию» is the dialog's Save classification (`class.confirmSave`); `program.fullDraftError` now quotes the former.
- **`class.samples` follows a number in the code** (`{rows} {t('class.samples')}`), so it is the uninflected «отсч. исходного
  файла»; `classLink.samples` follows a fixed 600 («нормированных каналов полученного спектра»).
- **Quoted names equal their labels:** «Задать диапазон длин волн классификации», «Перевернуть кривые», «Дискретизированные кривые
  интенсивности», «Совмещённый вид: снимок и кривая», «Эквивалентная ширина вручную», «Открыть результаты наблюдений», «Диапазон
  показа по умолчанию», «Применить диапазон показа»; RU1's classification guide now quotes «Предыдущий эталон» and «Следующий
  эталон» (it had «Назад»/«Далее»). Key names are not quoted (Пробел, Shift, Enter).
- **Counts:** `obs.records` and `feature.records` keep the English «· {count}» form («Результаты наблюдений · {count}»), other
  counts stay count-neutral («Результатов: {count}», «строк: {count}»).

**Other data files.** `identical.json` gains 8 rows (parameter formats such as «{position} · {object}», the unit format of
`viewport.range`, and SHA-256).

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts`: an RU3 coverage check (the 772 E03 keys).
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains `e03`, so `e03.spec.ts` runs in Russian (the E03 walk already reads every
  script, since HI3).
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the E03 family gains `ru` (the Edit observation result button and the heading by
  Russian's catalog).
- `tests/i18n/areas.spec.ts`: the Russian partial case pins `classification`, `cluster`, `exposure`, `fits`, `lss`, `photometer`.

**Review** (independent, two Opus readers: A the 363 classifier keys and the four changed guide rows; B the 378 notebook, inspector
and Program List keys and the data, test and docs changes). Fixed:
- *Blockers (4, reader A).* «Обмен снимков местами» for reversing the photos' grayscale (`combined.tracePolicy`); "bracket-only" read
  as brackets and Number as «число» (`sampledTrace.limits`); "one of the spectra" turned into "none" (`sampledDiff.unavailableOnly`);
  type and grade swapped in `feature.limit`.
- *Should-fix (about 45).* The terms above (photo, identifier, fallback, printed, inspect, canonical, layout, type code, encoding,
  raw, practice «тренировочный объект», feature «деталь спектра»); quoted names (`class.save`'s, «(группа «Режимы»)» for VIREO's
  Options, no quoted «Правка» for the notebook's edit buttons, «в списке «Отображение спектра»»); four «Просмотр…» buttons now
  infinitives; meaning («не меняет координаты под новый объект», «добавляет в начало», «копия для восстановления сохранена»,
  «Объект, заявленный в записи» keeps the claim, «модуль чтения» not «читатель», «подынтегральная функция», «Происхождение
  классификации полученной звезды/импортированного спектра», «состояние генератора случайных чисел · выборок»); agreement
  (`sampledDiff.help`, `classImport.gridSame`); `class.samples` (above).
- *Nits (about 30),* among them «Z» for the TXT column (as `nativeText.profile.exercise4` and the file write it), «нуль», «Записей
  в изменённой копии: {count}.», «латинские буквы и цифры» for the file-name advice, «отметка» for the student's marks (a line's
  marker stays «метка»), and «позитивная фотография».
- Left as is: `program.choose` «Тренировка по списку программ» (RU1's guide quotes it); `obs.title` «Результаты наблюдений», which
  equals VIREO's list name as the English nearly does.
- The readers confirmed: the placeholder sets, count-neutral forms, ё, quotes, no-break spaces and glyph counts; the caveats keep
  their strength; the 8 identical rows are true; the tests are as strict as HI3's and none is loosened.

## Japanese: the observatory and VIREO's names (JA1)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §5.3 and §6.2 "JA1" in the research root, following FR1, HI1 and RU1. Japanese stays
behind `?lang=ja`; its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR1's observatory keys (the instruments' prefixes and the Hubble hot-list reasons) and the 104 guide keys the
  observatory files render (read from `FR1_GUIDE_KEYS`): 555 keys, about 8,160 English words. `sky.searchMatches.one` and `.other`
  carry the same Japanese (one plural form).
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`, `observationMetadata`, `camera`
  and `polish` (partial since JA0).
- **All of VIREO's originals at once:** the 764 texts (Spanish's 760 plus `General` «全般» and the compass letters `E` «東», `N`
  «北», `S` «南»; `W` is «西»). 755 are in `originals.json`; nine are kept with an `identical.json` reason (NVO, Project CLEA, OK,
  YYYY/MM/DD, HH:MM:SS, (km/sec), PDot (S/S):, PEpoch (MJD):, V - Mv =); JA0's two kept names left `originals.json`, so a kept
  original is never also translated.

**How it was translated.** Ten Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning context only): three catalog
batches (185 each), three originals batches (252, 252, 250) and four map batches (observatory; camera; the six small maps; polish).
Then fix mode and the strict lint; the implementer read every original; an Opus reader read every catalog key and map string and
corrected 149 of them; then the independent review.

**Decisions.**
- *Display / View* (plan §5.3): VIREO has a Display menu and View/… items but no View menu in the same window, so both are «表示»: the
  menu «表示», the items «スペクトルの表示／保存...», «データの表示／印刷», «リストから表示／選択...».
- *One form per term:* target «目標» (VIREO's 目標座標; never ターゲット or 対象), pointing «指向», clock «時計», instrument «観測装置»,
  IR camera «赤外線カメラ» (VIREO's filter-set label stays «IR»), this version «このバージョン», checked domain «確認済みの範囲»,
  admitted «採用済み», spectral control «分光操作», import «インポート», fallback «代替設定», Base «基準» (基準等級, 基準移動速度,
  基準ゲイン係数, 背景の基準値), a start blocked «…の測定を開始できません», Reveal «正体を明かす» and a hidden target «正体がまだ明かされて
  いない天体Xの目標», the web adaptation tag «Web版の調整：» and «（Web版の制限）» (ordinary words keep ウェブ: ウェブサイト).
- *VIREO names:* Confirm is «確認» (VIREO uses it only as a dialog caption; JA0's glossary row said 確定); Item «項目» (現在の項目／位置を
  リストに追加...); Hour «時間» (the time-zone unit; the RA labels' Hours «時» is another original); signal-to-noise «S/N比» for both
  VIREO labels; flux «フラックス» (時間平均したフラックス密度); Multi-Spec stays VIREO's name.
- *Quoted VIREO messages* read as their Japanese originals («（未記録）», «（画面イメージは次のページに印刷されます）», «位置を…追加しました。»),
  with the English in brackets where a web text glosses them.
- *Pieces VIREO joins with a value* read in VIREO's order: «選択#{n}を削除しますか？», «保存した観測ファイル「…」を読み込みました。» (both pieces
  ours), «ファイル…にはLSS赤方偏移データが含まれていません！», «{diameter}メートル電波望遠鏡» (a `placeholders` allowance, as es, pt, fr and
  ru), and the tails after an ID take を («を観測リストに追加しました。»). Japanese joins with no space, so `ja.test.ts` requires every
  `fragment|` piece to have no edge space (Russian's rule copies Spanish's spaces instead) and every original to keep its `\r\n`
  count. Known compromises, where the other piece is VIREO's or core's: the two straight-quote pieces (`" Not Found.`, «…記録していま
  せん："», `quotes` allowances as in pt, fr, hi and ru), `Object: (` «天体：(» which VIREO closes with an ASCII `)` (kept by
  `ASCII_BRACKET_PIECES` in `ja.test.ts` and a `typography` allowance), the colon tails before a file name, and VIREO's range word
  `to` «–» (an en dash, plan §4.1: no wave dash; `script` and `glyphs` allowances).

**Lint and fixer changes (shared with Chinese).** `cjkTypography` in `src/i18n/lint/profiles.ts` and the fixer in
`cjkPunctuation.ts` now agree on two exceptions: a `.` before an ASCII letter, digit or `{` is a file extension, not a sentence
period («スペクトルを.SSPファイルとして保存»); and a symbol's own brackets stay ASCII: a pair right after an ASCII letter or digit whose
content has no space and is short or not all letters (E(B-V), E(B−V), StrToInt(x)), and the parenthetical that ends an English
name inside a full-width gloss («（App. Mag (V)）», «（Virtual Observatory (All Instruments)）»). An English gloss is no symbol:
«天体ID(Show Object ID)を表示» and «QSO(Quasar)は» still become full-width (fixer cases and lint fixtures for each).

**Tests.** `ja.test.ts`: the observatory and guide keys present, every VIREO original translated or kept (never both; 北 南 東 西 全般),
fragments with no edge space and every `\r\n` kept, the nine maps complete, the fixer a no-op, the lint clean. `vireoSites.test.ts`
strict for ja. `languageText.ts`: `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}` with the 16 maps; `walks/observatory.ts`
`SAME_IN.ja`, `NOT_SAME_IN.ja` (General, No, Min, E, N, S, Angstroms) and «望遠鏡 ·» in the Multi-Spec walk. `exports-identity.spec.ts`:
the hot list, options file and Field Information families gain `ja`. `areas.spec.ts`: Japanese's areas with text are
`classification`, `fits` and `photometer`. `dynamic-key-sites.json` gains the two ja map heads.

**Size.** The ja core chunk is 66.4 KB gzip (226.6 KB raw) after JA1, against Russian's 75.6 KB at RU1; `budgetFactor` 1.4 stays
(warning at 168 KB) for the Unicode help set JA-HELP measures.

**Review** (independent, two Opus readers: A the 559 catalog keys and nine maps, about 880 strings; B the 755 originals, identical
and allowance entries, the glossary, the lint changes and the tests). Fixed:
- *Meaning:* VIREO's save question said "if you will not analyze it later" («すぐに解析しない場合は»); the polar limit names either pole
  («天の極»); the Hubble guide's cached-pan view covers both reused fields; the CCD note's list of conversions.
- *Terms:* the one-form list above (instrument, IR, version, checked, admitted, spectral control, target, import, fallback, start
  blocked, reveal, Base); the quoted «望遠鏡の準備ができています» and «仮想天文台（全観測装置）» equal what the screen shows.
- *VIREO's menu items* «領域内のすべてのGCVS変光星» are no sentences; the radio date hints share one register; «中央にあるとは限りません»;
  «熱電冷却の場合のみ適用されます»; «（ユリウス日番号−2,400,000）» with the minus sign.
- *Lint:* the symbol-bracket exception was first broader (any ASCII gloss after a Latin word); it is narrowed as above.

**Names later fronts must keep.** JA1's guide keys quote E03 and E04 controls that JA3 and JA4 translate: «分類を確認», «標準星 − 練習»,
«完全な調査を保存», «元データの読み込みを再試行», «実習リストを読み込む», «HTLの選択位置へ向ける», «測光器で測定», «プレアデス測光器を有効化», «測光目標»,
«望遠鏡の実際の指向», «観測ノートに項目を追加», «B−Vを計算», «有効化», «開始». Those fronts use these forms or update the guide keys with them. The
radio receiver's Record stays «記録» (plan §5.3 named «受信データの記録»; JA5 decides on screen).

## Russian: E04 Pleiades photometry and E05 cluster H-R diagrams and variable stars (RU4)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU4 ∥ RU5" in the research root, following FR4, ID4 and HI4. RU4 is cut from main
after RU3's merge; RU5 (the radio telescope) follows on its own branch. Russian stays behind `?lang=ru`. The translations are web
adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR4's area, 587 new keys: every key of `ph`, `pa`, `pleiadesLab`, `nc`, `db`, `ce`, `clusterLab`, `ownHr` and
  `obsHr` (651 with RU1's guide and photometer keys), and the 24 Russian plural forms the `obsHr` families need: all four forms
  (one, few, many, other) of `reviewSummary`, `replaceQuestion`, `current`, `chartDescription` and `offAxes`, and the two-level
  `kindWarning` (4 outer × 5 inner, `none` included): 40 keys, pinned by `ru.test.ts`. `ru.json` holds 2,853 keys.
- **Maps, complete:** `clusterHr` and `clusterData` (new), and `variableStars` (partial since RU0, only `nav`), with the
  variable-star count grouped with U+202F («31 147»).
- **Identical to English** (`identical.json`, 12 rows with reasons): band letters and symbols (`ph.fixed`, `obsHr.blockedField.*`,
  `obsHr.modulus`, `obsHr.ebv`, `obsHr.colBV`, `obsHr.colIndex`), SHA-256, the variable-star type option format, the empty action
  column and the em dash for no period. `lint-allowed.json`: `pa.legend`'s «визуальные ориентиры» are guide lines on the chart, not
  an exercise guide.

**How it was translated.** Five Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches (113 and 112 `ph` keys; `pa`, `pleiadesLab` and `nc`, 108; `db`, `ce` and
`clusterLab`, 128; `ownHr` and `obsHr` with the 24 plural forms, 150) and one batch for the three maps, from the English with the
reviewed French and Spanish as meaning context, under RU0–RU3's briefs extended with the E04/E05 terms below and the control names
RU1's guides already quote. Then fix mode, the ru lint (count-neutral rewrites of four «{count} из {total} допущенных звёзд»
sentences, the Guide Star Catalog and the manual's title glossed in brackets) and a quoted-name check of every « » against the labels.

**Decisions.**
- **Terms:** turn-off «точка поворота главной последовательности», then «точка поворота»; ZAMS «начальная главная
  последовательность (НГП)», then «НГП» (the glossary's zero-age term now also accepts it; VIREO's Tools item keeps its literal name
  «Главная последовательность нулевого возраста»); a model trial «проба»; the fit «подгонка»; offset «сдвиг»; uncertainty estimate
  «оценка погрешности»; extinction correction «поправка на межзвёздное поглощение»; reference cluster and values «эталонный», a
  reference point «опорная точка»; VIREO's native ZAMS «НГП VIREO», native isochrones and models «исходный»; integration
  «накопление»; a reading «показание»; count rate «скорость счёта» (never a bare «скорость», which reads as a velocity); photon draw
  limit «предел в 1 000 000 фотонов на один розыгрыш»; 32-bit «32-разрядный»; Guide Star Catalog «каталог опорных звёзд (Guide Star
  Catalog)», as RU1; B and V as magnitudes are feminine («Ваша B», «Сохранённая V»), B−V masculine.
- **Data from Observations is «Данные из наблюдений»**, apart from VIREO's Observational Data «Данные наблюдений»: both names appear
  on the E04/E05 pages (French keeps them apart the same way); the glossary name row changed with it.
- **Control names:** the notebook's Cancel is «Отменить запись в журнал»; Retry selected model «Повторить загрузку выбранной модели»
  (also in `obsHr.retryModel`); Load saved observation results «Загрузить сохранённые результаты наблюдений (DAT)»; the guide's
  status Done is «Выполнено», as in the classification guide.
- **Counts after a placeholder** are count-neutral where the value can be fractional or any number («исходный радиус апертуры (в
  пикселях): {radius}», «световых лет: {ly}», «байт: {bytes}»); the variable-star results line puts the noun before the total
  («первые 100 подходящих записей из 121»).

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts`: the three maps in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`; the RU4 coverage check (`RU4_KEYS`, 651) and the 40
  `obsHr` family keys.
- `src/i18n/dynamic-key-sites.json`: the ru variableStars map's `printCurveLayout` head, as for the other languages.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains `e04` and `e05`; `E04_E05_MAPS.ru`. `e04.spec.ts` and `e05.spec.ts` run in
  Russian with no `SAME_IN.ru` (no English word stays on those screens beyond the shared set).
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the E04 and E05 families gain `ru` (`RU_E04(key)` throws for a key Russian lacks, with the
  `ph.title` and `db.title` headings), `catalogCount` writes «31 147» for Russian, and `SCRIPT_LETTERS` looks for Russian letters only
  in a file that is UTF-8 text: the E05 Cluster Data HTL's doubles happened to hold the bytes of a Cyrillic letter (D0–D3 then
  80–BF), as Front SC10 found for CJK in the pseudo-locales. Binary files are still compared whole.

**Independent review** (two Opus readers at high effort: A `ph` and `pa`; B the other prefixes, the plural families, the three maps
and the tests). Both: merge after fixes. Fixed after them:
- *Blockers:* «Откалибровать следующее показание цели может только небо в этом же фильтре» (the word order had made the reading
  calibrate the sky); the photon limit is per draw, not a number of draws; the instructor option adds a distance *line of the
  report* («строку расстояния», not a line on the plot); the archive member's provenance names the archive's SHA-256
  («Из {member} в архиве с SHA-256 {sha256}»).
- *VIREO is feminine* (RU0's rule) in eight strings that had «остановился бы», «записал бы», «он», «его рамка».
- *Meaning:* «список объектов поля» (not a list of fields); «если в записи скорость уже есть, она сохраняется»; «уровень может
  оказаться по любую сторону от 80»; the expected total of the longest series; the combined source + sky mean per callback; «явно
  включите фотометр»; «Повторите загрузку выбранной модели»; SVG and Number arithmetic as two things; confirmed comparisons; a
  hot-list entry «Запись {star} списка объектов» / «Строка {number} списка объектов»; «Ваше значение» for the transcription column.
- *One form:* the terms above; «режим «Небо» / «Объект»»; «общий запрет»; «правило» for policy (RU3); «начальное значение
  генератора»; «эталоны» for the printed standards; «Ручной B−V», «Ручная видимая V»; lime and fuchsia «салатовым», «фуксией».

## Russian: the radio telescope, and the hold keys under a non-Latin layout (RU5)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU4 ∥ RU5" and §1.2 #6 in the research root, following FR5, ID5 and HI5. RU5 was
cut from main beside RU4 and merged with it before its hand-off. Russian stays behind `?lang=ru`. The translations are web
adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR5's 646 keys, every key of `radio`, `radioRoute`, `radioAccess`, `radioReceiver`, `radioSimOptions` and
  `pulsarParameters`; 645 were new (`radioRoute.nav` came with RU0). `ru.test.ts` pins them (`RU5_KEYS`).
- **Maps, complete:** `radioAntenna` (324 strings), `radioR1`, `radioRecordSave` and `radioFinishSave`.
- **VIREO's direction buttons** N and S read «С» and «Ю» (`originals.json`, added to `RU_ONLY_ORIGINALS` as Hindi's are); E and W
  were «В» and «З» since RU1. In Russian the longitude letters and the button letters are the same, so no `direction|` context is
  needed (Indonesian's case).
- **Still English until RU6:** the receiver's Record Save dialog also renders texts of the `pulsar` map; `radio.spec.ts` lists them
  as `TODO_RU6_PULSAR_KEYS` (allowed only in `ru`, only on that step), to remove when RU6 merges. RU6 must label Pulsar Analysis'
  buttons exactly «Сохранить запись как .PLR (с проверкой)…» and «Скачать запись как .PLR…», which RU5's texts already quote.
- **Identical to English** (`identical.json`, 14 rows with reasons): parameter-only formats, the dome value 2, SHA-256, «{ut} UT»,
  «{id}» in « » and the hot-list notices that only carry VIREO's vireo()-translated text. `lint-allowed.json`: the question box
  title «Подтверждение» (VIREO's Confirmation caption; «Подтвердить» is the button).

**How it was translated.** Four Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches (174 keys of `radio`, `radioSimOptions` and `pulsarParameters`; 130 of
`radioRoute`; 141 of `radioAccess`; 200 of `radioReceiver`) and two map batches (`radioAntenna`; the three small maps), from the
English with the reviewed French and Spanish as meaning context, under RU0–RU3's briefs extended with the radio terms below; then
fix mode, the ru lint and a quoted-name check.

**Decisions.**
- **Terms:** slew «наведение»; Record «Записать»; a recording or capture «запись» («запись приёмника» where a table row could be
  meant), Record armed «запись взведена», the Record dialog «диалог «Записать»», the Record indicator «индикатор «Записать»»;
  a timer tick «такт» (RU1's and RU2's word; the brief's «тик» was withdrawn); the radio site «радиоплощадка» (the glossary's),
  radio UT «радио-UT» (neuter, as «UT»), radio longitude «долгота радиоплощадки»; beam «диаграмма направленности» (VIREO's
  captions keep their own wording); altitude limit «предел по высоте», horizon limit «предел горизонта»; rate base «базовая
  скорость наведения»; audition «прослушивание»; this browser version «эта версия для браузера» (never «версия браузера», which
  would name the visitor's browser); recorded original «записанный исходный»; identification «отождествление»; the radio control
  panel «панель управления радиотелескопом»; the radio route «страница радиотелескопа».
- **VIREO's Preferences menu item is «Настройки»**, its Options dialog «Параметры»: the Edit Mode original in `originals.json`
  now says «пункт «Настройки»» too (it had «Параметры»). The section heading is «Конфигурация радио».
- **Quoted names in the nominative** after a generic noun: «в окне «Анализ пульсаров»», «в разделе «Входные данные сценария»»,
  «кнопка «Начать работу по пульсарам»» (for the English shorthand "Start"), «кнопка «Запустить радиочасы»» (not «Пуск», which is
  VIREO's Go), «пункт «Задать координаты» отключён».
- **The glossary row "pulsar exercise"** now reads «по пульсарам», since «Начать работу по пульсарам» declines «работа».

**The hold keys follow the physical key under a non-Latin layout** (user decision 2026-11-19 Q3; plan §1.2 #6). Under the Russian
layout the E, N, S and W keys type у, т, ы, ц, which started no hold. `browserVirtualKey` (src/core/radioKeyboard.ts) now reads
`code` (KeyA…KeyZ) when `key` is a single letter or script-specific mark of a non-Latin script at the standard location (not
Latin, Common or Inherited, so AZERTY's é, Spanish ñ, Turkish ı, µ and a generic combining accent never reach it; Hindi InScript's
vowel signs ा, े, ै on E, S, W do, as an independent reader found). Latin layouts behave exactly as before; modifiers, composition
and releases are handled as for Latin letters. A key that types punctuation under its layout (Hebrew's W types ') still starts no
hold. Tests: Vitest on the mapping (Cyrillic, Greek, Devanagari; the Latin and Common cases refused), and a Chromium CDP app test in
`tests/radio-keyboard-app.spec.ts` holding N and E with Cyrillic `key` values and checking a Cyrillic letter on a non-letter key
does nothing. `antenna.manual.keys` drops "non-Latin letters" from its browser limits in English, Spanish, Portuguese, French,
Indonesian, Hindi and Russian, and docs/RADIO-ANTENNA.md describes the branch.

**One fix for every language:** the Pulsar Parameters "not found" notice passed VIREO's English field caption (J2000 Name, B1950
Name) into the message; it now passes it through vireo(), as the Find dialog's own radio buttons do (English is unchanged).

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts`: the four radio maps in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`, `RU5_KEYS` (646), N and S pinned.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains `radio`; `RADIO_MAPS.ru`. `radio.spec.ts` runs in Russian.
- `tests/i18n/walks/radio.ts`: `NOT_SAME_IN.ru` (Radio, Radio..., N, S, E, No, no, Min, Hrs, Bytes, which Russian translates), so an
  English letter left on a direction button fails the walk.
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the ES8a radio family gains `ru`, with the route's Russian `h1`.
- `tests/i18n/areas.spec.ts`: Russian's pinned areas gain `pulsar` and `radio`.
- At 320 px the receiver's Data Properties table overflowed with «Относительное усиление»; the column header is «Отн. усиление».

**Independent review** (two Opus readers at high effort: A `radio`, `radioSimOptions`, `pulsarParameters`, `radioRoute` and the
antenna map; B `radioAccess`, `radioReceiver`, the three small maps, the Q3 code and the tests). Both: merge after fixes. Fixed:
- *Blockers (A):* Preferences «Настройки» in two strings that said «Параметры»; «Конфигурация радио» (the heading) for the section;
  «Запустить радиочасы» for the radio clock's Start; a quote closed inside VIREO's «Задать дату и время наблюдения, ID»; «Байт
  запрошенного телескопа»; the failed Find returned to the shared cursor, which did not move; "nonfinite" «не конечное число».
- *Code:* the non-Latin branch also accepts a script-specific mark (Hindi InScript), and the notice's field caption goes through
  vireo().
- *One form:* the terms above, «помечает» for marking a recording as written, «веб-примечание», «в веб-версии», «завершение
  записи» for the finish procedure (not «окончание»).

## Japanese: E01 Hubble and E02 large-scale structure (JA2)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §6.2 in the research root, with FR2 and HI2 as its models. Japanese stays behind `?lang=ja`.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 786 keys of FR2's E01/E02 prefixes (continuity, lab, lssLab, hubble, velocity, ownResults, spectrum,
  spectrometer, the galaxy, sky and star exposures, ssp, exposureIO, drift, skyProfiles, fieldSpec, lss, wedge) and `classLink.open`;
  `ja.json` holds 1,499 keys. The maps `engineView`, `spectralRange` and `wedge` are complete (Japanese has one plural form, so the
  wedge's counts are template literals, «{n}件の記録»). Five Sonnet catalog batches and one map batch from the English (French and
  Spanish as meaning only), each given the Japanese of the VIREO names its English names (from `originals.json`) and the labels the
  earlier batches had decided; then the implementer's lint and consistency pass (a script compared every short English label with the
  sentences that name it) and two independent Opus readers (reader A the E01 analyser, Hubble and guide keys; reader B the exposures,
  the field spectrometer, E02, the maps and the tests): 126 review fixes, one a blocker (the analyser's "save derivative" read as a
  mathematical derivative, 導関数).
- **Decisions** (the brief records them; JA3 onwards keeps them):
  - wedge diagram «扇形図» (VIREO's LSS Wedge Plot «LSS扇形図», as JA1), analyser «解析ツール», acquire «取得», recession velocity
    «後退速度», fit «当てはめ», recipe «レシピ».
  - **One form each:** a light source «天体» (never ソース), a cited source «出典», raw counts «未加工カウント», segment «区間» (live
    «実行中の区間»), stellar «恒星», archived «アーカイブした», workspace «作業スペース», a control «操作», Telescope view «望遠鏡ビュー»
    (JA1's), activate «〜を有効化», Recenter «中心を合わせる», VIREO's Default button «既定» (its original), VIREO's Sp.Type and Remarks
    as its Observational Data dialog shows them, «スペクトル型» and «備考».
  - VIREO's buttons named in prose are quoted 「OK」, 「キャンセル」, 「既定」; a label that carries a number is quoted with «（番号）» in
    its place («「測定（番号）の取得を確認」»).
  - The wedge table's RA, Dec and Object ID columns read «赤経», «赤緯», «天体ID», as JA1's originals; #, V (km/s), Z, the units and
    binary32 formats stay (identical.json). The capacity reads «1万件».
- **Tests.** `ja.test.ts` pins every key of the E01/E02 prefixes and the three maps complete; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains e01 and e02
  with `E01_E02_MAPS.ja`, so `e01.spec.ts` and `e02.spec.ts` run in Japanese (`SAME_IN.ja`: OK, (km/sec) and «v · km/s · float32» in
  E01, OK in E02; the telescope-view word list gains «望遠鏡 ·»; the field-selector check reads a full-width bracketed count
  «（{rows}個の銀河）»); the seven E01/E02 export families gain `ja`; `areas.spec.ts` pins Japanese's areas (classification, exposure,
  fits, lss, photometer); `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts` runs every exercise walk Japanese has done (E01 and E02 here) under real
  Japanese and the CJK test fonts at 320 and 1440 px, with kana on screen, layoutProblems() and verticalInkProblems() after each step.
- **Seen, not changed here:** the LSS survey (`src/LssSurvey.tsx`) and Multi-Spec (`src/multispec/MultiSpecInstrument.tsx`) join reason
  fragments with an ASCII «; », which reads as Latin punctuation between Japanese fragments; a per-language list separator would be a
  shared code change.

## Japanese: E03 spectral classification (JA3)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §6.2 in the research root, with FR3 and HI3 as its models. JA3 is stacked on JA2 (JA2's
`continuity.restoreExposure` and `ssp.header` are E03's too). Japanese stays behind `?lang=ja`.

- **Catalog:** the 741 E03 keys not yet in `ja.json` (772 with JA1's guide keys and JA2's two shared keys: the classification module,
  classification+cluster, the observation notebook, the DAT/TXT/CSV inspectors and editors, and the Program List); `ja.json` holds
  2,240 keys. Five Sonnet batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning only), translated while JA2 was reviewed, with JA2's
  brief and each batch's decided labels; then the implementer's lint and consistency pass and two independent Opus readers (reader A
  the classifier, cursors, widths, photos and traces; reader B the notebook, inspectors, Program List and the tests): 160 review fixes,
  three of them blockers ("grade" read as 等級, the magnitude; "signed SSP" read as digitally signed, 署名付き; a derived product read
  as a calculus derivative, 導関数, the same slip JA2's reader found), and "owned bytes" (the bytes a view holds) read as ownership.
- **Decisions:**
  - **The notebook's results and VIREO's:** OpenVireo's observation notebook keeps «観測ノートの結果» (its buttons «結果{id}を編集»,
    «結果{id}を削除», «観測ノートに結果を記録»), so it never reads as VIREO's Observational Results list «観測結果» (JA1's original),
    which E03 also names.
  - **One form each:** shoulder «肩部» (the glossary's), a data source (an atlas standard, the unknown, an imported spectrum)
    «元データ» and its identity «元データの識別情報», a light source «天体» (as JA2), endpoint grid «端点グリッド», combined view and
    VIREO-style Combined photo and trace «統合表示» («写真とトレースの統合表示»), printed coordinates «記載座標», pan «パン», sampled
    «サンプリングした», display maximum «表示最大値», canonical (a float encoding) «標準形», type code «型コード», a DAT record
    «レコード», workspace «作業スペース», student manual «学生用マニュアル», Practice object «練習天体», Previous/Next standard
    «前の標準星／次の標準星» (JA1's guide keys now quote them so), recovery copy «復旧用コピー».
  - VIREO's DAT/TXT field name «MJD(Days)» stays byte-exact beside Japanese: `ja.test.ts` lists it in `ASCII_FIELD_NAMES`, which the
    fixer keeps (as a symbol's own brackets), and lint-allowed.json lets its two strings keep the ASCII brackets.
- **Tests.** `ja.test.ts` pins the 772 E03 keys; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains e03, so `e03.spec.ts` runs in Japanese and the real-Japanese
  CJK walk (`tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts`) runs the E03 walk at 320 and 1440 px; the E03 export family gains `ja` (`JA_E03` for the
  notebook's Edit button and the classifier's heading, as Russian's); `areas.spec.ts` pins Japanese's cluster area (the shared
  classification+cluster module).
