Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §6.2 in the research root, with FR4, HI4 and RU4 as its models. Japanese stays behind
`?lang=ja`.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 587 E04/E05 keys not yet in `ja.json` (651 with JA1's guide keys: `ph`, `pa`, `pleiadesLab`, `nc`, `db`,
  `ce`, `clusterLab`, `ownHr`, `obsHr`), so `ja.json` holds 2,827 keys; the `clusterHr` and `clusterData` maps, and `variableStars`
  completed (JA0 had only its header name). Four Sonnet catalog batches and one map batch from the English (French and Spanish as
  meaning only), the implementer's lint and consistency pass, then two independent Opus readers (reader A the photometer, the Pleiades
  analysis and guide; reader B the cluster H-R tools, the reference datasets, the observation workspace, the three maps and the tests):
  78 review fixes, three blockers (the aperture note dropped "with the 0.4 m"; a limit of 1,000,000 photons per draw read as a million
  draws; "drawn as well" on the chart read as a Poisson draw).
- **Decisions:** the glossary's turn-off «転向点» and absolute magnitude «絶対等級» (JA0) stand; light-year «光年» (the Pleiades distance
  «410光年»); a cluster fit accepts JA2's «当てはめ» (glossary); Photometry target «測光目標» and Actual telescope pointing
  «望遠鏡の実際の指向», the forms JA1's guide keys quote; **one form each:** progress «進捗», context «コンテキスト», bench «作業台»,
  nominal «公称», admitted «受け入れた», sample «標本», membership «メンバーシップ», a DAT record «レコード» (as JA3), Keep your work
  «作業内容を保存», workspace «作業スペース», restore «復元» (Restore all excluded points «除外した点をすべて復元»), color–magnitude
  diagram «色等級図», Data from Observations «観測からのデータ»; the Pleiades source title stays English in 『』 with a Japanese gloss.
  The button «自分の観測記録をプロット» is quoted as it reads where en.json's `ownHr.adaptation` names it "Plot my photometer records"
  (HI4 saw the same mismatch).
- **Tests.** `ja.test.ts` pins the 651 keys and the three maps complete; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains e04 and e05 with `E04_E05_MAPS.ja`, so
  `e04.spec.ts` and `e05.spec.ts` run in Japanese (`SAME_IN.ja`: OK and (km/sec) in both, GCVS ID in E05); the E04 and E05 export
  families gain `ja` (`JA_E04` throws on a missing key, as Russian's); lint data: identical.json gains the symbol-only labels (B−V,
  E(B−V), V − Mv, #, SHA-256…), lint-allowed.json lets "count toward", "visual guides" and the integration period pass the glossary.

## Japanese: the radio telescope (JA5)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §6.2 in the research root, with FR5 and HI5 as its models. JA5 is stacked on JA4. Japanese
stays behind `?lang=ja`.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 645 radio keys not yet in `ja.json` (646 with JA1's: `radio`, `radioRoute`, `radioAccess`, `radioReceiver`,
  `radioSimOptions`, `pulsarParameters`), so `ja.json` holds 3,472 keys, and the four radio maps `radioAntenna` (324 keys),
  `radioFinishSave`, `radioRecordSave` and `radioR1`. Five Sonnet catalog batches and two map batches (translated while JA4 was reviewed),
  the implementer's lint and consistency pass, then two independent Opus readers (reader A the route, access, options and Pulsar
  Parameters with the three small maps; reader B the receiver, the antenna map and the tests): 101 review fixes, three blockers (an
  opt-in condition read backwards, and the dish written 鏡, a mirror, in two numeric-option texts).
- **Decisions:**
  - **The direction buttons** read 北, 南, 東, 西 (JA1's originals); the keys stay E, N, S, W, and the keyboard note names each key's
    button («Eキー、Nキー、Sキー、Wキー（それぞれ「東」「北」「南」「西」のボタン）», keypad digits too), FR5's correction from the start.
  - **Three words:** the receiver's capture is VIREO's Record «記録», a recording (the noun) «受信データ», Save «保存» (never 録音). The
    PLR buttons are «受信データを.PLRで保存（検証済み）…» and «受信データを.PLRでダウンロード…» (`radioRecordSave`); JA6's pulsar map
    quotes them so.
  - The JA0 glossary's radio names stand: the page «電波望遠鏡の制御», radio clock «電波の時計», Scenario inputs «シナリオ入力», Use
    compiled defaults «コンパイル済みの既定値を使用», Reset radio site «電波観測地をリセット», Manual marks «手動マーク»; VIREO's Radio
    Telescope Control Panel window keeps its original «電波望遠鏡コントロールパネル» (lint-allowed.json).
  - **One form each:** dish «アンテナ» (never 鏡), tick «ティック», nominal «公称», compiled (a VIREO default) «組み込み», sweep «スイープ»,
    audition «試聴», Base Slew Rate «基準移動速度» and Derived Quantities «導出量» (originals), Reveal «正体を明かす» and Add Current Item
    to List «現在の項目をリストに追加» (JA1's), this browser version «このブラウザー版», Research notes «調査メモ» (the shell's), Delphi's
    True/False flags kept as written (identical.json).
- **Tests.** `ja.test.ts` pins the 646 keys and the four maps; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains radio, so `radio.spec.ts` runs in Japanese,
  with `TODO_JA6_PULSAR_KEYS` (as ID5's `TODO_ID6`) for the Record Save dialog's pulsar-map texts until JA6, `SAME_IN.ja` (OK, PDot
  (S/S):) and a mark-label pattern that accepts a full-width bracket; `RADIO_MAPS.ja`; the radio export family gains `ja`;
  `areas.spec.ts` pins Japanese's pulsar and radio areas; the real-Japanese CJK walk runs the radio walk at 320 and 1440 px. Under the
  local DejaVu-only fonts the 320 px radio layout test sees tofu crossing a fieldset; under the CJK test fonts it passes.

## Simplified Chinese: the observatory and VIREO's names (ZH1)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §5.3 and §6.2 "ZH1" in the research root, following FR1 and JA1. Simplified Chinese stays
behind `?lang=zh-Hans`; its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** FR1's observatory keys (the instruments' prefixes and the Hubble hot-list reasons) and the 104 guide keys the
  observatory files render (read from `FR1_GUIDE_KEYS`): 555 keys, about 8,160 English words. `sky.searchMatches.one` and `.other`
  carry the same Chinese (one plural form).
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`, `observationMetadata`, `camera`
  and `polish` (partial since ZH0).
- **All of VIREO's originals at once:** the 764 texts (Spanish's 760 plus `General` «常规» and the compass letters `E` «东», `N` «北»,
  `S` «南»; `W` is «西»). 756 are in `originals.json`; eight are kept with an `identical.json` reason (NVO, Project CLEA, HH:MM:SS,
  YYYY/MM/DD, V - Mv =, PDot (S/S):, PEpoch (MJD):, (km/sec)); ZH0's two kept names left `originals.json`, so a kept original is
  never also translated. OK is «确定» (a word with a Chinese form is never kept).

**How it was translated.** Ten Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Spanish as meaning context only): three catalog
batches (185 each), three originals batches (250, 250, 249) and four map batches (observatory; camera; the six small maps; polish),
each with a self-check script (placeholders, ASCII marks next to Han, Han–Latin spaces, marks counts, line breaks, Traditional or
out-of-GB2312 characters). The implementer merged them with one form per term, ran fix mode and the strict lint, then two Opus
readers reviewed independently (below).

**Decisions.**
- *Where the brief and ZH0's glossary disagreed, the reviewed glossary won:* site «站点» (站点信息, 站点参数, 站点名称), Finder Mag
  Limit «寻星镜星等极限» and every VIREO magnitude-limit name «星等极限» (prose «极限星等» stays the term for limiting magnitude),
  VIREO's Preferences «偏好设置», fallback «后备», VIREO's IR Camera «IR相机» (prose may say 红外相机).
- *One form per term:* Base «基准» (the glossary's Base Slew Rate became «基准转动速度»), Multi-Spec «多目标光谱仪» (the glossary's
  Close Multi-Spec became «关闭多目标光谱仪»; the instrument's title keeps «· Multi-Spec»), recovered «复原» (never 恢复, which is
  Restore), VIREO's original «原版» (the glossary's Original hot lists became «原版目标列表»; 原始 stays for raw bytes and counts),
  programmed slew «程序化转动», scenario «场景», calibration «定标», admitted «已纳入», checked domain «已检查的范围», uncertified
  «未经认证», shared «共用», laboratory table number «实验台编号», target «目标», pointing «指向», Point target «将望远镜指向目标».
- *Units in VIREO's names:* right-ascension hours «时» (赤经（时）), and Site Information's time-zone unit Hour and Hours both «时», so
  a line never mixes 小时 and 时 (VIREO's `Hours` is one text for both; Japanese has the same split).
- *Quoted VIREO messages* read as their Chinese originals («（未记录）», «（屏幕图像打印在下一页）», «视场中未找到恒星！»); messages that are
  not originals are translated («保存选项更改？», «无光谱»), with VIREO's English in brackets only where a web text names it.
- *Pieces VIREO joins with a value* read in Chinese order: «星团» + name + «中的所有恒星已放入目标列表», «已保存的观测文件“» + name +
  «”已载入。», «选择此标题下的主题：» before the heading, «米射电望远镜» after the number. Known compromises, as Japanese's: the two
  straight-quote pieces (`" Not Found.`, «…当前读数："», `quotes` allowances), and `Object: (` «天体：(» which VIREO closes with an
  ASCII `)` (`ASCII_BRACKET_PIECES` in `zh-hans.test.ts` and a `typography` allowance). The camera card's catalog bands keep VIREO's
  ASCII colon («NF: NOMAD R»).

**Lint changes (shared with Traditional Chinese).** Both Chinese profiles gain a `typographyCheck` (`src/i18n/lint/chinese.ts`), as
`japanese.ts` is Japanese's: what Front JA0's CJK punctuation fixer would still change in the variant's style, and each Han
character outside the variant's standard set, built on first use from the runtime's decoders as `scripts/han-variants.mjs` builds
them: GB2312's 6,763 hanzi for zh-Hans, Big5's 13,060 for zh-Hant. It catches the Japanese forms (図 対 観…) and rare variants a
model leaves; a real character outside the set takes a `lint-allowed.json` entry with a reason. `fixtures.ts` gains one failing case
per profile. The glossary's guide row no longer fires on "Guide Star Catalog" or a guide star, and its pulsar row not on the file
name Pulsars.HTL.

**Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts`: fix mode (`env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx vitest run src/i18n/zh-hans/zh-hans.test.ts`,
refused under CI), the observatory and guide keys present, every VIREO original translated or kept (never both; 北 南 东 西 常规),
fragments with no edge space and every `\r\n` kept, the nine maps complete, the fixer a no-op, the GB2312 and Big5 set sizes, the
lint clean. `vireoSites.test.ts` strict for zh-Hans. `languageText.ts`: `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}`
with the 16 maps; `walks/observatory.ts` `SAME_IN['zh-Hans']` (CCD, IR, VIREO's formats and formula labels, the catalog bands),
`NOT_SAME_IN['zh-Hans']` (General, No, Min, E, N, S, Angstroms) and «望远镜 ·» in the Multi-Spec walk. `exports-identity.spec.ts`:
the hot list, options file and Field Information families gain `zh-Hans`. `areas.spec.ts`: Simplified Chinese's areas with text
are `classification`, `fits` and `photometer`. `dynamic-key-sites.json` gains the two zh-Hans map heads.

**Size.** The zh-Hans core chunk is 60.7 KB gzip (166.2 KB raw) after ZH1, against Japanese's 66.4 KB at JA1; the four chunks hold
64.2 KB in all. `budgetFactor` 1.4 stays (warning at 168 KB) until ZH-HELP measures the Unicode help set.

**Review** (independent, two Opus readers: A the 555 catalog keys and nine maps; B the 756 originals, the identical and allowance
entries, the glossary, the new lint and the tests). Fixed:
- *Reader A, 89 strings:* meaning (fixed-in-web is «固定不变», not corrected; the target dropdown is «选择天体», not the hot list;
  the IR note's lost "live here"; a run-on sentence), the one-form list above, quoted control names equal to their labels
  («指向所选HTL项», «使用此望远镜», «应用显示调色板», «保存探究»), 请 dropped from plain instructions, no stacked or ASCII glosses.
- *Reader B, 20 originals:* «仅在热电制冷时适用», «以分钟为单位输入曝光时间（可带小数）», «与当地时间之差», «转仪钟» (Clock Drive),
  «全可动» and «仅中天观测» (telescope types), «包含R和I波段», «芯片制冷», the CSV-extension message, the cluster pieces' order, and
  «设置日期/时间、ID»; the guide glossary row and the lint fixtures.
- *Implementer, after both:* ZH0's tour now says «选择一个天体，并让望远镜指向它» (it read "point at the telescope") and ZH0's home text
  uses «复原».

**Names later fronts must keep.** ZH1's guide keys quote E03 and E04 controls that ZH3 and ZH4 translate: «分类来源», «原版练习库»,
«练习天体», «按程序列表练习», «上一个», «下一个», «标准星 − 练习天体», «核对分类», «保存分类», «重试载入来源», «保存完整探究», «恢复完整探究»;
«激活昴星团光度计» (short «激活»), «开始», «测光目标», «望远镜实际指向», «望远镜就绪», «天空位置», «光度计滤光片», «添加笔记本条目»,
«计算B−V», «实验颜色–星等分析», «孔径大小»; and ZH1's own «用光度计测量», «指向所选HTL项», «载入实验列表（PLEIADES.HTL）», «原版目标列表»,
«选择天体», «将望远镜指向目标», «指向坐标», «输入坐标», «移动望远镜», «设置坐标», «限制转动». Those fronts use these forms or update the
guide keys with them.

## Japanese: E06 pulsars and E08 Object X (JA6)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §6.2 "JA6 ∥ JA-HELP" in the research root, with FR6 as its model. JA6 is stacked on JA5.
Japanese stays behind `?lang=ja`; files the site writes stay English, and E08's object stays hidden until the reveal in Japanese too.

- **Catalog:** the 134 keys left (`pulsarLab.*`, `plr.*`, `fits.*`; 137 with JA0's): `ja.json` now holds every en.json key (3,606).
  **Maps, complete:** `pulsar` (443 strings), `pulsarSavedRows` (83), `pulsarLoadSave` (25), `objectXLab` (completing JA0's menu
  names), `objectXGuide`, `objectXMeasure` and `objectXSession`. The three flat pulsar maps went through the catalog batch pipeline as
  `map:<map>:<key>` items and were assembled into their files in the French key order; the Object X maps were written as files. Five
  Sonnet batches and one map batch, the implementer's lint pass, then two independent Opus readers (reader A the pulsar maps; reader
  B the Object X maps, the catalog keys and the tests): 61 review fixes, one blocker (the .PLR export's start-time precision dropped).
- **Decisions:** the verdict needs no article, «恒星です。», «正解です。小惑星です。» (`verdict` keeps the English map's three
  parameters); no string names or describes a target before the reveal; the pulsar map's PLR buttons read as JA5 fixed them and the
  receiver strings quote them, so `TODO_JA6` is gone from `tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts`; a receiver recording «受信機の受信データ» where
  the English says receiver; **one form each:** vertical cursor «縦カーソル», display factor «表示倍率», astrometry «位置天文学», Review
  «見直し», Choose file and save… «ファイルを選んで保存…». Kept as written: pulsar names, file names, VIREO's stored «No Data», the
  `Table Number:` label, the reserved-character list's straight quote (lint-allowed.json), ObjectX.HTL codes, FITS cards.
- **Tests.** `ja.test.ts` pins the 137 keys and that no en.json key is missing, and the seven maps complete; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains
  e06 and e08 with `E06_MAPS.ja` and `E08_MAPS.ja`; `e08.spec.ts`'s verdict list gains Japanese and its coordinates line reads the
  colon with no space; `e06.spec.ts`'s exists box quotes «「キャンセル」»; the E06 and E08 export families gain `ja`; the real-Japanese
  CJK walk runs E06 and E08. **The E06 and E08 walk collectors now read every script** (`\p{L}\p{M}*\p{L}`, as HI2 did for E01–E05):
  with `[A-Za-z]{2}` every all-Japanese text was invisible to their checks; the Latin languages collect the same texts as before.

## Russian: E06 pulsars and E08 Object X (RU6)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU6 ∥ RU-HELP" in the research root, with FR6 as its model. Russian stays behind
`?lang=ru`; files the site writes stay English, and E08's object stays hidden until the reveal in Russian too. The translations are
web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** the 134 keys left (`pulsarLab.*`, `plr.*`, `fits.*`; 137 with RU0's): `ru.json` now holds every en.json key.
  `ru.test.ts` pins them (`RU6_KEYS`) and that no en.json key is missing.
- **Maps, complete:** `pulsar` (443 strings), `pulsarSavedRows` (83), `pulsarLoadSave` (25), `objectXLab` (completing RU0's menu
  names), `objectXGuide`, `objectXMeasure` and `objectXSession`.
- **Identical to English** (`identical.json`, 24 more rows with reasons): intentionally empty statuses, SHA-256, CRLF/LF, the
  `{summary} · {session}` join, the ISO date placeholder, «{label}: {ut} UT», the axis and time symbols x, y, UT, the magnitude band V
  and the Asteroid List's column numbers. `lint-allowed.json`: the straight quote among the reserved file-name characters; «№» for
  "#" in the review and saved-rows tables (no Cyrillic letter); five strings that quote full control labels ending in an ellipsis
  where the English names the control in short.

**How it was translated.** Two Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches (82 keys of `pulsarLab`; 52 of `plr` and `fits`) and four map batches
(the `pulsar` map in two halves; `pulsarSavedRows` with `pulsarLoadSave`; the four Object X maps), from the English with the reviewed
French and Spanish as meaning context, under RU0–RU5's briefs extended with this front's terms; then fix mode, the ru lint and a
quoted-name check. Two independent Opus readers: reader A the three pulsar maps and `pulsarLab`/`plr` (11 blockers, 24 should-fix,
15 nits); reader B the Object X maps, `fits`, and the tests (no blockers, 12 should-fix, 17 nits). All were applied.

**Decisions.**
- **Fixed names** that RU5's receiver texts already quote: «Сохранить запись как .PLR (с проверкой)…» and «Скачать запись как
  .PLR…». The reveal button is «Раскрыть объект» (the reveal «раскрытие»); RU5's `radioRoute.date.storedNote` now quotes it so
  («до нажатия «Раскрыть объект»»).
- **Blink is «мигание»** (RU0's glossary row): «Запустить мигание», «Остановить мигание», «сравнить миганием». The plan's
  «блинк-сравнение» was withdrawn.
- **The verdict needs no article**: «Это звезда.», «Ваш ответ верен: это звезда.»; the kinds are nominative («звезда», «галактика»,
  «квазар (QSO)», «пульсар», «астероид»), so `verdict` keeps the English map's three parameters. No string names or describes a
  target before the reveal (both readers checked). The title is «Поиск Объекта X», the exercise name RU1 gave it (`polish`) and the help uses; the list of Object X codes is «список целей»
  («список объектов» is VIREO's Hot List).
- **The review's blockers were quoted names**: the Review dialog is VIREO's «Выбор записи измерения» (its button «Просмотреть
  записанные измерения…»), the Find dialog «Найти запись, содержащую...», the Identify dialog «Отождествить записанный объект»; in
  Pulsar Analysis the record control is «Записать текущие измерения…» («Записать» is the radio telescope's Record button), and the
  native save «Сохранить записанные измерения (.CSV)».
- **One form each:** emptied «очищен» (as the radio save maps), «в этой вкладке», viewer «средство просмотра», the native writer
  «исходная программа записи», pan rate «шаг прокрутки», a receiver's index «индекс», disarm «снять взведение», the recorded
  original «записанный исходный файл» (as `pulsarParameters`), a supplied file «предоставленный файл», the page bar «верхняя панель
  страницы», set the clock «установить», the save file picker «окно выбора файла для сохранения». OpenVireo is feminine, as in
  `appShell` («OpenVireo не сохранила запись в файл»). Counts stay count-neutral («Строк: {count}», «размер файла в байтах составил
  бы {bytes}»).

**Tests.** `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains e06 and e08 with `E06_MAPS.ru` and `E08_MAPS.ru`; `e08.spec.ts`'s verdict list gains Russian
and its coordinates line reads «П. в.» like the Latin «AR», «AD» and «RA»; the E08 walk's leak check knows «Это …» and «Ваш ответ
верен»; `e06.spec.ts`'s exists box quotes «Отмена» in « »; the E06 and E08 export families gain `ru`. `TODO_RU6` is gone from
`tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts` (the Record Save dialog's pulsar texts are now Russian), and with it the last pending-key list there.
`dynamic-key-sites.json` pins the two Object X coordinate template heads, whose «П. в.» has dots.

## Japanese: help (JA-HELP)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md §1.2 #6 and §6.2 "JA6 ∥ JA-HELP" in the research root, with FR-HELP and HI-HELP as its
models and SC7's Unicode help as its ground. JA-HELP is stacked on JA6. Japanese stays behind `?lang=ja`. VIREO's 38 bundled help
files and OpenVireo's own help are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`, their bytes,
SHA-256 and provenance are unchanged, and «原文を表示（英語）» shows VIREO's exact text.

- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/ja/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'ja',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`) in VIREO's own format, Unicode,
  NFC, LF, no tab: every TXT line at 72 display columns or fewer (36 kanji), every title at 65 or fewer; the same LST rows, kinds and
  file tokens (the unterminated CLASSHLP and VarTypeHlp `'begin` rows reproduced; HUBHLP and LSSHLP keep their blank lines; MLTSPHLP and
  SPLSTHLP stay empty; OBJXHLP and TOOLSHLP identical); each section header is the LST title it opens; SPECLNS.LNL equals the original
  and SPECLNS.TXT keeps VIREO's English line headers; the reminders keep the original line count. Not in `LATIN1_HELP`.
- **Registry:** `help:{related:'関連項目'}` in `src/i18n/languages.json`; "Related Topics:" reads «関連項目：», "See also:" «参照：», NOTE
  «注：»; quotes 「」; a list item ends in a noun or «〜こと。» / «〜場合。» (the です/ます check reads it as a sentence).
- **How it was made.** One Sonnet batch translated every LST index first and wrote the title table (206 titles), the only source of
  the section headers and 関連項目 lines that five TXT batches then used; a model cannot count display columns, so the batches wrote one
  line per paragraph and a scratch tool bundled from `src/core/cleaHelp.ts` (`wrapHelpText`, kinsoku) and `textColumns.ts` wrapped
  every line over 72 columns at the paragraph's hanging indent (the plan's pre-wrap step), and a reflow pass absorbs the one-character
  orphan a fixer change could leave after a full line. A seventh batch wrote the two maps. Three independent Opus readers (VIRMNHLP, the
  reminders, every index and the two maps; the radio, pulsar and spectra help; the observatory, photometer, camera, classification,
  measuring, cluster HR, variable-star and wedge help): 103 fixes, one blocker (SPECLNS's «K line = H line (blend) at ~F0», equal
  strengths, read as a blend), four titles renamed in every file («望遠鏡コントロールパネルを開く», «ゼロ年齢主系列を当てはめる»,
  «表計算ソフト», «投影基線を計算»), and VIREO's broken links kept broken (MEASHLP's typo "Measurem Individual Spectral Lines" and its
  "Individual Spectral Features" resolve to no row in English, so their Japanese do not either).
- **Column tables survive the fixer.** VIREO aligns columns with runs of spaces (VARTYPEHLP's type codes); the CJK fixer's
  no-space-beside-Japanese rule collapsed them («RRこと座RR型星»). `ja.test.ts` now fixes help sources with `fixHelp`, which keeps a run
  of two or more spaces between two characters, and the Japanese help lint reads such a gap as a column.
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («翻訳：OpenVireoによるWeb版の翻訳（CLEAの文章ではありません）» · «原文を表示（英語）» · «翻訳を
  表示»; the fold note says what `foldHelpText` does for Japanese, all under the translation's search: full-width and half-width,
  katakana and hiragana, accents and case are not told apart, a kanji's reading is not searched, VIREO's own search in the original view
  ignores only the case of A to Z) and `webHelp`, whose number paragraph reads «画面と入力では小数点に「.」を使い、数字は半角で入力しま
  す。サイトが保存するファイルは、VIREOと同じく英語のままです。»; every control it names is in 「」 with its on-screen label.
- **Tests.** `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts` runs the structure checks for Japanese ("the Japanese help files (Front JA-HELP)") and a
  new block runs the ja profile's typography, register and the glossary's `forbidden` over each paragraph of every Japanese help text
  (wrapped lines join without a space beside Japanese), with no straight `"` in a text file and no full-width digit; `ja.test.ts` pins
  the registry's `help` and the two maps complete; `JAPANESE_TEXT.done` gains help and `HELP_TEXT.ja` its rows (the search types
  «かーそるの縦位置» in hiragana and finds «カーソルの縦位置»), so `help.spec.ts` runs in Japanese; `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`'s
  help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=ja`; the real-Japanese CJK walk runs the help windows.

## Indonesian: release and search pages (ID-REL+SEO)

The plan's ID-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-id.md` §6.2, §2, §4 and the user's Indonesian decisions of 2026-11-19)
after ID0–ID6 and ID-HELP merged (main 06b264b8): the catalog (3,606 keys), all 33 maps, every VIREO original and the 38 help
modules complete (`node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang id`: 3606/3606 keys, 33/33 maps, 742 originals, 0 pending). User decision 5 of
2026-11-09 (AGENTS.md): Indonesian turns on once all of its sub-fronts are in. FR-REL+SEO is the model; the registry made the
release one field and the SEO table one row. AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: id has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: `?lang=en`,
`?lang=es-419`, `?lang=pt-BR`, `?lang=fr` or `?lang=id` exactly (that load only, not stored), then the stored choice, then the
first browser language: `id`, `id-ID`, any `id-*` in any case (`ID-id`), and the withdrawn ISO 639 code `in`, `in-ID` open
Indonesian (plan §2); Malay (`ms`, `ms-MY`, `ms-ID`, `zsm`), Javanese (`jv`), Sundanese (`su`), `en-ID` and a browser whose first
language is another (`['en-US','id-ID']`, `['es-MX','id-ID']`, `['fr-FR','id-ID']`) stay as they were. `?lang=ID`, `?lang=id-ID`,
`?lang=in` are no tag. The selector lists English, Español, Português (Brasil), Français and Bahasa Indonesia on every load, each in
its own `lang`; Hindi, Japanese, Simplified Chinese and Russian previews list those five and their own. A change from Indonesian is
stored. `<html lang="id">`, the title and the failure notices are ID0's.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for the earlier releases: `src/i18n/id/id.test.ts` checks the registry line (`released:true`,
`thousands`, `help`), `createI18n(id,'id')` without fallback, every en.json key, and every map of `MAP_IDS` against its English shape
without `partial`; `check-i18n-dist.mjs` requires marker text in every id module (the `pulsar` and `pulsar+radio` modules by ID0's
ASCII fallback) and no empty area. `infrastructure.test.ts` adds Indonesian to the formatter check: 14 values × 10 option sets print
English's with only the digit-group comma swapped for U+202F («24 000», «31 147», «1 234.5»), `fixed`/`scientific` unchanged, the
export SHA-256 unchanged.

**Tests.** The Indonesian specs open Indonesian by the browser language alone, as FR-REL did for French.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `INDONESIAN_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `contextLocale` gives it `id-ID` (ID0's
  `INDONESIAN_LOCALE`). Every language-parameterised spec now runs Indonesian in an id-ID browser without `?lang=`.
- `areas.spec.ts`: Indonesian leaves the partial-language list (Hindi and Simplified Chinese keep it) and joins the released-language
  loop («in an Indonesian browser › Indonesian (released) fetches its core…», no empty area).
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: five options with their `lang` on every load (`RELEASED_OPTIONS`); ID0's two preview tests became
  ID-REL's detection test (id, id-ID, ID-id, in, in-ID, `['id-ID','es-MX']` → id; `['en-US','id-ID']` → English; `['es-MX','id-ID']` →
  Spanish; `['fr-FR','id-ID']` → French; ms-MY and jv-ID → English; a stored id beats an fr-FR browser and a stored English an id-ID
  one, `?lang=` beats both; a change from Indonesian to French and back is stored with a clean address) and a keyboard test in an
  id-ID browser; the French, Portuguese, Russian and Japanese keyboard walks step over Bahasa Indonesia, and every preview's option
  list includes it.
- `narrow.spec.ts` and `browsers.spec.ts` loop the released languages, so Indonesian joins with no change.
- `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts`: the scripts.css computed-style walk gains id (320 and 1440 px).
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the routes and home loop gains Indonesian (id-ID, its own heading and title, five options);
  id-ID and in-ID browsers open Indonesian and read «31 147»; the 404 page has five languages; the Indonesian SEO pages (below).
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (ID0's test became ID-REL's), `languages.test.ts` (four released; five selector options; the gate rows of
  the planned-languages table move to Hindi `hi`, as FR-REL moved them to `id`), `partialLanguage.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/i18n/
  registry.ts` (id as released).

**The dist check and the 404 page.** The shared 404 page (Front LAUNCH-3, `notFoundPage` in `src/seo/pages.ts`) holds one `<section
lang="…">` block per language with static pages. Indonesian's block links «Buka observatorium», which is also the catalog's
`pleiadesLab.goObservatory`, an Indonesian core marker (16+ characters, unlike French's «Ouvrir l’observatoire», whose catalog says
«Aller à l’observatoire»), so `scripts/check-i18n-dist.mjs` reported `dist/404.html: 1 id core text(s) outside the core chunk`.
The page carries each language's text by design, as `dist/<pagePath>/` does: the check now removes the language's own `<section
lang="<tag>"…>…</section>` block of `dist/404.html` before searching it, so Indonesian text anywhere else on the page (the English
part or another language's block) still fails. A fixture test in `src/i18n/distCheck.test.ts` shows both cases.

**Release text.** The 404 page (`dist/404.html`, generated from the SEO table) has five languages: «Halaman tidak ditemukan … Alamat ini bukan
bagian dari OpenVireo.» under `lang="id"`, linking `/?lang=id`, and «Tidak ditemukan» in the title. README and THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt
carry "Indonesian translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's files and texts are unchanged." with the same uncertainty about the
recorded CLEA License.rtf terms. `src/i18n/id/index.ts`'s comment says it is released and complete.

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front ID-SEO"): an `id` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['id']`,
`ogLocale:'id_ID'`, prefix `/id`, `app:'/?lang=id'`), `src/seo/labs.id.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX.id='/id'`, the `/id/*` header rule and
`public/og-image-id.png`. Written by an Opus writer from OpenVireo's English pages and checked by the SC4 framework with profile `id`
and glossary field `id` (no explicit case needed): «Anda», base-form imperatives («Ukur», «Arahkan», «Catat»), base-form links
(«Buka praktikum», «Buka observatorium»), sentence case, «praktikum» for a lab, the app's own lab names, the glossary's terms,
«Cara kerja» with the line on numbers («24 000», U+202F), and the footer «Teks berbahasa Indonesia ini adalah adaptasi web
OpenVireo, bukan teks CLEA.» with the credit to Project CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). id: 13 chunks, 222.0 KB gzip in all (core 98.3 KB gzip, 317.0 raw), none loaded by
`index.html`, all under the plan's budgets (core 120 warn, sum 300 warn); 38 help chunks, 49.4 KB gzip. The release changes no id
chunk's content. Deploy limits: 2,376 files (nine Indonesian pages and the image are new). No byte-by-byte comparison against a
separate build of main was made for this front; the language-chunk check found every module of every language in its own chunk.

**Verification** (2026-11-20, worktree `claude/i18n-id-rel` with main merged). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 6,616 passed and 11 skipped in
484 files (`moduleEvaluation.test.ts` timed out once under load and passed alone); `npm run test:resources`; `npm run build` with every
check; `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright (Chromium, DejaVu fontconfig, two workers): `language-selector`, `areas`, `seo`,
`shell`, `browsers`, `narrow`, `help`, `page-bar`, `app-shell`, `seo-labs` and `home` 293 of 294 (the Russian preview's keyboard walk
needed one more step over Bahasa Indonesia, fixed), then `language-selector`, `seo` and `areas` 82; every Indonesian test of
`tests/i18n` in an id-ID browser 110; `routes.scripts.ts` and `rtl.scripts.ts` (the scripts config, its test fonts) 55; `npm run
test:pages` on `dist/` 126 (the Indonesian routes, home, «31 147» in id-ID and in-ID browsers, the five-language 404 and the
Indonesian SEO pages among them).

**Review ID-REL+SEO** (independent reviewer, Opus, 2026-11-20). Verdict: merge after fixes. The release, the tests (converted as
FR-REL's, none weakened: the gate rows moved to Hindi, the partial-language case on the qaa-XA fixture) and the 404 rule of the dist
check (narrow: only the language's own block of `dist/404.html`, any other placement still fails) were found correct, and the
Indonesian SEO texts natural and within the limits (titles 49–64, descriptions 146–160). Fixed: this section and the file's opening
paragraph (missing); main merged; the `about` credit no longer reads as Gettysburg College supporting itself («… milik Project CLEA di
Gettysburg College. Project CLEA didukung oleh NSF … dan Gettysburg College.»); the Hubble intro «seberapa cepat alam semesta
mengembang» (the app's `home.hubbleSummary`; «pengembangan» reads as development); «masing-masing dari ketiga belas galaksi»; «tidak
ada jawaban yang diberikan begitu saja» (not «dibocorkan»); the preview image keeps «CLEA VIREO» on one line (a no-break space;
re-rendered twice, the same SHA-256, `docs/SEO.md` updated); three stale test comments and titles. Also «daftar praktikum» for the
exercise list in `labs.id.ts` (the app's word since ID4; the draft had «daftar latihan»). Kept: the ms-MY "no Indonesian module
fetched" check of ID0's preview test, dropped as FR-REL dropped its equivalent.
## Russian: help (RU-HELP)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md §6.2 "RU6 ∥ RU-HELP" in the research root, with HI-HELP as its model and SC7's Unicode
help as its ground. Russian stays behind `?lang=ru`. VIREO's 38 bundled help files and OpenVireo's own help are web adaptations by
OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`, their bytes, SHA-256 and provenance are unchanged, and «Показать
оригинал (на английском)» shows VIREO's exact text.

**What is translated.**
- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/ru/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'ru',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`) in VIREO's own format,
  Unicode Cyrillic text (SC7), NFC, LF, no tab: every TXT line at 72 columns or fewer and every title at 65 or fewer; the same LST
  rows, kinds and file tokens, VIREO's quirks reproduced as the French and Hindi sets do (the unterminated `'begin` row, HUBHLP and
  LSSHLP refused, MLTSPHLP and SPLSTHLP empty); each section header is the row title it opens; SPECLNS.LNL equals the original and
  SPECLNS.TXT keeps VIREO's English line headers. Not in `LATIN1_HELP`.
- **Registry:** `help:{related:'СВЯЗАННЫЕ ТЕМЫ'}` in `src/i18n/languages.json`. VIREO's "Related Topics:" reads «Связанные темы:»,
  "See also:" «См. также:», NOTE «ПРИМЕЧАНИЕ:». The plan proposed «СМ. ТАКЖЕ» as the marker; it was not used, because the viewer
  takes any line containing the marker as the related-topics line and VIREO's "See also:" lines would then have been read as one.
  The cluster H-R tool's `linkNote` (RU4) now writes «Связанные темы» too.
- **Titles shared between files read the same everywhere** (the batches' variants were unified in the LST rows, the headers and
  the related-topic lines): «Пользовательский интерфейс», «Сервис», «Выбрать телескоп», «Открыть купол», «Задать дату и время»,
  «Войти», «Элементы управления телескопом/радиотелескопом/приёмником», «Начать/продолжить накопление» and others.
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («Перевод: веб-адаптация OpenVireo (не текст CLEA)» · «Показать оригинал (на английском)»; the
  fold note says what `foldHelpText` does for Russian: case is folded, ё is read as е, й stays a letter of its own, Latin accents
  are ignored, and «С учётом регистра» compares exactly) and `webHelp`, whose number paragraph carries the plan's sentence (§4.1:
  «На экране в числах используется десятичная точка, а разряды разделяются узким пробелом (например, 1.5 и 24 000)…»).
- **Names:** VIREO's own menus and windows as `originals.json` writes them; controls that OpenVireo draws as web controls (the
  photometer, camera, classification and measuring tools, the radio receiver, the H-R tool, the wedge plot) by their on-screen
  Russian labels («Начать накопление», «Начать экспозицию», «Сохранить снимок (FITS)», «Фотография в оттенках серого», «Записать
  измерение», «Данные из наблюдений», «Поправка (B−V)», «Секунды по горизонтали», «Режим», «Частота»…). VIREO's own errors in its
  text are kept, as in the other sets.

**How it was translated.** Six Sonnet 5.5 batches from the English (French and Hindi modules as models): VIRMNHLP with the two
reminders; PULSRHLP with PLSLSHLP and PULSRLST; PLSANHLP, PULSRANL, SPECHLP and SPECLNS; PHOTOHLP, OBSRVHLP and CAMHLP; CLASSHLP
and MEASHLP; HRANALHLP, VARTYPEHLP, WDGPLTHLP and the eight index-only files. A seventh batch wrote the two maps. The batches wrote
the modules in the worktree and ran the help test on them; the implementer unified the shared titles, re-wrapped the long lines and
ran the Russian typography fixer and lints. (Two batches copied French stubs to run the whole-set test and then deleted them,
which removed another batch's four finished modules; that batch wrote them again. Later language sessions: never stub a shared
folder.)

**Review** (two independent Opus readers): reader A (VIRMNHLP, the reminders, the indexes, the radio and pulsar help, the maps) 4
blockers, 24 should-fix, 15 nits; reader B (the observatory, photometer, camera, classification, measuring, spectra, H-R, variable
star and wedge help) 9 blockers, 15 should-fix, 14 nits. Every blocker was a control name the batches had made up where the
Russian screen shows a different label, or a lost hedge («примерно при F0»). The fixes were applied, each claim checked first.

**Tests.**
- `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts`: the structure checks run for Russian (registry `help`), titled "the Russian help files (Front
  RU-HELP)"; a new block runs the ru profile over each paragraph of every Russian help text (dashes, ё, mixed scripts, U+202F
  thousands, sentence case, Cyrillic units, the «вы» register, the glossary's `forbidden`, and on TXT paragraphs the Russian
  typography fixer's view), with no straight `"` or ” in a TXT file.
- `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts`: the registry's `help`, and `helpViewer` and `webHelp` in `ENGLISH_MAPS` and `COMPLETE_MAPS`.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT.done` gains `help` and `HELP_TEXT.ru` its rows (the search finds «Когда вы наведёте
  курсор» from «КОГДА ВЫ НАВЕДЕТЕ КУРСОР»: case folded, ё read as е); `help.spec.ts` runs in Russian.
  `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=ru`.

**Left for later fronts.** RU6 (E06 and E08) ran in parallel: the help's pulsar-analysis and Object X names follow RU6's maps, to
be rechecked at merge. RU-REL: `released:true`.


## Japanese: release and search pages (JA-REL+SEO)

The plan's JA-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ja.md` §6.2 and §1.2 rows 7–9) after JA0–JA6 and JA-HELP merged: the
catalog (3,606/3,606 keys), all 33 maps, every VIREO original (755 translated, 0 pending) and the 38 help modules complete
(`node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang ja`). User decision 5 of 2026-11-09 (AGENTS.md): Japanese turns on once all of its sub-fronts
are in. FR-REL+SEO and ID-REL+SEO are the model; the registry made the release one field and the SEO table one row. AGENTS.md records the release in
one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: ja has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: an exact
`?lang=` tag (that load only, not stored), then the stored choice, then the first browser language: `ja`, `ja-JP`, `JA-jp`,
`ja-JP-u-ca-japanese`, any `ja-*` in any case open Japanese; `jv` (Javanese), `ryu` (Okinawan), `en-JP` and a browser whose first
language is another (`['en-US','ja-JP']`, `['es-MX','ja-JP']`, `['fr-FR','ja-JP']`) stay as they were. `?lang=JA`, `?lang=ja-JP`
and `?lang=jp` are no tag. The selector lists English, Español, Português (Brasil), Français, Bahasa Indonesia (since ID-REL) and 日本語 (Japanese) on every load, each
in its own `lang`; a preview of a language before its release adds its own option in registry order (Hindi before 日本語,
Simplified Chinese and Russian after it). A change from Japanese is stored. `<html lang="ja">`, the title and the failure
notices are JA0's. A Traditional Chinese browser still opens English (decision of 2026-11-19), not Japanese.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for the other released languages: `src/i18n/ja/ja.test.ts` now checks the registry line
(`released:true`, no `thousands`: «24,000» as English, `help`), `createI18n(ja,'ja')` without fallback, every map of `MAP_IDS`
against its English shape without `partial`, and that no map is missing.

**Tests that move.** `src/i18n/locale.test.ts` (Japanese detection, with a `released:false` copy of the registry keeping the preview
behaviour; the ZH0 test's after-release English list uses `ko-KR` where it had `ja-JP`), `languages.test.ts` (the release row, the
selector lists, and the "English alone shows no selector" check over the unreleased languages), `partialLanguage.test.ts` (the
qaa-XA fixture's selector lists gain 日本語), `pseudoScripts.test.ts` (a `ja` browser opens Japanese, never `qps-cjk`).
`tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: Japanese has no query, every area done; `contextLocale('ja')` is `ja-JP`. `areas.spec.ts` moves Japanese
from its JA0 partial-language test to `RELEASED_AREAS` (after Indonesian, released by ID-REL) (a ja-JP browser fetches its core and exactly each area's modules);
`language-selector.spec.ts` replaces the two JA0 tests with JA-REL ones (browser detection, stored choice, a stored change and back,
and the keyboard in a ja-JP browser) and updates every other test's option list and keyboard steps for the sixth option;
`narrow`, `browsers`, `shell` and `seo` specs pick Japanese up as a released language. `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: every
route in a ja-JP browser (core and its area only, no error), the home with five options, the 404's Japanese section, the
variable-star count «CLEA_VStrs.dat、31,147» and the Japanese search pages.

**Scripts walk.** `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts` (CJK test fonts): the real-Japanese walks open in a ja-JP browser with no query,
and the shell walk of a released script language also visits the 16 routes («ja over every route», plan §6.2) at 320 and 1440 px with
`layoutProblems()` and `verticalInkProblems()`. The scripts.css on/off comparison stays Latin-only (en, es-419, pt-BR, fr, id): scripts.css
is meant to change Japanese.

**Search pages.** `docs/SEO.md`, "Front JA-SEO: Japanese search pages".

## Russian: release and search pages (RU-REL+SEO)

The plan's RU-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-ru.md` §6.2, §2, §4.1, §9 and the user's Russian decisions of
2026-11-19) after RU0–RU6 and RU-HELP merged (main 69abe643): the catalog (3,606 en.json keys and the 26 plural forms Russian adds),
all 33 maps, every VIREO original and the 38 help modules complete (`node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang ru`: 3606/3606 keys,
26/26 plural forms, 33/33 maps, 759 originals, 0 pending). User decision 5 of 2026-11-09 (AGENTS.md): Russian turns on once all of
its sub-fronts are in. ID-REL+SEO is the model (FR-REL+SEO before it); the registry made the release one field and the SEO table
one row. AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: ru has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: an exact
`?lang=` tag (that load only, not stored), then the stored choice, then the first browser language: `ru`, `ru-RU` and any `ru-*`
in any case (`RU-ru`, `ru-UA`, `ru-KZ`, `ru-BY`, `ru-MD`) open Russian (plan §2); Ukrainian (`uk`, `uk-UA`), Belarusian, Kazakh
and the other region languages (`ky`, `uz`, `tg`, `tt`, `ba`, `cv`, `sah`, `os`, `hy`, `ka`, `az`, `ro-MD`, `rue`) stay English,
and so does a browser whose first language is another (`['uk','ru']`, `['en-US','ru-RU']`): Ukrainian and Belarusian browsers
must not open Russian automatically, and only the first preference decides. `?lang=RU` and `?lang=ru-RU` are no tag. The selector
lists English, Español, Português (Brasil), Français, Bahasa Indonesia, 日本語 (Japanese) (since JA-REL) and «Русский (Russian)» on
every load, each in its own `lang`, Russian last in registry order; the Hindi preview lists its own option before 日本語, the
Simplified Chinese preview between 日本語 and Русский.
A change from Russian is stored. `<html lang="ru">`, the title and the failure notices are RU0's.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for the earlier releases: `src/i18n/ru/ru.test.ts` checks the registry line (`released:true`,
`thousands`, `help`), `createI18n(ru,'ru')` without fallback, every en.json key, every key en.json lacks being one of Russian's
plural forms (`pluralFormBase`, which `createI18n` also checks with its base's {params}), and every map of `MAP_IDS` against its
English shape without `partial`; `check-i18n-dist.mjs` requires marker text in every ru module and no empty area.
`infrastructure.test.ts` adds Russian to the formatter check: 14 values × 10 option sets print English's with only the digit-group
comma swapped for U+202F («24 000», «2 400 000», «1 234.5»), `fixed`/`scientific` unchanged, the export SHA-256 unchanged.

**Tests.** The Russian specs open Russian by the browser language alone, as ID-REL did for Indonesian.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `RUSSIAN_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `contextLocale` gives it `ru-RU` (RU0's
  `RUSSIAN_LOCALE`). Every language-parameterised spec now runs Russian in a ru-RU browser without `?lang=`.
- `areas.spec.ts`: RU0's partial-language test is gone (Hindi and Simplified Chinese keep theirs in `PARTIAL`; JA-REL, merged first,
  had moved the extra test from Japanese to Russian, so with both released it is removed; the qaa-XA fixture of
  `partialLanguage.test.ts` and `split.test.ts` keeps the case) and Russian joins the released-language loop («in a Russian browser
  › Russian (released) fetches its core…», no empty area).
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: seven options with their `lang` on every load (`RELEASED_OPTIONS`, `RELEASED_LANGS`; a preview's own
  option is placed in registry order by `withPreview(label, tag)`, which counts the released languages before it);
  RU0's two preview tests became RU-REL's detection test (ru, ru-RU, RU-ru,
  ru-UA, ru-KZ, ru-BY and `['ru-RU','uk-UA']` → ru; `['en-US','ru-RU']` and `['uk','ru']` → English; `['es-MX','ru-RU']` → Spanish;
  `['fr-FR','ru-RU']` → French; uk-UA, be-BY and kk-KZ → English with no Russian module fetched; a stored ru beats an fr-FR browser
  and a stored English a ru-RU one, `?lang=` beats both; a change from Russian to French and back is stored with a clean address)
  and a keyboard test in a ru-RU browser; the French, Portuguese, Indonesian and Japanese keyboard walks reach Русский with End
  and step through 日本語.
- `narrow.spec.ts` and `browsers.spec.ts` loop the released languages, so Russian joins with no change.
- `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts`: Russian is not added to the scripts.css computed-style walk, unlike Indonesian: that walk checks
  that scripts.css changes nothing on a Latin-script page, while it styles a Russian page on purpose (`:lang(ru)`); the real
  Russian shell keeps its layout walk at 320 and 1440 px, no longer a preview walk, and now also visits the 16 routes (JA-REL's
  rule for a released script language), opening in a ru-RU browser with no query (each script walk has its own `contextLocale`
  since JA-REL). In the computed-style walk, every page's selector now holds
  «Русский (Russian)» with `lang="ru"`, which scripts.css gives `overflow-wrap:break-word` on purpose: JA-REL's rule lists such an
  element by its language and requires it to be a selector option, and `ru` joins its `ja|zh|hi` list; every other element, the
  nested es, pt-BR, fr, id and en ones included, is still compared.
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the routes and home loop gains Russian (ru-RU, its own heading and title, seven options);
  ru-RU and ru-UA browsers open Russian and read «31 147», a uk-UA browser opens English and reads «31,147» (plan §6.2); the 404
  page has seven languages; the Russian SEO pages (below).
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (RU0's test became RU-REL's), `languages.test.ts` (six released; seven selector options; the planned
  registry's unreleased languages are Hindi and Simplified Chinese, so `selectorShown` is checked on those two, and the preview
  check uses Simplified Chinese, placed between 日本語 and Русский; a ru-RU
  browser and a stored ru open Russian there, `['uk','ru']` English), `partialLanguage.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts`
  (ru as released).

**Release text.** The 404 page (`dist/404.html`, generated from the SEO table) has seven languages: «Страница не найдена … Этот адрес
не относится к OpenVireo.» under `lang="ru"`, linking «Открыть обсерваторию» to `/?lang=ru#observatory`, and «Не найдена» in the
title; the dist check reads Russian's own `<section lang="ru">` block as its text, as ID-REL made it do. README and
THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt carry "Russian translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's files and texts are unchanged." with the
same uncertainty about the recorded CLEA License.rtf terms. `src/i18n/ru/index.ts`'s comment says it is released and complete.

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front RU-SEO"): a `ru` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['ru']`,
`ogLocale:'ru_RU'`, prefix `/ru`, `app:'/?lang=ru'`), `src/seo/labs.ru.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX.ru='/ru'`, the `/ru/*` header rule and
`public/og-image-ru.png`. Written by the implementer (Opus) from OpenVireo's English pages and checked by the SC4 framework with
profile `ru` and glossary field `ru`: lower-case «вы» and вы-imperatives («Измерьте», «Наведите», «Запишите»), infinitive links
(«Открыть работу», «Открыть обсерваторию»), sentence case, ё, « — » with U+00A0 before it, «лабораторная работа» (or «работа») for
a lab, the app's own lab names from its welcome (home.*; «Объект X», as every other language names the lab after home.objectX,
while the guide that «Открыть работу» opens is «Поиск Объекта X»), the glossary's terms, «Как это работает» with the plan's numbers
sentence («24 000», U+202F), and the footer «Этот текст на русском языке — веб-адаптация OpenVireo, а не текст CLEA.» with the
credit to Project CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF. OpenVireo is feminine («не связана»), as in the app. The only rule
adjustment: as for Spanish, "star cluster" may read «скопление» alone (`english` in the test's ru rules), so the cluster lab keeps
the app's name «Диаграммы Г — Р скоплений».

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). ru: 13 chunks, 293.9 KB gzip in all (core 129.1 KB gzip, 522.1 raw), none loaded
by `index.html`, under the plan's budgets with Russian's factor 1.2 (core 144 warn, sum 360 warn); the largest extra download per
route is the radio telescope's 65.8 KB gzip; 38 help chunks, 64.6 KB gzip. The release changes no ru chunk's content (the catalog,
maps and help are RU0–RU-HELP's). Deploy limits: 2,462 files (nine Russian pages and the image are new), every file under 25 MiB.

**Verification** (2026-11-20, worktree `claude/i18n-ru-rel` at main 69abe643). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 6,794 passed and 11 skipped in
487 files (the first run found two tests that assumed Russian unreleased: the pseudo-locale test, where a ru browser now opens
Russian rather than English, and the plural-family tests, whose partial stand-in `ru` catalogs are now activated as a split core,
as the Spanish and French stand-ins are); `npm run build` with every check; `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright (Chromium,
DejaVu fontconfig, two workers): `language-selector`, `seo` and `areas` 91; every Russian test of `tests/i18n` (narrow and browsers
included) in a ru-RU browser 123; `page-bar`, `project-links` and `shell` 106. `routes.scripts.ts` and `rtl.scripts.ts` (the
scripts config) 57, the failures of a first parallel run rerun with one worker (two were real and are fixed above: the Russian
shell walk needed `?lang=ru`, and the computed-style walk met the Russian option of the selector); `npm run test:pages` on `dist/`
150 (the variable-star scope reads «CLEA_VStrs.dat, записей: 31 147» in Russian, its count-neutral wording, so the smoke expects
that).

**Merge with JA-REL+SEO** (main 0e09d45f, Japanese released first). Every shared release file was resolved add-only in registry
order (Japanese before Russian): README, THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES, `public/_headers`, `src/seo/*`, the registry fixture and the language,
locale, selector, SEO, areas and pages-smoke tests; seven languages, 63 search pages, nine alternates per page.

**Review RU-REL+SEO** (independent reviewer, Opus, 2026-11-20). Verdict: no blocker. Detection (ru and ru-* open Russian; uk, be,
kk and the other region languages stay English), the selector order, hreflang, canonical, og:locale and the sitemap on all 54 pages,
`/ru/*` no-cache, the 404 page, labs.ru.ts's shape and the docs were found correct; the converted tests (RU0's partial-language case
removed as ID-REL did, Russian in the released loops) were found justified, none weakened; the Russian SEO texts within the limits
(titles 48–63, descriptions 149–160). Fixed: the scripts.css computed-style walk had dropped every element in another language than
the page's; nested es, pt-BR, fr, id and en elements are compared again (after the merge, through JA-REL's stricter rule with `ru`
added, see Tests); Object X's description no longer reads «по одним координатам, снимкам…» (only the
coordinates are given, not only the images); the home lead reads «воссоздаёт в браузере VIREO», the labs index «получаете собственные
данные», the home description «Объект X и другие», and the pulsars' last step «…периодом, затем сохраните…». Kept: «снимите спектр»
on the search pages beside the app's «получите спектр» (both correct). Title widths were checked in characters, not rendered pixels.

**For the user** (not done by the site). After the deploy: in Google Search Console resubmit `https://openvireo.space/sitemap.xml`
(63 addresses) and use URL Inspection on `https://openvireo.space/ru/` and one Russian lab page (for example `/ru/labs/hubble/`),
then «Request indexing»; the same in Bing Webmaster Tools if wanted. Yandex Webmaster, the main Russian search console, would be a
new external service and needs the user's own decision. Then confirm on the live site that a ru-RU browser opens Russian and a
uk-UA browser English.

## Simplified Chinese: E01 Hubble and E02 large-scale structure (ZH2)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.2 "ZH2" in the research root, with FR2 and JA2 as its models. Simplified Chinese stays
behind `?lang=zh-Hans`; its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 786 keys of FR2's E01/E02 prefixes not yet translated (continuity, lab, lssLab, hubble, velocity,
  ownResults, spectrum, spectrometer, the galaxy, sky and star exposures, ssp, exposureIO, drift, skyProfiles, fieldSpec, lss, wedge)
  and `classLink.open`, 803 with ZH1's guide keys; `zh-hans.json` holds 1,499 keys. The maps `engineView`, `spectralRange` and
  `wedge` are complete (one plural form, so the wedge's counts are template literals with their measure word, «{n}条记录»).
- **How:** four Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches (197, 197, 197, 195 keys) and one map batch from the English (French and Spanish as
  meaning only), each with the self-check script; the implementer merged them, ran fix mode and the strict lint, and checked every
  quoted control name against the label the screen shows (a script lists each “…” that matches no catalog, original or map label).
  Two independent Opus readers then read every string (reader A the E01 analyser, Hubble and guide keys, 394 keys; reader B the
  exposures, the field spectrometer, E02, the three maps and the test wiring, 409 keys): no blocker, 120 fixes, among them a
  positive-count bin read as a positive bin width, saving read as what builds the separate dataset, a scaling clause attached to one
  galaxy instead of every field, and «望远镜头信息» (a telescope header that reads as a telescope lens) → «望远镜文件头».
- **Decisions** (ZH3 onwards keeps them):
  - wedge diagram «楔形图» (VIREO's LSS Wedge Plot «LSS楔形图», ZH1's original), analyser «分析器» and sky background «天空背景» (the
    glossary's, which wins over the batch brief), acquire and acquisition «采集», recession velocity «退行速度», fit «拟合», recipe
    «步骤», summary (of the Hubble fit) «汇总结果», peculiar velocity «本动速度», derivative (a derived data product) «派生数据».
  - **One form each:** source plus sky «天体加天空» (a light source is «天体»; a cited source «出处»), bin «区间», segment «片段»,
    activate «激活» (the galaxy, sky and star buttons alike), commit «提交», recovered «复原» (as ZH1), identification «证认»,
    documented input «有文献依据的输入», Apparent V «视星等（V）», control (a slider) «控件», workspace «工作区», Evidence «依据»,
    the page bar «页面顶栏», galaxy cluster «星系团» (the glossary's cluster row accepts «星团|星系团»; ZH4 checks that no star
    cluster reads «星系团»).
  - VIREO's messages quoted in prose read as their Chinese originals («视场中未找到恒星！»); the web-rule mark is «［W］» next to
    Chinese; the wedge's hour unit stays «h» (the wedge joins it after a space and a number), RA and Dec read «赤经»/«赤纬».
- **Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the 803 E01/E02 keys and the three maps complete; `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains e01 and
  e02 with `E01_E02_MAPS['zh-Hans']`, so `e01.spec.ts` and `e02.spec.ts` run in Chinese (`SAME_IN['zh-Hans']`: VIREO's (km/sec) and
  «v · km/s · float32» in E01; the telescope-view word list gains «望远镜 ·»); the seven E01/E02 export families gain zh-Hans;
  `areas.spec.ts` pins Chinese's areas (classification, exposure, fits, lss, photometer); `tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts` runs
  every exercise walk a CJK language has done under real Japanese and real Simplified Chinese (`REAL_SCRIPT_LANGUAGES`; Japanese's
  test titles are unchanged), with kana or Han on screen, layoutProblems() and verticalInkProblems() after each step at 320 and
  1440 px.
- **Seen, not changed here:** VIREO's message «Access violation … in module ‘CLEA_VEO.exe’» loses its space before ‘ under the CJK
  fixer (Chinese reads ‘ ’ as its own quote marks); the LSS Save Data button renders `{vireo('Save Data')} (.TXT)`
  (`src/LssSurvey.tsx`), so Chinese shows «保存数据 (.TXT)» with a space and ASCII brackets after Han — a per-language suffix would be a
  shared code change; the LSS survey and Multi-Spec join reason fragments with an ASCII «; », as JA2 noted.

## Simplified Chinese: E03 spectral classification (ZH3)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.2 "ZH3" in the research root, with FR3 and JA3 as its models. ZH3 is stacked on ZH2
(ZH2's `continuity.restoreExposure` and `ssp.header` are E03's too). Simplified Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hans`.

- **Catalog:** the 741 E03 keys not yet in `zh-hans.json` (772 with ZH1's guide keys and ZH2's two shared keys: the classification
  module, classification+cluster, the observation notebook, the DAT/TXT/CSV inspectors and editors, and the Program List);
  `zh-hans.json` holds 2,240 keys. Four Sonnet 5.5 batches (186, 186, 186, 183 keys) from the English (French and Spanish as meaning
  only), translated while ZH2 was implemented, with ZH2's brief; then the implementer's merge, one form per term, fix mode, the strict
  lint and the quoted-label check, and two independent Opus readers (reader A the classifier, cursors, widths, photos and traces, 392
  keys; reader B the notebook, inspectors, Program List and the tests, 378 keys): 101 review fixes, two of them blockers ("library
  practice" read as the atlas, 星图集练习天体; three characters of the current JD prepended to the field read as prepended before the
  JD), and round half to even read as "the nearest even integer", the lower pane's standard read as a lesser one, "owned bytes" read
  as the site's (本站持有) rather than the page's (本页持有).
- **Decisions:**
  - **The notebook's results and VIREO's:** OpenVireo's observation notebook keeps «观测笔记» and its results «观测笔记结果» (buttons
    «编辑观测笔记结果{id}», «删除观测笔记结果{id}»), so it never reads as VIREO's Observational Results list «观测结果» (ZH1's
    original), which E03 also names.
  - **One form each:** atlas «星图集» (Spectral atlas «光谱星图集»), standard «标准星», unknown «未知星», Practice object «练习天体»,
    trace «迹线», shoulder «肩部», printed (points, grid, coordinates) «标注», Inspect «检查», Review (a dialog or check) «核对» (as
    «核对分类»), compare «对比», source identity «来源标识», native (VIREO's own runtime and formats) «原生», recovered «复原»,
    admitted (accepted by a check) «已纳入» (ZH1's), replay «回放», bin «区间», fallback «后备», acquire «采集», source plus sky
    «天体加天空», canonical «规范», type code «类型代码», recovery copy «恢复副本», grade «评级», the JavaScript Number type kept as
    «Number» (Number差值, Number策略), VIREO's Program List «观测程序列表» (originals.json's) and Program-list practice
    «观测程序列表中的练习».
  - VIREO's DAT/TXT field name «MJD(Days)» stays byte-exact beside Chinese: `zh-hans.test.ts` lists it in `ASCII_FIELD_NAMES`, which
    the fixer keeps (as JA3), and lint-allowed.json lets its two strings keep the ASCII brackets.
- **Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the 772 E03 keys; `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains e03, so `e03.spec.ts` runs in Chinese
  and the real-Chinese CJK walk (`tests/scripts/routes.scripts.ts`) runs the E03 walk at 320 and 1440 px; the E03 export family gains
  zh-Hans (`ZH_E03` for the notebook's Edit button and the classifier's heading, as Japanese's); `areas.spec.ts` pins Chinese's
  cluster area (the shared classification+cluster module).

  (French as meaning only), the implementer's lint pass, then two independent Opus readers (reader A the cluster database, reference
  datasets, observation workspace and E05 guide, 294 keys and the clusterData map; reader B the photometer, the Pleiades analysis and
  guide, 293 keys and the clusterHr and variableStars maps): 108 review fixes, five blockers («instructor» options and parameters read
  as 仪器, the instrument, in five photometer rows), and a limit of 1,000,000 photons per draw read as a million draws, the photometer's
  notebook named as VIREO's Observer's Notebook or E03's observation notebook, "before/after the blocking record" read as "before
  blocking", B/V pairs read as star pairs (星对, a double star).
- **Decisions:** the glossary's absolute magnitude «绝对星等» and turn-off «拐点» (the main-sequence turnoff «主序拐点») stand; a
  double-click «双击» now satisfies the click row (glossary `点击|单击|双击`). **One form each:** B/V pair «数据对», admitted
  «已纳入» (ZH1's), acquire «采集», recovered «复原», native «原生» and original «原版» (the forms the merged fronts use 78 and 169
  times), the photometer's notebook «笔记» (Observer's Notebook stays «观测者笔记本», E03's notebook «观测笔记»), calibration «定标»,
  the compact GSC «精简版», seeded session «带种子的会话», replay «回放», correction «修正», Data from Observations «来自观测的数据»
  (glossary) and Recorded Results «已记录的结果» (glossary). Classification Results and Hubble Record (not VIREO captions) are
  described unquoted, «分类结果» and «哈勃记录»; «记录/回看» is originals.json's. VIREO's "(" before an amplitude is said in words
  («左括号之后»), as Japanese does, so no lone bracket reaches the pair check.
- **Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the 651 keys and the three maps complete; `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains e04 and e05 with
  `E04_E05_MAPS['zh-Hans']`, so `e04.spec.ts` and `e05.spec.ts` run in Chinese (`SAME_IN['zh-Hans']`: VIREO's identical title
  «(km/sec)», and «GCVS ID» in E05) and the real-Chinese CJK walk runs E04 and E05 at 320 and 1440 px; the E04 and E05 export families
  gain zh-Hans (`ZH_E04` throws on a missing key, as Japanese's); identical.json gains the symbol-only labels (B−V, E(B−V), V − Mv,

  ZH1's observatory map calls the photometer's notebook «网页笔记本».

## Simplified Chinese: the radio telescope (ZH5)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.2 "ZH5" in the research root, with FR5 and JA5 as its models. ZH5 is stacked on ZH4.
Simplified Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hans`.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 645 radio keys not yet in `zh-hans.json` (646 with ZH1's: `radio`, `radioRoute`, `radioAccess`,
  `radioReceiver`, `radioSimOptions`, `pulsarParameters`), so `zh-hans.json` holds 3,472 keys; the `radioAntenna`, `radioFinishSave`,
  `radioR1` and `radioRecordSave` maps. Five Sonnet 5.5 catalog batches and two map batches from the English (French as meaning
  only), the implementer's consistency pass (scenario «场景», as the glossary's «射电时钟场景»; the Pulsar Analysis buttons
  «将接收数据保存为.PLR（已验证）…» and «将接收数据下载为.PLR…» as ZH6's pulsar map writes them), then two independent Opus readers
  (reader A the access options, control panel and scenario inputs, 258 keys and the radioAntenna map; reader B Pulsar Parameters, the
  receiver, the route, clock and date, 387 keys and the three small maps): 110 review fixes, seven blockers: with the transit option on
  slews change Declination only (read as "slews change Declination only when transit is on", Japanese's misreading too); "until
  radio telescope access completes" means until access is granted; a slew-rate limit at either end (read as the top only); VIREO's one
  item "Set Observation Date/Time, ID" split in two; the clock's Start «启动射电时钟» and VIREO's Add Channel «添加通道» quoted as the
  exercise's Start and a bare Add; the 1,000,000 Poisson limit put on the source flux instead of the noise draw's mean.
- **Decisions:** the glossary's names stand: «使用编译的默认值» (the button reads «使用编译的默认值：直径100，访问选项关闭»),
  «重置射电站点», «显示天体编号», «曲线试听»; **one form each:** received data / a capture «接收数据» (the verb record «记录»), armed /
  disarmed «待命» / «取消待命», recovered «复原» (restore stays «恢复»), tick «节拍», slew-rate index «档位», nominal «标称», shared
  «共用», holds the pulsar data «占用», Align stacks the windows «堆叠», the dish diameter «抛物面天线直径» (VIREO's Antenna Aperture
  stays «天线口径»), site in prose «台址» (VIREO's labels keep originals.json's «站点»), the on-screen N/S/E/W buttons «北/南/东/西»
  with the keyboard keys in Latin letters.
- **Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the 646 keys and the four maps complete; `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains radio with
  `RADIO_MAPS['zh-Hans']`, so `radio.spec.ts` runs in Chinese (`SAME_IN['zh-Hans']`: VIREO's «PDot (S/S):») and the real-Chinese CJK
  walk runs the radio telescope at 320 and 1440 px; `TODO_ZH6` lets only the Record Save dialog's pulsar-map texts read as English until
  ZH6 translates that map (ZH6 removes it); the radio export family gains zh-Hans; `areas.spec.ts` pins Chinese's `pulsar` and `radio`
  areas; identical.json gains the symbol-only rows (UT, h m s, ° ′ ″, SHA-256, a dome-state number) and lint-allowed.json the
  full-width semicolon separator and the two joined placeholders, as Japanese's.

## Simplified Chinese: E06 pulsars and E08 Object X (ZH6)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.2 "ZH6 ∥ ZH-HELP" in the research root, with FR6 and JA6 as its models. ZH6 is stacked
on ZH5. Simplified Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hans`; files the site writes stay English, and E08's object stays hidden until the
reveal in Chinese too.

- **Catalog and maps:** the 134 keys left (`pulsarLab.*`, `plr.*`, `fits.*`; 137 with ZH0's): `zh-hans.json` now holds every en.json
  key (3,606). **Maps, complete:** `pulsar` (443 strings), `pulsarSavedRows`, `pulsarLoadSave`, `objectXLab` (completing ZH0's menu
  names), `objectXGuide`, `objectXMeasure` and `objectXSession`. One Sonnet 5.5 catalog batch and three map batches (the pulsar map,
  the two small pulsar maps, the four Object X maps), translated while ZH4 was checked; the pulsar map defined the .PLR buttons
  «将接收数据保存为.PLR（已验证）…» and «将接收数据下载为.PLR…», which ZH5's receiver strings quote. Two independent Opus readers
  (reader A the three pulsar maps, 556 strings; reader B the catalog and the four Object X maps): 60 review fixes, three blockers (a
  zoom limit and a display-factor limit at either end read as the top only; «返回脉冲星指南», "go back to the guide", for the button
  that closes it, now «从脉冲星指南返回»).
- **Decisions:** the verdict needs no article and takes the kind's measure word, «这是一颗恒星。», «答对了，这是一个星系。» (`verdict`
  keeps the English map's three parameters; 个 for a galaxy or a quasar, 颗 otherwise); no string names or describes a target before
  the reveal (the guide says «寻找脉冲», not «听脉冲»); `TODO_ZH6` is gone from `tests/i18n/radio.spec.ts`; **one form each:** finishing
  «收尾», verified «已验证» / with verification «带验证», header lines «文件头行», column header «列标题行», line endings «换行符», name
  lines «姓名行», vertical cursor «竖直光标», display factor «显示倍率», horizontal scale «水平标度», potentially hazardous «潜在危险»,
  the raw bytes «原始字节» (never «原版», which reads as CLEA's), exercise sheet «实验单». The row and coordinate formats use 时/分/秒
  and 秒 beside Chinese; the reserved file-name characters stay a double-quoted literal with its straight quote (lint-allowed.json),
  as Japanese's.
- **Tests.** `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the 137 keys, that no en.json key is missing, and the seven maps complete;
  `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains e06 and e08 with `E06_MAPS['zh-Hans']` and `E08_MAPS['zh-Hans']`; `e08.spec.ts`'s verdict list
  gains Chinese and its coordinates line reads the colon with no space, as Japanese's; the E06 and E08 export families gain zh-Hans (and
  the camera's live-source option is found by its Chinese label); the real-Chinese CJK walk runs E06 and E08; identical.json and
  lint-allowed.json gain the symbol-only rows Japanese lists (SHA-256, CRLF/LF, column symbols, outcome codes).

## Simplified Chinese: help (ZH-HELP)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.2 "ZH6 ∥ ZH-HELP" in the research root, with JA-HELP as its model and SC7's Unicode
help as its ground. ZH-HELP is stacked on ZH6. Simplified Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hans`. VIREO's 38 bundled help files and
OpenVireo's own help are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`, their bytes, SHA-256 and
provenance are unchanged, and «查看原文（英文）» shows VIREO's exact text.

- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/zh-hans/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'zh-Hans',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`) in VIREO's own
  format, Unicode, NFC, LF, no tab: every TXT line at 72 display columns or fewer (36 Han characters), every title at 65 or fewer; the
  same LST rows, kinds and file tokens as JA-HELP lists (the unterminated CLASSHLP and VarTypeHlp `'begin` rows reproduced; HUBHLP and
  LSSHLP keep their blank lines; MLTSPHLP and SPLSTHLP stay empty; OBJXHLP and TOOLSHLP identical); each section header is the LST title
  it opens; SPECLNS.LNL equals the original and SPECLNS.TXT keeps VIREO's English line headers; the reminders keep the original line
  count. Not in `LATIN1_HELP`.
- **Registry:** `help:{related:'相关主题'}` in `src/i18n/languages.json`; "Related Topics:" reads «相关主题：», "See also:" «另见：»,
  NOTE «注意：»; quotes “ ” with ‘ ’ inside; a control is named by its on-screen Chinese label, with VIREO's English in （） where the
  help first names it (as JA-HELP).
- **How it was made.** As JA-HELP: one Sonnet 5.5 batch translated every LST index first and wrote the title table (206 titles), the
  only source of the section headers and 相关主题 lines that the TXT batches then used; the batches wrote one line per paragraph and a
  scratch tool bundled from `src/core/cleaHelp.ts` (`wrapHelpText`, kinsoku) wrapped every line over 72 columns at the paragraph's
  hanging indent. Another batch wrote the two maps. Three independent Opus readers (A: VIRMNHLP, the reminders, every index and the two
  maps; B: the radio, pulsar and spectra help; C: the observatory, photometer, camera, classification, measuring, cluster HR,
  variable-star and wedge help): 43 text fixes (two more of reader C's declined, under Terms), six title renames in every file («设置孔径大小», «站点和望远镜参数»,
  «转动按钮（“北”“南”“东”“西”）» after the screen's slew buttons, «使滑块重新居中», «选择供研究的脉冲星», «恢复为当前值») and four map strings; one blocker
  (SPECLNS's blend "until mid F" read as «晚F型», late F). VIREO's broken links stay broken (MEASHLP's «单个光谱特征» and «测量单个光谱线»
  resolve to no row, as in English).
- **Terms:** 载入 for load (the glossary forbids 加载), 讲师 for instructor, 星图集 for atlas, 观测程序列表 for VIREO's Program List,
  站点 for VIREO's Site, 迹线 for trace, 等龄线 and 零龄主序. The photometer's aperture is «孔径», as the web photometer's own strings
  (`ph.*`) write it; the telescope's and the antenna's aperture stay «口径» as VIREO's site and radio originals («口径：», «天线口径»).
  Reader C asked for 口径 in the photometer help too, reading «口径：» as the photometer's label; that label is the site window's
  (`src/core/observatorySite.ts`), so the photometer help keeps 孔径.
- **Column tables survive the fixer.** As `ja.test.ts`: `zh-hans.test.ts` fixes help sources with `fixHelp`, which keeps a run of two
  or more spaces between two characters (VARTYPEHLP's type codes), and the Chinese help lint reads such a gap as a column. The fixer
  also took the space out of CLASSHLP's English gloss «（Grayscale ‘Photo’）», which now reads «（Grayscale Photo）» as Japanese's.
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («翻译：OpenVireo的网页改编译文（不是CLEA的文本）» · «查看原文（英文）» · «查看译文»; the fold note says
  what `foldHelpText` does for Chinese: full-width and half-width, Latin accents and case are not told apart, a Han character is not
  found by its pinyin, VIREO's own search in the original view ignores only the case of A to Z) and `webHelp`, every control it names in
  “ ” with its on-screen label.
- **Tests.** `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts` runs the structure checks for Simplified Chinese ("the Simplified Chinese help files
  (Front ZH-HELP)") and a new block runs the zh-Hans profile's typography, its check (what the CJK fixer would still change, any Han
  character outside GB2312), its 你 register and the glossary's `forbidden` over each paragraph of every Chinese help text (wrapped
  lines join without a space beside Han, full-width marks or curly quotes), with no straight `"` in a text file and no full-width digit;
  one wording was changed for it (CLASSHLP's «谱线条目», which the forbidden «谱线条» matched across two words, now «相应的谱线»).
  `zh-hans.test.ts` pins the registry's `help` and the two maps complete (every Simplified Chinese map is now complete);
  `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT.done` gains help and `HELP_TEXT['zh-Hans']` its rows (the search types the Latin letter full-width and
  lower-case, «垂直位置（ｙ坐标）», and finds «光标的垂直位置（Y坐标）»), so `help.spec.ts` runs in Chinese;
  `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`'s help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=zh-Hans`; the real-Chinese CJK walk runs the help windows.

**Review ZH-HELP** (independent reviewer, Opus, 2026-11-21). Verdict: approve with minor fixes, no blocker. Wiring was checked against
JA-HELP (every place has its zh-Hans counterpart; only JA's identical.json row for OK has none, since Chinese writes 确定); all 38
`sourceSha256` match `public/original`; the zh-Hans help lint is stronger than Japanese's (it also runs the fixer's view and the GB2312
check); every `HELP_TEXT['zh-Hans']` string is where `help.spec.ts` looks. Fixed: PHOTOHLP's "fainter yet in the ultraviolet"
(«在紫外（U）下还要更暗», rewrapped); webHelp's 图集 now 星图集 as the screen and CLASSHLP; a title split across a line of this section; the
comments of `src/i18n/zh-hans/index.ts` and `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`.

## Simplified Chinese: release and search pages (ZH-REL+SEO)

The plan's ZH-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md` §6.2, §2 and §10, and the user's Chinese decisions of
2026-11-09 and 2026-11-19) after ZH0–ZH6 and ZH-HELP merged: the catalog (3,606 keys), all 33 maps, every VIREO original and the 38
help modules complete (`node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs --lang zh-Hans`: 33/33 maps, 756 originals translated, 0 pending). User
decision 5 of 2026-11-09 (AGENTS.md): a language turns on once all of its sub-fronts are in. RU-REL+SEO and JA-REL+SEO are the
models; the registry made the release one field and the SEO table one row. AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: zh-Hans has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: an
exact `?lang=` tag (that load only, not stored), then the stored choice, then the first browser language: `zh`, `zh-CN`, `zh-SG`,
`zh-MY`, `zh-Hans` and any `zh-Hans-*` (`zh-Hans-TW` too, the script subtag beating the region), in any case (`ZH-cn`), open
Simplified Chinese. Traditional browsers (`zh-TW`, `zh-HK`, `zh-MO`, `zh-Hant`, `zh-Hant-*`; the registry's `except`) keep English
until zh-Hant exists, with 简体中文 offered in the selector (decided 2026-11-09); Cantonese (`yue`), Korean and a browser whose
first language is another (`['en-US','zh-CN']`, `['zh-TW','zh-CN']`) stay as they were. A stored zh-Hans opens it in a Traditional
browser too. `?lang=zh`, `?lang=zh-CN`, `?lang=zh-hans` are no tag. The selector lists all nine languages on every load, each in
its own `lang`, with «简体中文 (Simplified Chinese)» between 日本語 and Русский. A change from Chinese is stored. `<html
lang="zh-Hans">`, the title and the failure notices are ZH0's.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for the earlier releases: `src/i18n/zh-hans/zh-hans.test.ts` checks the registry line
(`released:true`, no `thousands`, `help`), `createI18n(zh,'zh-Hans')` without fallback, every en.json key and no extra one, and
every map of `MAP_IDS` against its English shape without `partial`; `check-i18n-dist.mjs` finds every zh-Hans module in its own
chunk and no empty area.

**No registered language is a preview any more.** Simplified Chinese was the last unreleased line of the registry, so the tests
that used "the language still unreleased" moved to fixtures: `languages.test.ts`'s preview rows (an exact `?lang=` opens it, a
stored or detected tag does not, the selector adds it only on its own preview, `selectorShown`) use the fixture's Traditional
Chinese (`ZH_HANT`, unreleased), and the partial-language case is the qaa-XA fixture's alone, at unit level (`partialLanguage.test.ts`,
`split.test.ts`: the plan, the inline empty areas, the English completion): `areas.spec.ts`'s ZH0 partial test is gone and
Simplified Chinese joins `RELEASED_AREAS`. Until ZT0 registers zh-Hant unreleased, no browser test loads a partial language or
changes language from a preview (the dialog's `language-preview` note, the choice travelling as `?lang=`, nothing stored); ZT0
brings both back for zh-Hant.

**Tests.** The Chinese specs open Chinese by the browser language alone, as RU-REL did for Russian.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `CHINESE_SIMPLIFIED_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `contextLocale` gives it `zh-CN` (ZH0's
  `CHINESE_LOCALE`). Every language-parameterised spec, the CJK walks of `routes.scripts.ts` included, now runs Chinese in a zh-CN
  browser without `?lang=`. Chinese is not in `LAYOUT_IN_SCRIPTS_WALK`: as Japanese, its narrow and browser sweeps run in the main
  suite (whose CI fonts draw Han).
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: nine options with their `lang` on every load; ZH0's preview test became ZH-REL's detection test (zh,
  zh-CN, zh-SG, zh-MY, ZH-cn, zh-Hans-TW and `['zh-CN','en-US']` → zh-Hans; zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO, zh-Hant, yue-HK, ko-KR,
  `['en-US','zh-CN']` and `['zh-TW','zh-CN']` → English with no Chinese module fetched; `['ja-JP','zh-CN']` → Japanese; a stored
  zh-Hans beats an fr-FR and a zh-TW browser, a stored English a zh-CN one, `?lang=` beats both; a change from Chinese to French and
  back is stored with a clean address) and a keyboard test in a zh-CN browser; the French, Indonesian, Japanese, Portuguese and
  Russian keyboard walks step over 简体中文. The `withPreview` helpers are gone (no preview adds an option now).
