- `areas.spec.ts`: Simplified Chinese joins the released-language loop («in a Simplified Chinese browser › Simplified Chinese
  (released) fetches its core…»).
- `seo.spec.ts`, `narrow.spec.ts` and `browsers.spec.ts` loop the released languages and the SEO table, so Chinese joins.
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the routes and home loop gains Simplified Chinese (zh-CN, its own heading and title, nine
  options); zh-CN, zh-SG and bare zh browsers open Chinese and read «CLEA_VStrs.dat，31,147条记录» (a comma, as English, with the
  full-width comma and the measure word of the Chinese sentence), zh-TW and zh-HK browsers open English; the 404 page has nine
  languages; the Chinese SEO pages (below).
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (ZH0's test became ZH-REL's), `languages.test.ts`, `partialLanguage.test.ts`, `direction.test.ts`,
  `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` (zh-Hans as released).

**Release text.** The 404 page (`dist/404.html`, generated from the SEO table) has nine languages: «找不到页面 · 此地址不属于
OpenVireo。» under `lang="zh-Hans"`, linking `/?lang=zh-Hans`, and «未找到» in the title. README and THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt carry
"Simplified Chinese translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's files and texts are unchanged." with the same uncertainty about
the recorded CLEA License.rtf terms, and the preview image's font (Noto Sans CJK SC, SIL OFL 1.1, from the Ubuntu archive; no font
file ships). `src/i18n/zh-hans/index.ts`'s comment says it is released and complete. `docs/CHINA-CHECKLIST.md` gains the Chinese
interface (plan §10): items 1–5 keep their English names through `?lang=en` links (a Chinese tester's browser would otherwise open
Chinese), and a new item 7 asks whether a zh-CN browser opens Chinese, the selector shows 简体中文, the help is Chinese, and how
mixed Chinese and Latin text looks (one optional screenshot, the spacing question of plan §4.2); item 6 also asks which language
WeChat's browser shows.

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front ZH-SEO"): a `zh-Hans` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['zh-Hans']`,
`ogLocale:'zh_CN'`, prefix `/zh-hans`, `app:'/?lang=zh-Hans'`), `src/seo/labs.zh-hans.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX['zh-Hans']='/zh-hans'`,
the `/zh-hans/*` header rule and `public/og-image-zh-hans.png`. Written by Opus from OpenVireo's English pages and checked by the
SC4 framework with profile `zh-Hans` and glossary field `zh-Hans`: 你, full-width marks with no space between Han and Latin text,
«实验» for a lab, «探究» for an investigation, the app's own lab names, the glossary's terms («光谱分析器» after a first draft's
«光谱分析工具» failed the glossary check), «使用方式» with the line on numbers (decimal point, half-width digits), and the footer
«本页中文文本是OpenVireo的网页改编，不是CLEA的文本。» with the credit to Project CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF. Google's
hreflang guide names `zh-Hans` (checked 2026-11-21), so the table's format check admits a script subtag.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). zh-Hans: 13 chunks, 239.6 KB gzip in all (core 104.5 KB gzip, 288.5 raw), none
loaded by `index.html`, all under the plan's budgets; 38 help chunks, 52.9 KB gzip. The release changes no zh-Hans chunk's
content. Deploy limits: 2,539 files (nine Chinese pages and the image are new).

**Verification** (2026-11-21, worktree `claude/i18n-zh-rel` at 09ea83ef, then the review fixes; Playwright under the scripts test
fontconfig, Noto Sans CJK and DejaVu). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 6,905 passed and 11 skipped; `npm run test:resources`; `npm run build`
with every check; `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright: `language-selector`, `areas`, `seo`, `narrow`, `browsers`, `help`,
`shell`, `page-bar`, `app-shell`, `seo-labs` and `home` 286; the export families with zh-Hans 41; the scripts suite 265 (the
real-Chinese CJK walks in a zh-CN browser among them); every Simplified Chinese test of `tests/i18n` in a zh-CN browser 89 of 91:
the two Multi-Spec layout tests report a plot label «3» 2 px outside its card under the scripts fontconfig, and Japanese's do the
same there, while all sixteen of that test pass under the main suite's fontconfig (DejaVu), which the main suite runs, so it is the
CJK test font's metrics, not this front; `npm run test:pages` on `dist/` 216 (the Chinese routes and home in a zh-CN browser, zh-CN,
zh-SG, zh and zh-TW, zh-HK count checks, the nine-language 404 and the Chinese SEO pages among them).

**Review ZH-REL+SEO** (independent reviewer, Opus, 2026-11-21). Verdict: approve after fixes (one major, nine minor, four nits; no
blocker). The release wiring, the moved preview tests and all six keyboard walks (nine options in registry order) were found
correct, and `zh-Hans` / `zh_CN` confirmed against Google's guide. Fixed: the China checklist's item 7a quoted the observatory's
«从天空开始。», but a bare openvireo.space opens the welcome page («Project CLEA的VIREO天文实验，为你的浏览器重新打造», with the tour
box «欢迎使用OpenVireo» / «跳过导览» on a first visit) (major); the checklist's older slips on the lines this front edits (step 1a's
English title is the welcome's, step 3a reopens `?lang=en#observatory`, the phone's header button is «Menu», not «Tools»); the SEO
texts' «钙K线、钙H线和G带» (the G band is not calcium's), «巨墙» and «标识符» as the app writes them, «以从原版程序复原的行为为基础»,
«改编自CLEA VIREO的一项实验», «星团的赫罗图», «射电观测»; this section's statement that the partial-language case now lives at unit
level only (the preview reload dialog and a partial language's area fetches have no end-to-end test until zh-Hant's ZT0 brings
them back); stale comments in `routes.scripts.ts` and `areas.spec.ts`; the 404 count in `docs/SEO.md`.

## Traditional Chinese: the folder, lints and shell (ZT0)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §1.2 #7–#8, §2, §5, §6.3 (ZT0) in the research root, with ZH0 as its model and the user's
Chinese decisions of 2026-11-19 (AGENTS.md): Traditional Chinese is **adapted from the reviewed Simplified Chinese with the English as
the source of truth** (never converted character by character), to **Taiwan's standard**, and opened, once released, by every
Traditional browser (zh-Hant, zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO). ZT0 is stacked on ZH-REL+SEO. Traditional Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hant`
until ZT-REL; files the site writes stay English.

- **Registry.** `src/i18n/languages.json` gains `zh-Hant` between zh-Hans and Russian: dir and page path `zh-hant`, `<html
  lang="zh-Hant">`, autonym «繁體中文», English name Traditional Chinese (the selector shows «繁體中文 (Traditional Chinese)», as
  «简体中文 (Simplified Chinese)»), `match` zh-Hant, zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO, `budgetFactor` 1.4, no `thousands` (comma grouping as
  English), `released:false`, and Taiwan-worded notices («無法載入繁體中文；已改為顯示英文。請重新整理頁面再試一次。» and the area
  notice). zh-Hans's `except` already keeps those ranges off Simplified Chinese, and a script subtag beats a region (zh-Hans-TW is
  Simplified, zh-Hant-CN Traditional). Until ZT-REL a Traditional browser keeps English with 简体中文 offered, as decided.
- **The fixture.** `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts`'s `ZH_HANT` stand-in (SC8's, «Chinese, Traditional») is replaced by the real
  line, which joins `PLANNED` in registry order; the detection table's Traditional rows now name zh-Hant, and the preview rows that
  ZH-REL moved to the stand-in use the real unreleased line.
- **Folder** `src/i18n/zh-hant/`: `index.ts` (`CHINESE_TRADITIONAL`), `zh-hant.json` (the shell's 131 keys and the welcome's 27, as
  ZH0's slice), `originals.json` (ZH0's 10 shell originals), the maps appShell, helpMenu, languageChoice («語言 / Language»),
  projectLinks and tour complete and the menu names of objectXLab, polish and variableStars, `glossary.json`, `identical.json`,
  `lint-allowed.json`, `zh-hant.test.ts`.
- **Glossary.** Every row of the reviewed zh-Hans glossary (151 terms, 208 names) with a `zh-Hant` field beside the zh-Hans one,
  adapted to Taiwan (檔案, 儲存, 另存新檔, 尋找, 搜尋, 檢視, 開啟, 還原, 重設, 列印, 結束, 說明, 選單, 目標清單, 示範模式, 電波望遠鏡,
  哈伯定律, 訊噪比, 週期, 都卜勒…); the English patterns are unchanged. **Taiwan-vocabulary rule** (plan §1.2 #8): its `forbidden` list
  holds 您, the zh-Hans list's rejected forms in Traditional characters, and 27 mainland words written in Traditional characters, which
  the script-purity check cannot see (軟件, 信息, 默認, 屏幕, 鼠標, 網絡, 視頻, 打印, 菜單, 窗口, 加載, 導出, 導入, 用戶, 搜索, 字符,
  鏈接, 射電, 多普勒, 哈勃, 信噪比, 數據, 保存, 點擊, 光標, 刷新, 另存為). Words that are mainland usage in one sense but Taiwan usage in
  another stay allowed (程序 procedure, 設置 to set up, 支持 to support, 質量 mass, 圖像, 文件 document, 打開, 退出, 幫助, 查看, 重置,
  恢復, 查找, 列表): the term and name rows prefer the Taiwan form and the readers check for them. A browser tab is 分頁 (Taiwan software);
  標籤頁 is forbidden with the other mainland interface words the readers added (視圖, 對話框, 激活, 回調, 創建, 噪聲, 緩存, 芯片,
  坐標, 視寧度…), which the zh-Hans text ZT1 and ZT2 adapt uses often.
- **Lint and fixer.** The zh-Hant profile (SC4, calibrated in ZH0 with zh-Hans) applies unchanged: full-width ，。、：；？！ next to Han,
  「」 outer and 『』 inner quotes, no space between Han and Latin text, 你 not 您, no Simplified-only character (the generated table),
  Big5's 13,060 standard hanzi only (built from the runtime's Big5 decoder). Fix mode `env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx
  vitest run src/i18n/zh-hant/zh-hant.test.ts` runs the CJK punctuation fixer in its Traditional style (`TRADITIONAL_CHINESE_STYLE`);
  it removed one space («2MASS星表»). **Calibration** (232 strings of 12+ characters): grapheme ratio p10 0.24, median 0.34, p90 0.49,
  as zh-Hans's (0.24, 0.33, 0.47), so the shared band 0.15–0.8 stays; two strings are reported for the reader (projectLinks.groupLabel
  0.89, variableStars.nav 0.14).
- **How it was made.** Four Sonnet 5.5 batches, each given the English as source, the reviewed zh-Hans as reference and the Taiwan
  tables of the plan (§1.2 #8, §5.2–5.3): the glossary's terms and forbidden list, its names, the catalog with the originals, and the
  eight maps. Implementer's fixes before review: the browser tab term gains 分頁; «保存» in two session strings became «保留» (to keep);
  the batches chose 昴宿星團 for the Pleiades, 偏好設定, 工作階段 (session), 雜湊值 (hash), 跳到主要內容 (skip to content), 略過導覽, 離開
  for instructor mode's Leave.
- **Tests.** `zh-hant.test.ts`: the registry line, the shell and welcome coverage, every key also in the zh-Hans catalog it adapts,
  map shapes, the lint with the Taiwan glossary, the fixer a no-op, the glossary free of Simplified-only characters with the
  Taiwan-vocabulary words forbidden and the legitimate ones not, Big5 only, the calibration. `languages.test.ts` (the zh-Hant notices,
  selector label, ranges; the detection table), `locale.test.ts` (Front ZT0: only `?lang=zh-Hant` opens it; Traditional browsers and a
  stored zh-Hant get English; once released, zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO open it and Simplified browsers do not), `direction.test.ts`,
  `pageBar.test.ts`. End to end: `CHINESE_TRADITIONAL_TEXT` (done: shell; letters Han and ，。、：；？！「」（）; `contextLocale` zh-TW once
  released), so `shell.spec.ts` runs in zh-Hant; `areas.spec.ts` gets its partial-language case back (zh-Hant fetches only its core,
  and no chunk for an empty area; only `photometer` holds text); `language-selector.spec.ts` gets a preview test back (Traditional
  browsers and a stored zh-Hant get English and fetch no zh-Hant module; `?lang=zh-Hant` offers 繁體中文 between 简体中文 and Русский; a
  change on the preview stores nothing); `exports-identity.spec.ts`'s toolbar family gains zh-Hant; `routes.scripts.ts`'s real-Chinese
  shell walk runs zh-Hant at 320 and 1440 px with Noto Sans CJK TC alone.
- **Sizes** (`npm run build`): zh-Hant 2 chunks, 9.5 KB gzip (core 9.2 KB gzip, 22.5 raw), none loaded by `index.html`.

**Review ZT0** (two independent Opus readers, 2026-11-22, each reading every string, as the user's decision requires). Reader A:
approve after one major fix and 23 minor; reader B (who also checked the wiring against ZH0 and found nothing missing): accept after
2 major and 14 minor. Both found the same major: «觀測程式清單» in `project.toolbarHelp`, a 程序→程式 conversion that reads as a
software program, now «觀測計畫清單», the glossary's Program List. Fixed: the forbidden list gains the mainland interface words both
readers listed (視圖, 對話框, 激活, 標籤頁, 回調, 創建, 噪聲, 緩存, 文件夾, 賬號/賬戶, 服務器, 芯片, 分辨率, 粘貼, 內存, 硬盤, 圖標, 坐標, 全屏,
實時, 視寧度, 滑塊, 回看: 60 entries in all), and the terms stop accepting mainland alternatives (tab 分頁, seeing 視相度, colour excess
色超|色餘, epoch 曆元, IR camera IR相機|紅外線相機, callback 回呼, slider 滑桿, fallback 備用|備援|後援, Tab character without a space);
the site names read 觀測站 (觀測站參數, 觀測站資訊, 重設電波觀測站), Record/Review «記錄／查閱», «列印所選視野»; the tour's «基於» and «按»
(mainland «by») rewritten («以…為本», «依分區»), «起不到…作用» → «完全沒有保護作用», the stray space in «為 −1», «本機» for a control's
local range, «請» where the English has no "please", the single «…» in the reminder's loading line, «執行「執行實驗」» in the exit note,
and «復原» for recovered behaviour (one form, as zh-Hans's 复原).

## Traditional Chinese: the observatory, VIREO's names and E01–E03 (ZT1)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.3 (ZT1: the observatory, all originals and E01–E03) in the research root, with ZH1,
ZH2 and ZH3 as its models. ZT1 is stacked on ZT0. Traditional Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hant`; files the site writes stay
English. As the user decided on 2026-11-19, every string is adapted from the reviewed zh-Hans with the English as the source of truth,
to Taiwan's standard, and read by two Opus readers.

- **Scope.** Every en.json key outside ZT2's areas (the zh-Hans fronts ZH4–ZH6: `ph`, `pa`, `pleiadesLab`, `nc`, `db`, `ce`, `clusterLab`,
  `ownHr`, `obsHr`, `radio*`, `pulsarParameters`, `pulsarLab`, `plr`, `fits`): 2,021 keys, which with ZT0's shell make 2,172
  (the shell's menu names of ZT2's areas included, 2,179 keys in all); all of VIREO's 756 originals (the same set as zh-Hans, with the
  four only Chinese translates: General, E, N, S, and 北/南/東/西 for the buttons); and the maps observatory, fieldInfo,
  fieldReadDetails, nakedEye, observationMetadata, qsoList, sound, camera, polish (now complete), engineView, spectralRange and wedge.
- **How it was made.** Two Sonnet batches for the originals first (so that the catalog could quote VIREO's names as they read), nine
  catalog batches of up to 250 keys, each merged before the next so that quoted labels match, and three map batches; each batch got the
  English as source, the reviewed zh-Hans as reference, the zh-Hant glossary and originals. Implementer's fixes before review: one form
  for "acquire" (擷取; an early batch wrote 採集, 105 strings unified) and for "fallback" (備用); the file term widened to 檔案|檔
  (Taiwan writes 檔名, 文字檔) and the help term to 說明|幫助 (the sense "will not help"); VIREO's `...` dropped from names quoted in
  prose, as zh-Hans; «日誌» for journal; the zh-Hans allowances for the same keys (identical.json, lint-allowed.json) carried over.
- **Review** (four Opus readers in two pairs, each pair reading the same strings independently). Part 1, the originals, the 12 maps and
  964 catalog keys: reader A1 52 major and 58 minor, reader B1 19 major and 104 minor; part 2, the other 1,057 keys: reader A2 3 major
  and 59 minor, reader B2 55 minor. Applied: quoted labels made to match their own labels (Leave «離開», Lab Table «實驗檯», Program
  List «觀測計畫清單», Write Log File, Add saved measurements, Keep your work, 微調／粗調); one form per term (recovered «復原», restore
  «還原», identify «辨認», workbook «活頁簿», profile «設定檔», file header «標頭» with 檔頭 now forbidden, calibration, normalized,
  template «範本», records «筆», canonical, rating); two meaning errors («通量» for throughput, «備用» for prepared); the slit level
  «位準»; mainland words (對象, 運行, 校驗, 上下文, 生成, 牆上時間) and stray «請» in help texts; the same VIREO message worded one way.
  Reader A2 found that "runner gap" (the gap to the runner-up, the second-nearest object) had been read as a running gap, in zh-Hant and
  in the reviewed zh-Hans alike: both now say «最近者與次近者的距離差» / «最近者与次近者的距离差».
- **Tests**, as ZH1–ZH3 wired them: `zh-hant.test.ts` (every key outside ZT2 present and no ZT2 key beyond the shell's menu names,
  the 17 complete maps, every original translated or kept with a reason and the same set as zh-Hans, line breaks and fragment edges,
  the fixer a no-op, the lint); `vireoSites.test.ts` STRICT gains zh-Hant; `dynamic-key-sites.json` pins its two map heads;
  `CHINESE_TRADITIONAL_TEXT.done` gains observatory, e01, e02 and e03 with the maps; the observatory and E01 walks' `SAME_IN` and
  `NOT_SAME_IN`; the export families of the observatory (hot list, options file, Field Information), the spectrum analysis, E01, Hubble,
  star and field spectrometers, E01 exposures, E02 and E03 gain zh-Hant; the real-script walks of `routes.scripts.ts` run Traditional
  Chinese; `areas.spec.ts`'s partial case pins classification, cluster, exposure, fits, lss and photometer, as ZH3 did for zh-Hans.

## Traditional Chinese: E04, E05, the radio telescope, E06 and E08 (ZT2)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.3 (ZT2) in the research root, with ZH4, ZH5 and ZH6 as its models. ZT2 is stacked on
ZT1. Traditional Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hant`; files the site writes stay English; E08's object stays hidden until the reveal.
As decided on 2026-11-19, every string is adapted from the reviewed zh-Hans with the English as source and read by two Opus readers.

- **Scope.** The 1,427 keys left (the prefixes of ZH4–ZH6: `ph`, `pa`, `pleiadesLab`, `nc`, `db`, `ce`, `clusterLab`, `ownHr`,
  `obsHr`, `radio*`, `pulsarParameters`, `pulsarLab`, `plr`, `fits`): `zh-hant.json` now holds every en.json key (3,606). The maps
  clusterHr, clusterData, variableStars (complete), radioAntenna, radioFinishSave, radioR1, radioRecordSave, pulsar, pulsarSavedRows,
  pulsarLoadSave, objectXLab (complete), objectXGuide, objectXMeasure and objectXSession: every map but the help maps (ZT-HELP).
- **How it was made.** Seven Sonnet catalog batches, merged one after another, and four map batches (radioAntenna, 324 strings, and
  pulsar, 443, each on its own); the task files carried ZT1's term decisions from the start. Implementer's fixes before review:
  metallicity «金屬量» (a batch wrote 金屬豐度), «天文台望遠鏡», «曲線試聽» then «軌跡試聽» (below), the zh-Hans allowances for the same
  keys carried over, «目視輔助線» for the plot's visual guides, «保持» for a held hour angle (not the forbidden 保存), and the E06 Save
  dialog label «檔案名稱» (檔名 also matched inside the folder note's 副檔名, which the E06 spec found).
- **Review** (four Opus readers, two per half, each pair reading the same strings independently). Part 1, 1,000 catalog keys: reader A1
  3 major and 49 minor, reader B1 3 major and 38 minor; part 2, 427 keys and the 14 maps (1,272 strings): reader A2 2 major and 23
  minor, reader B2 2 major and 24 minor. Applied: colour excess «色超»; quoted labels matched («載入內建的脈衝星檔案Pulsars.dat», «繪製我的
  觀測記錄»); Trace audition «軌跡試聽», its glossary name changed, as the receiver's 16 «軌跡» strings read; "recording" «記錄資料» in the
  pulsar load and saved-rows maps (they had 接收資料); VIREO's «資料未寫入檔案» where two raw-export notes left it in English; extinction
  «消光修正» (not reddening) in the cluster test-mode note; "not reproduced" «未重現»; one form per term (telescope access «使用權», B/V
  pairs «配對», Poisson «卜瓦松», now forbidden as 帕松, numeric keypad «數字鍵台», records «筆»); mainland or inconsistent words (站點,
  探究操作, 多天體光譜儀, 可選/選用) and stray «請» in help. No reader found a hint at an E08 target's identity before the reveal.
- **Tests**, as ZH4–ZH6 wired them: `zh-hant.test.ts` (every en.json key and no other, 31 complete maps); `CHINESE_TRADITIONAL_TEXT.done`
  gains e04, e05, radio, e06 and e08 with the E04/E05, radio, E06 and E08 map entries; the e04, e05 and radio walks' `SAME_IN`;
  `e08.spec.ts`'s verdicts («這是一顆恆星。», «這是一個星系。», «這是一個類星體（QSO）。», «這是一顆脈衝星。», «這是一顆小行星。») and its
  coordinates line (赤經, no space); the E04, E05, radio telescope, E06 and E08 export families gain zh-Hant; `areas.spec.ts`'s
  partial pin gains pulsar and radio (every area now holds zh-Hant text).
- **Seen, not changed (outside this front):** en.json's `ownHr.adaptation` names the button "Plot my photometer records", but the
  button reads "Plot my observation records" (a reader of part 1); the zh-Hant text quotes the real button.

## Traditional Chinese: help (ZT-HELP)

Plan: analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md §6.3 in the research root, with ZH-HELP as its model. ZT-HELP is stacked on ZT2.
Traditional Chinese stays behind `?lang=zh-Hant`. VIREO's 38 bundled help files and OpenVireo's own help are web adaptations by
OpenVireo, never CLEA's: the originals under `public/original`, their bytes, SHA-256 and provenance are unchanged, and «檢視原文（英文）»
shows VIREO's exact text.

- **The 38 help modules** `src/i18n/zh-hant/help/<NAME>.ts` (`{language:'zh-Hant',sourceName,sourceSha256,text}`), as ZH-HELP's: Unicode,
  NFC, LF, no tab; every TXT line at 72 display columns or fewer and every title at 65 or fewer; the same LST rows, kinds and file tokens,
  the same section headers, blank lines and 相關主題 blocks as the zh-Hans set (a scratch check compared each file's skeleton); SPECLNS.LNL
  equals the original and SPECLNS.TXT keeps VIREO's English line headers; the reminders keep the original line count. Not in `LATIN1_HELP`.
- **Registry:** `help:{related:'相關主題'}`; "See also:" reads «另見：», NOTE «注意：»; quotes 「」 with 『』 inside; a control is named by its
  zh-Hant on-screen label, with VIREO's English in （） where the help first names it, as the zh-Hans.
- **How it was made.** Adapted from the reviewed zh-Hans with the English as the source (user decision of 2026-11-19). One Sonnet 5.5
  batch adapted the 206 LST titles (the only source of section headers and 相關主題 entries), another the two maps, then five batches the
  TXT files, one line per paragraph; ZH-HELP's scratch tool (`wrapHelpText`, kinsoku) wrapped them at 72 columns with the paragraph's
  hanging indent. Four independent Opus readers, two on every string (part 1: the titles, the two maps, VIRMNHLP, the reminders, the
  pulsar help and VARTYPEHLP; part 2: the observatory, photometer, camera, classification, spectra, cluster HR, measuring and wedge help):
  no blocker; four majors (PULSRANL's vertical-scale control is the panel's «顯示倍率»; its Results items quote the pulsar panel's labels,
  «記錄目前的測量...», «查閱已記錄的測量...», «天體辨認名稱»; PULSRHLP's «「從」接收機»; SPECLNS's ionized calcium is the interface's «電離鈣»)
  and about 150 minor fixes: about 100 stray 請 where the English says no "please"; mainland usage (自帶 → 內建, 報錯, 選中, 給出, 作業 →
  工作階段, 新建, 列印件); one form per term («滑鼠按鈕», «變更», «發出警告», «巴耳末系», «汙染», the wedge's group file «小組»); six titles
  («設定日期和時間», «使用各望遠鏡», «查閱資料», «查閱已記錄的測量», «儲存、查閱、分析結果», «轉動按鈕（「北」、「南」、「東」、「西」）»).
- **Terms:** 載入 for load, 講師 for instructor, 星圖集 for atlas, 觀測計畫清單 for VIREO's Program List, 觀測站 for VIREO's Site, 軌跡 for
  trace, 查閱 for Review (originals.json's «記錄／查閱»), 等齡線 and 零齡主序. The photometer's aperture is «孔徑» as the web photometer's
  strings; the telescope's and the antenna's stay «口徑». VARTYPEHLP's Mira type reads «米拉型變星»: the batch's 芻藁 has 藁, outside Big5.
- **Maps, complete:** `helpViewer` («翻譯：OpenVireo的網頁改編譯文（不是CLEA的文本）» · «檢視原文（英文）» · «檢視譯文»; the fold note says what
  `foldHelpText` does: full-width and half-width, Latin accents and case are not told apart, a Han character is not found by its pinyin or
  zhuyin nor a Traditional character by its Simplified form, VIREO's own search in the original view ignores only the case of A to Z)
  and `webHelp`, every control in 「」 with its on-screen label (the glossary's «載入已儲存資料...»).
- **Tests.** `src/help/helpTranslation.test.ts` runs the structure checks for Traditional Chinese ("the Traditional Chinese help files
  (Front ZT-HELP)") and a zh-Hant block as ZH-HELP's: the profile's typography, its check (what the CJK fixer would still change in the
  「」 style, any Han character outside Big5), the 你 register and the glossary's `forbidden` over each paragraph, with no straight `"` in a
  text file and no full-width digit. `zh-hant.test.ts` fixes help sources with `fixHelp` (column gaps kept), pins the registry's `help` and
  the two maps complete (every Traditional Chinese map is now complete); `CHINESE_TRADITIONAL_TEXT.done` gains help and
  `HELP_TEXT['zh-Hant']` its rows (the query «垂直位置（ｙ座標）» finds «游標的垂直位置（Y座標）»), so `help.spec.ts` runs in Traditional
  Chinese; the Pages help-chunk smoke gains `?lang=zh-Hant`; the real-Chinese CJK walk runs the help windows.
- **Sizes and one build lesson.** 38 zh-Hant help chunks, 53.0 KB gzip in all, fetched only by the help viewer; zh-Hant's 13 interface
  chunks are 240.7 KB gzip. `check-i18n-dist.mjs` reads a map file's single-quoted literals, so a doc comment with two ASCII apostrophes
  around Han text («OpenVireo's own help topics (說明 › 說明主題…) … OpenVireo's») read as a marker no chunk holds and failed the build;
  the two help maps' comments write ’, as the zh-Hans maps do.
- **Verification** (worktree `claude/i18n-zh-zt-help` at 8d4ae653 with origin/main f8a5ade6 merged, then the comment fix): `npx tsc -b`;
  Vitest 7,014 passed and 11 skipped; `npm run test:resources`; `npm run build` with every check (after the comment fix);
  `npm run test:deploy-limits`; Playwright `help`, `areas`, `shell` and `language-selector` 247; the toolbar export family 14; the
  scripts suite 287 (the real-Chinese CJK walks of the help windows among them); `npm run test:pages` 217.

## Traditional Chinese: release and search pages (ZT-REL+SEO)

The plan's ZT-REL+SEO item (`analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-zh.md` §6.3, §2 and §10, the user's Chinese decisions of 2026-11-09
and 2026-11-19 and the coordinator's zh-Hant settings of 2026-11-21) after ZT0, ZT1, ZT2 and ZT-HELP merged: the catalog (3,606
keys), all 33 maps, every VIREO original and the 38 help modules complete. User decision 5 of 2026-11-09 (AGENTS.md): a language
turns on once all of its sub-fronts are in. ZH-REL+SEO is the model; AGENTS.md records the release in one dated line. Every
language of the 2026-11-09 order is now released.

**Detection and the selector.** `src/i18n/languages.json`: zh-Hant has `"released":true`. One load decides once, unchanged: an
exact `?lang=` tag (that load only), then the stored choice, then the first browser language: `zh-TW`, `zh-HK`, `zh-MO`, `zh-Hant`
and any `zh-Hant-*`, in any case (`ZH-tw`), open Traditional Chinese, Hong Kong and Macau included (user decision of 2026-11-19).
A script subtag beats the region: `zh-Hans-TW` and `zh-Hans-HK` stay Simplified. Cantonese (`yue`, `yue-HK`), `en-HK` and a browser
whose first language is another (`['en-US','zh-TW']`) stay as they were; `['zh-TW','zh-CN']` opens Traditional, `['zh-CN','zh-TW']`
Simplified. A stored zh-Hant opens it in a Simplified browser too. `?lang=zh-hant`, `?lang=zh-TW` and `?lang=zh-HK` are no tag. The
selector lists all ten languages on every load, each in its own `lang`, with «繁體中文 (Traditional Chinese)» between 简体中文 and
Русский. `<html lang="zh-Hant">`, the title and the failure notices are ZT0's.

**Strict catalog and maps.** As for the earlier releases: `src/i18n/zh-hant/zh-hant.test.ts` checks the registry line
(`released:true`, no `except`, no `thousands`, `help`), `createI18n(zh,'zh-Hant')` without fallback, every en.json key and no extra
one, and every map of `MAP_IDS` against its English shape without `partial`; `check-i18n-dist.mjs` finds every zh-Hant module in its
own chunk and no empty area.

**No registered language is a preview any more.** Traditional Chinese was the last unreleased line of the registry, as Simplified
Chinese was before ZT0. The unit preview rows of `languages.test.ts` (an exact `?lang=` opens it, a stored or detected tag does not,
the selector adds it only on its own preview, `selectorShown`) now use the fixture's `ZH_HANT_PREVIEW` (`tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts`:
zh-Hant's own line with `released:false`), and `locale.test.ts` keeps a before-release check on a registry copy, as ZH-REL's. ZT0's
browser tests of a language before its release are gone again: `areas.spec.ts`'s partial-language test (Traditional Chinese joins
`RELEASED_AREAS`) and `language-selector.spec.ts`'s preview test (the dialog's `language-preview` note, the choice travelling as
`?lang=`, nothing stored), with its `withPreview` helpers. The partial-language case lives at unit level only (the qaa-XA fixture of
`partialLanguage.test.ts` and `split.test.ts`) until a new language's first front registers one.

**Tests.** The Traditional Chinese specs open it by the browser language alone.
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `CHINESE_TRADITIONAL_TEXT` has every area done and no query; `contextLocale` gives it `zh-TW`
  (`TRADITIONAL_CHINESE_LOCALE`, ZT0's). Every language-parameterised spec, the CJK walks of `routes.scripts.ts` included, now runs
  Traditional Chinese in a zh-TW browser without `?lang=`.
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: ten options with their `lang` on every load; ZH-REL's detection test now expects zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO,
  zh-Hant and `['zh-TW','zh-CN']` to open Traditional Chinese; ZT0's preview test became ZT-REL's detection test (zh-TW, zh-HK, zh-MO,
  zh-Hant, zh-Hant-CN, ZH-tw and `['zh-TW','en-US']` → zh-Hant with no Simplified module fetched; zh-CN and zh-Hans-TW → zh-Hans,
  `['en-US','zh-TW']` and yue-HK → English with no Traditional module fetched; a stored zh-Hant beats a zh-CN browser, a stored English a
  zh-TW one, `?lang=` beats both; a change from Traditional Chinese to French and back is stored with a clean address) and a keyboard
  test in a zh-TW browser; every keyboard walk steps over 繁體中文.
- `areas.spec.ts`: Traditional Chinese joins the released-language loop («in a Traditional Chinese browser › Traditional Chinese
  (released) fetches its core…»).
- `seo.spec.ts`, `narrow.spec.ts` and `browsers.spec.ts` loop the released languages and the SEO table, so Traditional Chinese joins.
- `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`: the routes and home loop gains Traditional Chinese (zh-TW, its own heading and title, ten
  options); zh-TW, zh-HK and zh-MO browsers open it and read «CLEA_VStrs.dat，31,147筆記錄» (a comma, as English, with the full-width
  comma and Traditional Chinese's measure word); the 404 page has ten languages; the Traditional Chinese SEO pages (below); the help
  smoke keeps its `?lang=zh-Hant` link.
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (ZT0's test became ZT-REL's), `languages.test.ts`, `partialLanguage.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts`.

**Release text.** The 404 page (`dist/404.html`, generated from the SEO table) has ten languages: «找不到網頁 · 此網址不屬於
OpenVireo。» under `lang="zh-Hant"`, linking `/?lang=zh-Hant`, and «找不到網頁» in the title. README and THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt carry
"Traditional Chinese translation: an OpenVireo web adaptation; CLEA's files and texts are unchanged." with the same uncertainty about
the recorded CLEA License.rtf terms, and the preview image's font (Noto Sans CJK TC, SIL OFL 1.1, from the Ubuntu archive; no font
file ships). `src/i18n/zh-hant/index.ts`'s comment says it is released and complete. `docs/CHINA-CHECKLIST.md` is unchanged: its
testers are in mainland China, where Simplified Chinese is written.

**Search pages.** Described in `docs/SEO.md` ("Front ZT-SEO"): a `zh-Hant` row in `src/seo/languages.ts` (`hreflang:['zh-Hant']`,
`ogLocale:'zh_TW'`, prefix `/zh-hant`, `app:'/?lang=zh-Hant'`), `src/seo/labs.zh-hant.ts`, `SEO_PATH_PREFIX['zh-Hant']='/zh-hant'`, the
`/zh-hant/*` header rule and `public/og-image-zh-hant.png`. Adapted by Opus from the reviewed Simplified Chinese pages with
OpenVireo's English pages as the source, to Taiwan's standard, and checked by the SC4 framework with profile `zh-Hant` and glossary
field `zh-Hant` (its Taiwan-vocabulary `forbidden` list among the rules): 你, 「」, full-width marks with no space between Han and Latin
text, «實驗» for a lab, «探究» for an investigation, the app's own lab names, «識別碼» for Object X's identifier as E08 writes it, and the
footer «本頁的繁體中文文字是OpenVireo的網頁改編，不是CLEA的文本。» with the credit to Project CLEA, Gettysburg College and the NSF.

**Review ZT-REL+SEO** (two independent Opus reviewers, 2026-11-22, each reading every new Traditional Chinese string and the release
wiring). Verdicts: approve after minor fixes (RA: 4 minor, 8 nits; RB: 2 minor, 6 nits; no blocker or major). Both found the wiring
equal to ZH-REL's point by point. Fixed: Hertzsprung is «赫茲史普龍» in Taiwan (the first draft's 赫茨普龍 is the mainland form);
«直接告訴你答案» (ZT-HELP dropped 給出 as mainland usage), «神祕», «提出論證», «透露», «網站產生的檔案»; the notices name the coordinator's
delegation of 21 November; `docs/SEO.md` records the image's size and hash and marks ZH-SEO's "until zh-Hant exists" as past; stale
comments in `locale.test.ts`, `languages.test.ts` and `language-selector.spec.ts`. Kept: the breadcrumb «導覽路徑» (RB's alternative
was optional). Not this front's: `tests/digit-notice.spec.ts`'s per-language test lists neither Chinese (it predates ZH-REL).
The browser-level preview gap (no registered language left to preview) is recorded above, as ZH-REL recorded it.

The full checks also caught two test slips of the release itself, fixed: the Russian keyboard walk needed one more ArrowUp to pass
繁體中文, and the E02 and E05 walks read the print date of a zh-TW browser, «2026/11/9 上午7:00:11» (上午/下午), on the English and the
Traditional Chinese page alike, as HI-REL found for Hindi's «am» (`SAME_PATTERN_IN['zh-Hant']` in `walks/e02.ts`, `PRINT_DATE_ZH_TW` in
`walks/e05.ts`); a zh-CN browser's «2026/11/9 07:00:11» was NUMERIC already.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`). zh-Hant: 13 chunks, 240.7 KB gzip in all, none loaded by `index.html`; 38 help
chunks, 53.0 KB gzip. The release changes no zh-Hant chunk's content. Deploy limits: 2,600 files (nine Traditional Chinese pages and
the image are new).

**Verification** (2026-11-22, worktree `claude/i18n-zh-zt-rel` at 1c0e0228 with origin/main b5166609 merged, then the review and
test fixes). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest 7,021 passed and 11 skipped (1,506 of `src/seo`, `src/i18n` and the headers test after the fixes);
`npm run test:resources`; `npm run build` with every check; `npm run test:deploy-limits`. Playwright under the main fontconfig
(DejaVu): `language-selector`, `areas`, `seo`, `narrow`, `browsers`, `help`, `shell`, `page-bar`, `app-shell`, `seo-labs` and `home`
407 of 411; every Traditional Chinese test of `tests/i18n` in a zh-TW browser 103 of 108; the export families in zh-Hant 17; the
scripts suite 287 (the real-Chinese CJK walks in a zh-TW browser among them); `npm run test:pages` on `dist/` 237. The failures,
rerun after the fixes: the Russian keyboard walk and the zh-Hant E02/E05 print dates (fixed above, 26 passed with the zh-Hant seo
flows); the 320 px Japanese, Simplified and Traditional Chinese narrow sweeps and the Traditional Chinese E08 and radio screens,
which fail under DejaVu for every CJK language (the known test-font artefact) and pass under the scripts fontconfig (9 passed); the
Pages suite again after the text fixes, 237.

## Guide companion: its strings in ten languages (GUIDE-COMPANION)

Front GUIDE-COMPANION (user decision, 2026-11-23; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md`) adds a strip that keeps the current step of the guide in
use at the bottom of every page. Almost all of its text is the guides' own, already translated: the guide's menu name, its
"Next:" line (or the step's title in the LSS and Object X guides) and the text under the step. New text:

- `companion` in `src/components/AppShellMessages.ts` (map `appShell`, so it is in every language's core module): `label`, `stepOf(n,total)`,
  `actions`, `show`, `details`, `full`, `hide`, `hideName`, `notYet` and `allDone`.
- One paragraph in the web help topic `exercise-guides` (`src/help/webHelp.ts`) and one sentence in the tour's `guides` step
  (`src/help/tour.ts`).

| | Show me where | Step details | Full guide | Hide · its name | Step n of m |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Muéstrame dónde | Detalles del paso | Guía completa | Ocultar · Ocultar esta guía | Paso 3 de 9 |
| pt-BR | Mostrar onde fica | Detalhes da etapa | Guia completo | Ocultar · Ocultar este guia | Etapa 3 de 9 |
| fr | Me montrer où | Détails de l’étape | Guide complet | Masquer · Masquer ce guide | Étape 3 sur 9 |
| id | Tunjukkan letaknya | Detail langkah | Panduan lengkap | Sembunyikan · Sembunyikan panduan ini | Langkah 3 dari 9 |
| hi | स्थान दिखाएँ | चरण का विवरण | पूरा गाइड | छिपाएँ · छिपाएँ: यह गाइड | चरण 3 / 9 |
| ja | 場所を表示 | 手順の詳細 | ガイド全体 | 非表示 · 非表示：このガイド | 手順3／9 |
| zh-Hans | 指给我看 | 步骤详情 | 完整指南 | 隐藏 · 隐藏此指南 | 第3步，共9步 |
| zh-Hant | 指出位置 | 步驟詳情 | 完整指南 | 隱藏 · 隱藏此指南 | 第3步，共9步 |
| ru | Показать, где | Описание шага | Всё руководство | Скрыть · Скрыть это руководство | Шаг 3 из 9 |

The Spanish wordings are the user's (2026-11-23). Rules:

- **The Hide button's name starts with its label** (WCAG 2.5.3) in every language; where the natural phrase would put the object
  first (Japanese, Hindi), the name is the label, a colon and the object. `src/help/guideTargets.test.ts` checks it for every
  language's map, with the step counter keeping both numbers.
- **The strip is shell text** (`data-i18n-shell`): the translation walks of `tests/i18n` skip it, as they skip the sidebar and the
  page bar's place; `tests/guide-companion.spec.ts` checks it in Spanish and under the right-to-left pseudo-locale.
- **Area keys the strip names moved to the core.** The planner (`scripts/i18n-split.mjs`) sends a key to the core when a
  core-reachable file names it. The six shell guides were already in the core; the cluster HR guide's "Next:" lines, step texts and
  keep-your-work lines (`clusterLab.next.*`, `clusterLab.step.*`, `clusterLab.preserve.save|restore`) now are too, through
  `src/ClusterLabGuideText.tsx`. `dynamic-key-sites.json` was refreshed for the functions that moved (`hubbleNextText`,
  `pulsarStepBody`, `companionContent`…), `vireo-literals.json` lost the receipt of `LSS_LAB_REPEAT_RECORDS` in `LssLabGuide.tsx`
  (it is now read outside JSX, in `lssStepHelp`), and the coverage audit counts three more files (150).
- **Line heights.** The strip's text and buttons have their CJK, Hindi and Arabic-script floors in `src/theme/scripts.css` and
  `src/theme/rtl.css`, as the tour's card has.

Translation batches ran on Sonnet 5.5 subagents, one per language, each with its language's glossary and lints; the lints caught
a space before a digit in Japanese (`手順 3`) and a plain space before «;» in French, both fixed by the batch.

**Review** (an independent Opus reviewer read every new string in the nine languages against each glossary and catalog,
2026-11-23). One major, fixed: Indonesian «Tunjukkan» said only "Show" and is «Tunjukkan letaknya». Minor, fixed: Indonesian
«Panduan lengkap» (not «penuh») and «Anda sudah berada di halaman yang tepat»; Portuguese «Detalhes da etapa», «Mostrar onde fica»,
«o destaca com um contorno» (not «o contorna», "goes around it") and the tour's «etapas» (it mixed «passos» and «etapa»); Spanish
«Completaste todos los pasos.», as the guides' closing lines say; French «Une fois le guide fermé» (the possessive of «Après sa
fermeture» could be the page's) and «l’encadre»; Hindi «स्थान दिखाएँ» and an outline that is a box, not an underline; Japanese «非表示»,
the catalog's word for Hide (58 uses against one «隠す»); Traditional Chinese «指出位置» («帶我前往» only said "take me there");
Russian «когда будет выполнено одно из предшествующих действий». Simplified Chinese needed no change. English: the "not on screen
yet" line now ends "it appears once an earlier action is done. You are on the right page." Kept: "Hide this guide" (the
reviewer's "Hide the guide strip" is longer in every language and the help says what Hide removes) and Russian «Всё руководство».

Restart exercise (user request, 2026-11-23) adds seven strings to `companion`: `restart` (the button), `restartTitle`,
`restartWhat`, `restartLost`, `restartSave`, `restartConfirm` and `restartCancel` (the confirmation). Rules, checked in
`src/help/guideTargets.test.ts` for every language: `restartSave` names Save investigation exactly as that language's
`recovery.save` does (in the language's own quotation marks where the sentence needs them); the confirming answer uses the
button's own words, so the button and the answer read as one action; Cancel is that language's usual Cancel. French calls the
exercise «TP» and Russian «работа», Indonesian «praktikum», Japanese «実習», Chinese «实验/實驗» and Hindi «प्रयोग», as their
guides' own titles do. zh-Hans writes 载入, never 加载 (its glossary).


## First look: its strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC1)

Front NEWCOMER NC1 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "First look") adds nine strings, `firstLook` in
`src/components/AppShellMessages.ts` (map `appShell`, so in every language's core module): `name` (in the strip), the four step
lines `dome`, `point`, `look` and `spectrum`, `spectrumDetail` (the last step's Step details), `closing`, `start` (the button
in the observatory heading) and `help` (the Help menu item). Three Step details reuse the band's `sky.step1Help..3Help` and the
closing line's reuses `home.labsHint`; `src/components/guideCompanionContent.tsx` now names those four catalog keys, which the
planner keeps in the core. No key is built at run time.

| | name | point | start |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Primer vistazo | Elige la galaxia M82 y apunta el telescopio hacia ella | Seguir la guía (unos 3 min) |
| pt-BR | Primeira olhada | Escolha a galáxia M82 e aponte o telescópio para ela | Seguir o guia (cerca de 3 min) |
| fr | Premier aperçu | Choisissez la galaxie M82 et pointez le télescope vers elle | Me guider (environ 3 min) |
| id | Tinjauan pertama | Pilih galaksi M82 dan arahkan teleskop ke sana | Ikuti panduan (sekitar 3 menit) |
| hi | पहली नज़र | आकाशगंगा M82 चुनें और दूरबीन को उसकी ओर इंगित करें | मुझे गाइड करें (लगभग 3 मिनट) |
| ja | まず見てみる | 銀河M82を選び、望遠鏡を向ける | ガイドを開始（約3分） |
| zh-Hans | 初步观察 | 选择星系M82，并将望远镜指向它 | 跟着指南走（约3分钟） |
| zh-Hant | 初步觀察 | 選擇星系M82，並將望遠鏡指向它 | 跟著指南走（約3分鐘） |
| ru | Первый взгляд | Выберите галактику M82 и наведите на неё телескоп | Пройти с гидом (около 3 мин) |

Rules:

- **The name is each catalog's own phrase for a first look** at the library (`sky.referenceHelp`: «primer vistazo», «primeira
  olhada», «premier aperçu», «tinjauan pertama», «पहली नज़र», «初步观察/初步觀察», «первый взгляд»); Japanese, whose
  `sky.referenceHelp` says «最初の手がかり», uses «まず見てみる» for both the name and the Help item.
- **Step lines follow each language's step style:** the band's imperatives (es «Abre», pt-BR «Abra», fr «Ouvrez», ru «Откройте»,
  hi «खोलें», id base form), the Japanese dictionary form of the band («ドームを開く»), Chinese as the band. The pronoun of
  `point`, `look` and `spectrum` refers to M82, which `point` names as a galaxy in every language (English «the galaxy M82»,
  es «la galaxia», pt-BR «a galáxia», fr «la galaxie», id «galaksi», hi «आकाशगंगा», ja «銀河», zh «星系», ru «галактику»), so the
  pronouns are anchored (es «ella», pt-BR «ela», fr «elle», ru «неё»).
- **`start` holds the guide word of each glossary** (es «Seguir la guía», pt-BR «guia», id «panduan», ru «гид», zh «指南»; the lints flagged
  «Me guie», «Pandu saya», «Провести по шагам» and «带我体验» for the missing noun) and a rough time with the digit 3 and the
  language's minute abbreviation or word; Russian has a no-break space in «3 мин» (the typography fixer).
- `src/help/guideTargets.test.ts` checks for every language: nine non-empty strings, each different from English, `point` and
  `spectrumDetail` naming M82 and `spectrumDetail` naming VIREO, and a 3 in `start`.

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints. The independent
review (2026-11-24) anchored the pronouns («the galaxy M82» in all ten), and changed es `start` to «Seguir la guía (unos 3 min)»,
fr `closing` to «Vous avez jeté un premier coup d’œil. Choisissez un TP pour aller plus loin.» (no « ; : ! ? », so no U+202F) and
ru `closing` to «Первый взгляд брошен. Чтобы продолжить, выберите лабораторную работу.».

## The tour's last step: its strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC2)

Front NEWCOMER NC2 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "The tour's last step") shortens the tour (map `tour`,
`src/help/tour.ts`, in every language's core module) from six steps to four. **Removed** in all ten languages (deletion only):
`steps.sections`, `steps.guides` and `steps.observatory`, title and text each (6 strings; no glossary row, lint exemption or
`identical.json` entry named them). **Added** (4 strings):
`steps.start.title`, `steps.start.text`, `firstLook` and `chooseLab` (the last step's two buttons). No key is built at run time.

| | `firstLook` | `chooseLab` | `steps.start.title` |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Dar un primer vistazo (unos 3 min) | Elegir una práctica | ¿Cómo quieres empezar? |
| pt-BR | Dar uma primeira olhada (cerca de 3 min) | Escolher uma prática | Como você quer começar? |
| fr | Jeter un premier coup d’œil (environ 3 min) | Choisir un TP | Comment voulez-vous commencer ? |
| id | Lakukan tinjauan pertama (sekitar 3 menit) | Pilih praktikum | Bagaimana Anda ingin memulai? |
| hi | पहली नज़र डालें (लगभग 3 मिनट) | कोई प्रयोग चुनें | आप कैसे शुरू करना चाहेंगे? |
| ja | まず見てみる（約3分） | 実習を選ぶ | どのように始めますか？ |
| zh-Hans | 进行初步观察（约3分钟） | 选择实验 | 你想怎样开始？ |
| zh-Hant | 進行初步觀察（約3分鐘） | 選擇實驗 | 你想怎麼開始？ |
| ru | Бросить первый взгляд (около 3 мин) | Выбрать лабораторную работу | С чего вы хотите начать? |

Rules:

- **`firstLook` is each language's Help item for the first look** (`appShell.firstLook.help`, NC1) with the rough time of
  `appShell.firstLook.start` («unos 3 min», «cerca de 3 min», «environ 3 min», «sekitar 3 menit», «लगभग 3 मिनट», «約3分»,
  «约3分钟/約3分鐘», «около 3 мин» with a no-break space); the button form follows each tour's own `done` and `skip` (es, pt-BR,
  fr and ru infinitives, id and hi as their buttons, ja a dictionary form, Chinese a verb phrase).
- **`chooseLab` names a lab by each catalog's word** (`home.labsHeading`): práctica, prática, TP, praktikum, प्रयोग, 実習,
  实验/實驗, лабораторная работа; never "experiment" in English.
- **The phone clause names the button as it reads**, each language's `appShell.toolsMenu` (Menú, Menu, मेनू, 「メニュー」,
  “菜单”, 「選單」, «Меню»): the old sections text said "Tools" in English and «टूल्स» in Hindi (plan F10); the other eight
  translations already said Menu. `src/help/tour.test.ts` checks it in all ten languages, with a 3 in `firstLook`, the four steps
  in order and the four strings differing from English.
- French puts U+202F before « ? » and « ; »; Hindi ends its sentences with «।»; the CJK texts use full-width punctuation with no
  space beside Latin letters or digits; the zh-Hant text keeps Taiwan's 選單; Russian addresses the reader as lower-case «вы».

- **"Tools button" becomes "Menu button" (NC2 review).** The help topic Menus, keyboard and phones (`src/help/webHelp.ts`) had the
  same stale "behind the Tools button"; it now says "behind the Menu button". The nine glossaries' row `Tools button` is renamed
  `Menu button` (Hindi «टूल्स बटन» → «मेनू बटन»; the others already said Menu), so the glossary lint now also checks the tour's
  start text. Hindi's help sentence reads «फ़ोन पर यह मेनू बटन के पीछे रहता है।» and its lint exemption
  `map:webHelp.groups.3.topics.0.blocks.0.0` is removed.

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints
(`src/i18n/<lang>/<lang>.test.ts` pass); the independent review (2026-11-24) found all nine fine.

## Plain step titles and More detail: its strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC3)

Front NEWCOMER NC3 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "Plain step titles and More detail") rewrites twelve
step titles in plain words (same keys, so nothing moves between modules; the window shows them and the companion strip's line
now does too) and adds one string, `lab.moreDetail`, the visible text of every step's disclosure. Its accessible form needs no
string: the summary is named by its own text followed by the step's title (`aria-labelledby`). No key is built at run time
beyond the existing title templates; the cluster titles now come from `src/ClusterLabGuideText.tsx` (`clusterStepTitle`), which
the strip also reads, so the planner places `clusterLab.step.*.title` in the core (the `dynamic-key-sites.json` pin refreshed:
the title templates moved into `classificationStepTitle`, `clusterStepTitle`, `pulsarStepTitle` and `pleiadesStepTitle`, the
companion's `classLab.next.*` and `pleiadesLab.next.*` sites from its line to its Step details).

| key | English before | English after |
|---|---|---|
| `lab.acquireTitle` | Acquire each galaxy | Take the spectrum of each galaxy |
| `lab.measureTitle` | Measure the line shift manually | Measure how far the lines have shifted |
| `lab.hubbleTitle` | Build the sourced diagram | Plot speed against distance |
| `lab.saveTitle` | Keep a reproducible investigation | Save your work |
| `lab.interpretTitle` | Interpret your own result | Explain what your graph shows |
| `clusterLab.step.trial.title` | Fit the ZAMS and an isochrone by eye | Match the model curves to the stars |
| `clusterLab.step.report.title` | Read the parameter report | Read your results |
| `pulsarLab.step.delay.title` | Measure the dispersion delay | Measure the delay between two frequencies |
| `pleiadesLab.step.measure.title` | Measure sky and star through B and V | Measure the star through two filters |
| `pleiadesLab.step.color.title` | Form B−V | Work out the star’s color (B−V) |
| `pleiadesLab.step.sample.title` | Build the sample | Repeat for more stars |
| `objectXGuide.steps.measure[0]` (map) | Blink and measure | Compare the two images |

| | `lab.moreDetail` | `lab.hubbleTitle` | `pleiadesLab.step.color.title` |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Más detalles | Grafica la velocidad frente a la distancia | Calcula el color de la estrella (B−V) |
| pt-BR | Mais detalhes | Faça o gráfico da velocidade em função da distância | Calcule a cor da estrela (B−V) |
| fr | Plus de détails | Tracer la vitesse en fonction de la distance | Calculer la couleur de l’étoile (B−V) |
| id | Detail lebih lanjut | Buat grafik kecepatan terhadap jarak | Hitung warna bintang (B−V) |
| hi | अधिक जानकारी | दूरी के सापेक्ष वेग का ग्राफ़ बनाएँ | तारे का रंग (B−V) निकालें |
| ja | 詳細 | 速度を距離に対してプロットする | 星の色（B−V）を求める |
| zh-Hans | 更多详情 | 绘制速度与距离的关系图 | 算出恒星的颜色（B−V） |
| zh-Hant | 更多詳情 | 繪製速度與距離的關係圖 | 算出恆星的顏色（B−V） |
| ru | Подробнее | Построить график скорости в зависимости от расстояния | Вычислить цвет звезды (B−V) |

Rules:

- **Titles keep each guide's step form:** es «tú» imperatives, pt-BR imperatives, fr, ru infinitives, id base forms, hi the
  polite -एँ forms, ja dictionary forms, Chinese verb phrases; Russian keeps its earlier `lab.acquireTitle` («Получить спектр
  каждой галактики»), which already said the new English.
- **No value, answer or Object X hint:** the titles name actions only («Compare the two images» says nothing about motion).
- **Vocabulary from each catalog:** the graph word of each language (gráfica, gráfico, graphique, grafik, ग्राफ़, ja 図 as
  `lab.hubbleTitle` had, zh 图/圖, график), its filter word (ja フィルター, zh 滤光片/濾光片) and the B−V symbol unchanged; zh-Hant
  keeps Taiwan's 儲存 and 影像 and its catalog's 擷取; CJK full-width parentheses around B−V with no space beside Latin.
- The classification titles keep their «n · » numbers in the window; the strip leaves the number out (`withoutStepNumber`,
  tested on the ten catalogs in `src/guideMoreDetail.test.ts`), so no string changed for it.

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints
(`src/i18n/<lang>/<lang>.test.ts`).
The independent review (2026-11-25) changed two Japanese strings: `lab.moreDetail` «詳しく» → «詳細», and
`pulsarLab.step.delay.title` to the noun form of its neighbours («周期を測定»), «2つの周波数間の遅延を測定».

## Why-lines and What next: their strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC4)

Front NEWCOMER NC4 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "Why it matters, and what next") adds ten strings and
rewords two. **Added:** the seven why-lines that open the guide windows (`lab.why`, `lssLab.why`, `classLab.why`,
`pleiadesLab.why`, `clusterLab.why`, `pulsarLab.why`, and `why` in the Object X guide map `objectXGuide`) and the three strings of
What next (`lab.whatNext`, `lab.tryNext` with `{lab}`, `lab.openNext`). The plan named the last three `guide.*`; they are `lab.*`
like `lab.moreDetail`, the other string every guide window shares, so the per-language coverage tests (which group keys by
prefix) cover them with no new list. **Changed:** `home.hubbleSummary` and `home.lssSummary` (same keys), which stated the labs'
results (plan F11, answer Q14). `{lab}` is the next lab's name as on its home card (`home.<lab>`), placed after a colon so it stays
in the nominative (the Russian rule of 2026-11-19) and needs no article in any language. No key is built at run time
(`src/guideLead.tsx` `labName` names the seven `home.*` keys literally); `lab.*`, `classLab.why`, `pleiadesLab.why`,
`pulsarLab.why` and the `home.*` names are read by the windows and by the strip, so the planner keeps them where their users are.

| key | English |
|---|---|
| `lab.why` | How do the speeds of distant galaxies compare with their distances? You will answer with spectra you take and measure yourself. |
| `lssLab.why` | How are galaxies arranged in space? You will map a strip of sky with redshifts you measure yourself, and look for the answer in your map. |
| `classLab.why` | What can the dark lines in a star’s light tell us about the star? You will compare a star’s spectrum with standard stars and decide its type yourself. |
| `pleiadesLab.why` | How far away is the Pleiades cluster? You will work it out from your own measurements of its stars’ brightness and color. |
| `clusterLab.why` | What is the age of a star cluster, and how far away is it? You will compare its stars with model curves and decide for yourself. |
| `pulsarLab.why` | What are the regular radio pulses some objects send, and what can timing them at two frequencies tell us? You will time them yourself with the radio telescope. |
| `objectXGuide.why` (map) | What kind of object lies at the coordinates you are given? You will decide with the observatory’s instruments, before its identity is revealed. |
| `lab.whatNext` | What next |
| `lab.tryNext` | A lab to try next: {lab}. |
| `lab.openNext` | Open its guide |
| `home.hubbleSummary` | Measure how fast galaxies are moving and how far away they are, then compare the two. (was «…then work out how fast the universe expands.») |
| `home.lssSummary` | Measure the redshifts of many galaxies across a strip of sky and map where they lie. (was «…and map the cosmic web.») |

| | `pleiadesLab.why` | `lab.tryNext` | `lab.openNext` |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | ¿A qué distancia está el cúmulo de las Pléyades? Lo calcularás con tus propias mediciones del brillo y el color de sus estrellas. | Una práctica para probar después: {lab}. | Abrir la guía |
| pt-BR | A que distância está o aglomerado das Plêiades? Você vai calcular isso com suas próprias medidas do brilho e da cor das estrelas dele. | Uma prática para experimentar depois: {lab}. | Abrir o guia |
| fr | À quelle distance se trouve l’amas des Pléiades ? Vous la déterminerez à partir de vos propres mesures de l’éclat et de la couleur de ses étoiles. | Un TP à essayer ensuite : {lab}. | Ouvrir son guide |
| id | Seberapa jauh gugus Pleiades? Anda akan menghitungnya dari pengukuran Anda sendiri atas kecerlangan dan warna bintang-bintangnya. | Praktikum yang bisa Anda coba berikutnya: {lab}. | Buka panduannya |
| hi | कृत्तिका (Pleiades) तारागुच्छ कितनी दूर है? आप इसे उसके तारों की चमक और वर्ण के अपने मापों से निकालेंगे। | आगे आज़माने के लिए एक प्रयोग: {lab}। | उसका गाइड खोलें |
| ja | プレアデス星団はどれくらい遠くにあるでしょうか？星団の星の明るさと色を自分で測定し、その結果から求めます。 | おすすめは「{lab}」の実習です。 | ガイドを開く |
| zh-Hans | 昴星团离我们有多远？你将根据自己对其恒星亮度和颜色的测量来算出答案。 | 接下来可以试试这个实验：{lab}。 | 打开它的指南 |
| zh-Hant | 昴宿星團離我們有多遠？你將根據自己對其恆星亮度和顏色的測量來算出答案。 | 接下來可以試試這個實驗：{lab}。 | 開啟它的指南 |
| ru | Как далеко находится скопление Плеяды? Вы определите это по собственным измерениям блеска и цвета его звёзд. | Дальше можно попробовать лабораторную работу: {lab}. | Открыть руководство |

Rules:

- **A why-line asks, then says what the visitor does, and never answers** (Q14 «no»): no result word of its guide, no digit of any
  script (Japanese writes «二つの周波数», not «2つ»), no hint of Object X's kind. `src/guideLead.test.ts` checks each language
  against its own list of result words (expansion, proportional, slope; walls, voids, the cosmic web; temperature; light-years,
  young, old; neutron, electrons, dispersion; the five Object X kind names of the language's `objectXLab.kinds`) and requires a
  «?» or «？». The cluster why-line asks «What is the age…» rather than «How old…», so that its own wording is not caught by the
  word «old» it must never state.
- **Register:** es «tú» (Compararás, decidirás tú mismo), pt-BR «você», fr «vous» with U+202F before « ? » and « : », id «Anda»,
  hi आप with «।», ja です/ます (でしょうか？, 判断します), zh-Hans and zh-Hant 你, ru «вы»; the button follows each language's button
  form (es, pt-BR, fr, ru infinitives, id base form, hi -एँ, ja dictionary form, Chinese a verb phrase; zh-Hant 開啟, as its
  catalog's other Open buttons).
- **Vocabulary from each catalog and glossary:** the lab word (práctica, prática, TP, praktikum, प्रयोग, 実習, 实验/實驗,
  лабораторная работа; the zh and ru glossaries require it in `lab.tryNext`), redshift (corrimiento al rojo, desvio para o
  vermelho, décalage vers le rouge, pergeseran merah, अभिरक्त विस्थापन (रेडशिफ़्ट), 赤方偏移, 红移/紅移, красное смещение), the
  Indonesian glossary's «denyut» for a pulsar's pulse (never «pulsa»), standard stars (étoiles étalons, эталонные звёзды,
  標準星), Hindi «स्पंद» and «वर्ण», and the Chinese pulsar line with one question mark, as the English (the glyph-count lint).
- `pt-br.test.ts`, `fr.test.ts`, `id.test.ts`, `hi.test.ts`, `ja.test.ts`, `ru.test.ts`, `zh-hans.test.ts` and `zh-hant.test.ts`
  pins move by the new keys of their prefixes (E03 773, E04/E05 653, E06/E08 138, zh-Hans ZH2 809, zh-Hant ZT2 1437).

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints
(`src/i18n/<lang>/<lang>.test.ts` pass).
The independent review (2026-11-25, N1, N2, N7) changed: `pulsarLab.why`, which said «what happens to them on their way to us»
and so presupposed the result, now asks what timing the pulses at two frequencies can tell (ten languages); `home.hubbleSummary`
drops «moving away» (ten languages); ja `lab.whatNext` «次は» → «次の実習» and `lab.tryNext` «おすすめは「{lab}」の実習です。» (no
repeated 次は); fr `lab.why` «Quel lien y a-t-il entre la vitesse des galaxies lointaines et leur distance ?» (the calque «se
comparent-elles» removed); pt-BR `lab.whatNext` «O que fazer depois» (not «Próximo passo», which read as a guide step); id
`lssLab.why` «sebuah jalur langit».

## Quick versions: their strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC5)

Front NEWCOMER NC5 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "Quick versions of the two long labs") adds ten strings
and makes nine goal-neutral (same keys). **Added:** `lab.version.legend`, `lab.version.quick` and `lab.version.full` (`{count}` =
3 and 13), `lab.version.note`, `lab.version.tip`, `lab.version.quickDone`, `lab.version.continueFull`, `lab.version.strip`,
`pleiadesLab.version.quick` and `pleiadesLab.version.full` (`{count}` = 8 and 24). The shared ones are `lab.*`, like
`lab.moreDetail` and `lab.whatNext`. **Changed:** `lab.counts` («… Complete Hubble targets: {rows} (goal {total})», `{total}` is now
the version's goal), `lab.next.acquire` and `lab.next.measure` (the next galaxy, not each one), `lab.next.interpretSupported` (adds
`{count}`, the complete targets), the last clause of `lab.recipeHelp` («…the distinct acquired targets of the version you chose»),
`pleiadesLab.coverage` and `pleiadesLab.status.sample` («…: {count} (goal {total})»), `pleiadesLab.next.sample` (no «All 24») and
`pleiadesLab.step.sample.help` (adds `{total}` = 24, the full version). No key is built at run time. The `{count}` of the four
labels is a fixed number, so each language writes the form for that number (Russian keeps its count-neutral «(галактик: {count})»).

| key | English |
|---|---|
| `lab.version.legend` | Version |
| `lab.version.quick` | Quick: {count} galaxies |
| `lab.version.full` | Full: all {count} galaxies, as in the original |
| `pleiadesLab.version.quick` | Quick: {count} stars |
| `pleiadesLab.version.full` | Full: all {count} stars, as in the original |
| `lab.version.note` | The quick version is a web suggestion, a part of the original exercise; the full version is the original. |
| `lab.version.tip` | Galaxies from different groups (UM-I, CorBor, Peg, Coma, Boo) make a clearer graph. |
| `lab.version.quickDone` | You finished the quick version. Continue with the full exercise for the whole list. |
| `lab.version.continueFull` | Continue with the full exercise |
| `lab.version.strip` | Quick version |

| | `lab.version.quick` | `lab.version.continueFull` | goal in the counts |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Rápida: {count} galaxias | Continuar con el ejercicio completo | (meta {total}) |
| pt-BR | Rápida: {count} galáxias | Continuar com o exercício completo | (meta {total}) |
| fr | Rapide : {count} galaxies | Poursuivre avec l’exercice complet | (objectif {total}) |
| id | Cepat: {count} galaksi | Lanjutkan dengan praktikum lengkap | (sasaran {total}) |
| hi | त्वरित: {count} आकाशगंगाएँ | पूर्ण प्रयोग जारी रखें | (ध्येय {total}) |
| ja | 短縮版：銀河{count}個 | 完全版の実習に進む | （必要数{total}個） |
| zh-Hans | 快速版：{count}个星系 | 继续完整实验 | （需{total}个） |
| zh-Hant | 快速版：{count}個星系 | 繼續完整實驗 | （需{total}個） |
| ru | Быстрый (галактик: {count}) | Продолжить полную работу | (нужно: {total}) |

Rules:

- **The goal is named beside the real count, never as «count of goal»**, so a count above the goal reads naturally («4 (goal 3)»).
  Where «target» already took the obvious word (ja 目標, zh 目标/目標, hi लक्ष्य), the goal is «the number needed» (必要数, 需, ध्येय).
- **Russian says «вариант»** for the quick and full versions, so that «эта версия» keeps meaning the web version in
  `lab.next.interpretSupported`; ja uses 短縮版 and 完全版 under the legend バージョン.
- **Register and form** as in NC4: es «tú», pt-BR «você», fr «vous» with U+202F before « : » and « ; » and inside « », id
  «Anda», hi आप with «।», ja です/ます (buttons in the dictionary form), zh-Hans and zh-Hant 你 (zh-Hant 清單 for list), ru «вы»
  with a no-break space before «—»; the lab word of each glossary (práctica/ejercicio, prática/exercício, exercice, praktikum,
  प्रयोग, 実習, 实验/實驗, работа).
- fr `identical.json` lists `catalog:lab.version.legend` («Version» is the French word). `src/guideLead.test.ts` checks the version
  strings in every language against the Hubble and Pleiades result words. The E04/E05 pins move to 655 (fr, hi, id, ja, ru,
  zh-Hans), zh-Hans ZH2 to 817 and zh-Hant ZT2 to 1439; `plural-sites.json` lists the six new count-printing messages and
  `vireo-literals.json` three numeric receipts (`HUBBLE_QUICK_GOAL`, `PLEIADES_QUICK_GOAL`, `PLEIADES_SAMPLE_TARGETS`).

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints.
In Japanese 8 of the 9 changed strings have a new value: `lab.next.measure` already read neutrally («アーカイブした銀河のスペクトルを開き直し…»)
and kept its text.

The independent review (2026-11-25) changed, in all ten languages: `lab.acquireTitle` (the strip's line during acquire) no longer
says «each galaxy» beside «(goal 3)»: «Take a galaxy’s spectrum» (N4); `lab.coverage` and `lab.next.interpretSupported` name
OpenVireo instead of «this version», so that «version» means only the quick or full version («Supported in OpenVireo: …»,
«OpenVireo supports …»; Russian already said «вариант» for these, N5; the ja `lab.recipeHelp` «選んだバージョン» now refers only to
the quick or full version under the legend バージョン); `lab.version.tip` names the selector's group labels «(UM-I, CorBor, Peg,
Coma, Boo)» (N6); `pleiadesLab.next.sample` and `pleiadesLab.step.sample.help` say that Mayall's 7 stars are not enough even
for the quick version (N1). ja `lab.counts` writes «（必要数{total}個）», as the Pleiades lines do (N7).

## Lab facts: their strings in ten languages (NEWCOMER NC6)

Front NEWCOMER NC6 (user decision, 2026-11-24; `docs/GUIDE-COMPANION.md` "Time, level and Start here") adds seven strings, shown
on the home cards and in the guide windows: `home.time` and `home.quickTime` (`{low}` and `{high}`, minutes through `formatNumber`),
`home.level1`, `home.level2`, `home.level3`, `home.startHere` and `home.estimateNote`. Literal keys, no key built at run time; the
`home.*` pins move from 27 to 34 (fr, hi, id, ja, ru, zh-Hans, zh-Hant). The static pages carry the same seven texts in each
row's `ui` of `src/seo/languages.ts` (`time`, `quickTime`, `level1`–`level3`, `startHere`, `estimateNote`, English included),
checked by each language's SEO lint; the rows' `educationalLevel` is removed (Q9).

| key | English |
|---|---|
| `home.time` | About {low}–{high} min |
| `home.quickTime` | Quick version: about {low}–{high} min |
| `home.level1` | No background needed |
| `home.level2` | Some background helps |
| `home.level3` | Challenging |
| `home.startHere` | Start here |
| `home.estimateNote` | Times are rough estimates computed from the steps, not measured with visitors. |

| | `home.time` | `home.level1` | `home.startHere` |
|---|---|---|---|
| es-419 | Unos {low}–{high} min | No necesitas conocimientos previos | Empieza aquí |
| pt-BR | Cerca de {low}–{high} min | Não exige conhecimento prévio | Comece aqui |
| fr | Environ {low}–{high} min (no-break space before «min») | Aucune connaissance préalable requise | Commencez ici |
| id | Sekitar {low}–{high} min | Tidak perlu pengetahuan awal | Mulai di sini |
| hi | लगभग {low}–{high} मिनट | किसी पूर्व ज्ञान की ज़रूरत नहीं | यहाँ से शुरू करें |
| ja | 約{low}–{high}分 | 予備知識は不要 | 最初はここから |
| zh-Hans | 约{low}–{high}分钟 | 无需背景知识 | 从这里开始 |
| zh-Hant | 約{low}–{high}分鐘 | 不需要背景知識 | 從這裡開始 |
| ru | Около {low}–{high} мин | Подготовка не нужна | Начните отсюда |

Rules:

- **Minutes** as «min» (en, es, pt-BR, fr, id), «मिनट», «分», «分钟», «分鐘», «мин» (no plural form), with an en dash between the
  two numbers and no spaces (Japanese included, «約{low}–{high}分»: review N4 proposed the wave dash, but the coordinator kept the en dash of
  the ja plan §4.1, «no wave dash», which 54 of the catalog's ranges follow); French and Russian put a no-break space before «min» and «мин». Review N7: id
  `home.estimateNote` «Perkiraan waktu ini dihitung kasar dari langkah-langkahnya, bukan diukur pada pengunjung.», ru
  `home.level3` «Требует усилий». The quick time reuses each
  language's NC5 word for the quick version (Versión rápida, Versão rápida, Version rapide, Versi cepat, त्वरित संस्करण, 短縮版,
  快速版, Быстрый вариант).
- **The note says computed, not measured**, in every language; no language calls the times measured or typical.
- **Register** as in NC4 and NC5: es «tú» (No necesitas…), pt-BR «você», fr «vous» (Commencez ici) with U+202F before « : », id
  «Anda», hi आप with «।», ja です/ます for the note and noun forms for the labels, zh-Hans and zh-Hant 你 (zh-Hant Taiwan
  vocabulary), ru «вы» (Начните отсюда) with a no-break space before «—».

The translations were written by the implementer (Opus) with each language's glossary, profile and lints.
